### [medium] wrong-note: D-6 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-6's `how` says the status is for "Religious or social-welfare work for a religious organisation", which affirmatively misstates the rule: religious work performed for a Korean religious organisation that hired you does NOT qualify for D-6. Only (a) dispatch from a FOREIGN religious/social-welfare organisation to its Korean branch for religious activity, or (b) invitation by a Korean religious/social-welfare organisation for SOCIAL WELFARE activity, qualify.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-2 row 9 종교(D-6), extracted verbatim from the decree PDF: "가. 외국의 종교단체 또는 사회복지단체로부터 파견되어 대한민국에 있는 지부 또는 유관 종교단체에서 종교활동을 하려는 사람 / 나. 대한민국 내의 종교단체 또는 사회복지단체의 초청을 받아 사회복지활동을 하려는 사람 / 다. 그 밖에 법무부장관이 인정하는 종교활동 또는 사회복지활동에 종사하려는 사람". Note that 가 (religious activity) requires 파견 from abroad, while 나 (invitation by a Korean body) is limited to 사회복지활동. The KIS visa-manual D-6 reference note states this explicitly: "「외국의 종교단체로부터 파견된」 종교인의 활동에 한정되기 때문에, 종교인이 대한민국의 종교단체에 취직하여 행하는 활동은 「종교(D-6)」의 체류자격에 해당되지 않음", and "초청자는 종단이나 교단의 대표자이며, 단위 교회 목사 등은 초청자가 될 수 없음". The current wording invites exactly the reading immigration rejects (hired by a local church), so this is not a harmless simplification.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, row 9 종교(D-6) (https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 종교(D-6) 참고사항(종교단체) (https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=204)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, visa D-6 (line 1073), replace the `how.en` text "Religious or social-welfare work for a religious organisation." with "Dispatched by a foreign religious or social-welfare organisation to its branch in Korea, or invited by a Korean religious or social-welfare organisation for social-welfare work. Being hired by a Korean church does not qualify." and update the ru / uz / uz-cyrl strings to match.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - Confirmed on both legs. The decree row is verbatim as quoted (local extract of 별표 1의2 row 9 shows 가 = 외국의 종교단체/사회복지단체로부터 파견되어 … 종교활동, 나 = 대한민국 내의 … 초청을 받아 사회복지활동, 다 = MOJ-recognised catch-all), so religious activity requires 파견 from abroad and the Korean-invitation branch is limited to 사회복지활동. I fetched the cited KIS visa-manual page myself and it states verbatim '종교인이 대한민국의 종교단체에 취직하여 행하는 활동은 종교(D-6)의 체류자격에 해당되지 않음' and that the inviter must be a 종단/교단 대표자, not a unit-church pastor. The official KIS 체류 길잡이 in the repo scratchpad (navi.txt lines 108 and 722-724) also describes D-6 using only the two 파견 branches. The current `how` drops the dispatch condition entirely and the phrase 'work for a religious organisation' reads as local employment, which is the exact case immigration rejects, so this is a substantive misstatement, not a harmless shortening. `how` is display-only in the engine, but that is what a wrong-note finding is about.
  - corrected claim: The claim stands. One nuance the finding omits: decree branch 다 lets the MOJ recognise other religious or social-welfare activity, so the dispatch requirement is not absolute in the decree text itself - but the manual's operational rule is as the finding states.
  - corrected fix: Same edit, but widen the destination to match the decree ('지부 또는 유관 종교단체'): 'Dispatched by a foreign religious or social-welfare organisation to its branch or an affiliated body in Korea, or invited by a Korean religious or social-welfare organisation for social-welfare work. Being hired by a Korean church does not qualify.' Update ru / uz / uz-cyrl to match.
  - citation: 별표 1의2 row 9 종교(D-6), scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 39-45; KIS 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 D-6 참고사항, fetched from https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-종교주재투자경영구직/?mod=document&uid=204; scratchpad/navi.txt lines 108, 722-724
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Decree text verified verbatim in the local copy of 별표 1의2 (header: "■ 출입국관리법 시행령 [별표 1의2] <개정 2026. 9. 15.> 장기체류자격(제12조 관련)", row "9. 종교 (D-6)"): 가 = 외국의 종교단체 또는 사회복지단체로부터 **파견되어** ... 종교활동, 나 = 대한민국 내의 종교단체 또는 사회복지단체의 **초청**을 받아 **사회복지활동**. So religious activity does require dispatch from abroad, and an invitation by a Korean body covers only social-welfare activity. I also fetched the finding's KIS-manual citation and it returns the quoted sentence verbatim: "「외국의 종교단체로부터 파견된」 종교인의 활동에 한정되기 때문에, 종교인이 대한민국의 종교단체에 취직하여 행하는 활동은 「종교(D-6)」의 체류자격에 해당되지 않음", plus "초청자는 종단이나 교단의 대표자이며, 단위 교회 목사 등은 초청자가 될 수 없음". The current how.en ("Religious or social-welfare work for a religious organisation") does read as permitting exactly the case the manual rejects. One overstatement: the decree has a third limb 다 (그 밖에 법무부장관이 인정하는 종교활동 또는 사회복지활동), so "only (a) or (b) qualify" is not literally complete.
  - corrected claim: D-6's how.en is misleading: under decree 별표 1의2 row 9, religious activity requires 파견 from a foreign religious/social-welfare body (가), an invitation by a Korean body covers social-welfare activity only (나), and there is a residual MOJ-discretion limb (다). Employment by a Korean religious organisation is explicitly excluded by the KIS manual.
  - corrected fix: Replace D-6 how.en (data/visamap.yaml line 1073) with text that keeps all three limbs, e.g. "Dispatched by a foreign religious or social-welfare organisation to its branch in Korea for religious work, or invited by a Korean religious or social-welfare organisation for social-welfare work (or other activity the Justice Ministry recognises). Simply being hired by a Korean church does not qualify." Update ru / uz / uz-cyrl to match.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 (개정 2026.9.15), row 9 종교(D-6), local text at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 39-45; 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 종교(D-6) 참고사항 via https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=204

### [medium] missing-edge: D-3 -> F-6-1

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-3's `next` offers no family route at all (no F-6-1, no F-2-3), so a D-3 trainee who marries a Korean citizen is told the tool has nothing for them. Every other status in the graph, including E-9, E-10 and H-2, offers F-6-1.
- **evidence**: F-6-1 (국민의 배우자) is open to any lawfully staying foreigner married to a Korean national; visadocs-targets.txt shows F-6-1 has both `change` and `abroad` lists. For D-3 specifically the in-Korea change is restricted (the 기술연수(D-3) chapter of the sojourn manual records 자격변경 허가 - 불가 원칙, and the F-6 chapter's 제한대상 pushes restricted-status holders to obtain the visa at a 재외공관, with an in-Korea exception only for 임신·출산/child-rearing), so the correct representation is the abroad route - which is exactly what the other three non-D-10 D-3 edges already use. Either way the edge must exist; right now the route is unreachable.
- **citation**: KIS 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 기술연수(D-3) chapter, 체류자격 변경허가 ("불가 원칙") - https://yoonhjs.com/dtype-visa%EB%AC%B8%ED%99%94%EC%9C%A0%ED%95%99%EC%97%B0%EC%88%98%EC%B7%A8%EC%9E%AC/?mod=document&uid=123 ; 결혼이민(F-6) 체류자격 변경 제한대상 및 재외공관 사증발급 - https://f-6.co.kr/%EA%B2%B0%ED%98%BC%EC%9D%B4%EB%AF%BC%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-f-6-1-%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9%EC%9C%BC%EB%A1%9C-%EC%B2%B4%EB%A5%98%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9-%EB%B3%80%EA%B2%BD/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-3 (line 245), add to `next`, after the existing `- {to: E-9, abroad: true}` entry: `- {to: F-6-1, abroad: true}` and `- {to: F-2-3, abroad: true}`.
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The finding's load-bearing evidence is false against the very data it audits. 'Every other status in the graph, including E-9, E-10 and H-2, offers F-6-1' - H-2 does NOT: its next is [F-4, F-5-14, E-7-4, E-7-4R]. Counting graph.txt, 68 of 114 nodes have no F-6-1 edge, including D-8-1/2/3/4, D-9-1/4/5, E-8, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2/3/T, G-1-5, G-1-6. So omitting F-6-1 from D-3 follows the file's existing editorial scope rather than being a unique unreachable route. Second, the fix is contradicted by its own citation: I fetched the f-6.co.kr page and its F-6-1 변경 제한대상 list is short-stay (C-3, C-4, B-1, B-2), illegal stayers, departure-extension holders, criminal records, and G-1 obtained while in those categories - D-3 and 연수 statuses are not on it, so nothing in that source pushes a D-3 holder to a 재외공관, and `abroad: true` is unsupported (the closest analogues E-9 and E-10 both use an in-Korea change for F-6-1, with no abroad flag). Third, F-2-3 in this file is 'Family of a permanent resident' with needs [{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}] - it has nothing to do with marrying a Korean citizen, so half the proposed fix does not serve the stated motivation. No primary source was offered for a D-3-specific rule.
  - corrected claim: D-3 has no F-6-1 edge, which is arguably a gap because its nearest analogues E-9 and E-10 do. But it is not a graph-wide inconsistency (most nodes lack it), H-2 does not have it, and F-2-3 is irrelevant to a Korean-citizen marriage.
  - corrected fix: If anything is added, add only `- F-6-1` (in-Korea change, no abroad flag, matching E-9 and E-10) and do not add F-2-3. Treat it as an optional scope extension, not a correctness fix, and the same argument would then apply to D-8-x, D-9-x and H-2.
  - citation: data/visamap.yaml H-2 next (lines ~1049-1058) and F-2-3 (lines 1183-1189); scratchpad/graph.txt (68/114 nodes without an F-6-1 edge); F-6-1 변경 제한대상 fetched from https://f-6.co.kr/결혼이민비자-f-6-1-자격으로-체류자격-변경/
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The structural claim is true: D-3's next in data/visamap.yaml (line 245) is exactly [D-10-1, D-2-2 abroad, E-7-1 abroad, E-9 abroad] and graph.txt confirms 4 edges with no F-6-1 or F-2-3, so a D-3 holder who marries a Korean is offered nothing. But three parts of the finding's support are wrong. (1) "Every other status in the graph, including E-9, E-10 and H-2, offers F-6-1" is false - I enumerated graph.txt: H-2, D-8-1/2/3/4, D-9-1/4/5, E-8, D-4-3, D-10-2/3/T also have no F-6-1. (2) The 기술연수(D-3) chapter's "자격변경 허가 - 불가 원칙" is the chapter's own 체류자격 변경허가 section, i.e. you cannot change INTO D-3 in Korea; that matches visadocs-targets.txt "D-3: abroad,extend" (no change). It says nothing about changing out of D-3. (3) I fetched the finding's own F-6 citation: the 제한대상 for an in-Korea change to F-6 is 단기사증 소지자 (C-3, C-4, B-1, B-2), 불법체류자, 출국을 위한 체류기간 연장 허가자, 일반 형사범, and G-1 derived from those - D-3 is not listed, so there is no documented basis for abroad: true; the plain in-Korea edge is the better-supported representation. Also F-2-3 is "family of a permanent resident", not a Korean citizen's spouse, so the marriage rationale does not support adding F-2-3.
  - corrected claim: D-3 offers no family route (F-6-1 or F-2-3) at all, which is a real gap - but it is not unique (H-2, D-8-*, D-9-*, E-8 and others also lack F-6-1), and the F-6 manual's 제한대상 does not restrict D-3, so the move is an ordinary in-Korea status change, not an abroad-only one.
  - corrected fix: In data/visamap.yaml under D-3 (line 245), add a plain `- F-6-1` entry to `next` (no abroad flag; D-3 is a long-term status and is absent from the F-6 자격변경 제한대상). Do not add F-2-3 on the strength of the marriage argument - add it only if you also intend D-3 holders to be shown the permanent-resident-family route. `abroad: true` is defensible only as a house convention matching the other three D-3 edges, not from the cited sources.
  - citation: data/visamap.yaml lines 245-256; scratchpad/graph.txt "=== D-3" block (next (4)); scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt line "D-3: abroad,extend"; 기술연수(D-3) 자격변경 "불가 원칙" https://yoonhjs.com/dtype-visa%EB%AC%B8%ED%99%94%EC%9C%A0%ED%95%99%EC%97%B0%EC%88%98%EC%B7%A8%EC%9E%AC/?mod=document&uid=123 ; F-6 제한대상 https://f-6.co.kr/%EA%B2%B0%ED%98%BC%EC%9D%B4%EB%AF%BC%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-f-6-1-%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9%EC%9C%BC%EB%A1%9C-%EC%B2%B4%EB%A5%98%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9-%EB%B3%80%EA%B2%BD/

### [medium] missing-edge: D-7 -> D-10-1

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-7's `next` has no D-10-1, so a transferee whose posting ends has no way to stay in Korea and look for a local job - the single most common exit from D-7. The graph gives D-10-1 to E-1..E-7, D-2, D-4 and even D-3, B-1 and C-3, but to none of D-5/D-6/D-7/D-8/D-9.
- **evidence**: KIS sojourn manual, 구직(D-10) chapter, 체류자격 변경허가 가. 허용대상 1-1), extracted verbatim from the official manual PDF: "학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자로 구직 점수표에서 총 190점 중 기본항목이 20점 이상으로 총 득점이 60점 이상인자 - 단, 법무부장관 고시국가* 국민 중 B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 자격에 대해서는 총 득점이 80점 이상인 경우에만 체류자격 변경 허용". D-7 is a 국내 합법 체류자 and is not in the restricted-status list, so a D-7 holder with a degree and 60+ points may change to D-10-1 in Korea. The same reasoning applies to D-1, D-5 and D-6 (see the separate D-1 finding).
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) - 체류자격 변경허가, 허용대상 1-1) (pp. 141-142) - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-7 (line 1083), add to `next` after `- E-7-1`: an entry `- to: D-10-1` with the same note the other D-10-1 edges use ("Bachelor's degree and 60+ points (80+ for citizens of the 21 notified countries, including Uzbekistan); 1 year in total, 3 years with 80+ points."). Add the same entry to D-5 (line 1063) and D-6 (line 1073).
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - I could not refute this. The quoted 허용대상 text is verbatim in the repo's own extract of the official 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 구직(D-10) chapter (scratchpad/d10.txt lines ~69-80): '학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자로 … 총 득점이 60점 이상인자 - 단, 법무부장관 고시국가 국민 중 B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 자격에 대해서는 … 80점 이상인 경우에만'. I also read the chapter's 나. 제한대상 (lines 161-180): the D-10-1 restrictions are criminal/immigration-record grounds plus an unexpired contract with the previous employer - D-5, D-6 and D-7 are nowhere excluded. So a D-7/D-5/D-6 holder with a degree and 60+ points may change in Korea, and the engine can only ever offer what is in `next`, so the route is genuinely unreachable today (verified: no D-1/D-5/D-6/D-7/D-8/D-9 node offers D-10-1).
  - corrected claim: The rule and the gap are right. 'The single most common exit from D-7' is an unsupported assertion, and note the mechanism differs from the E-1..E-7 edges: those rest on the separate 전문직종 근무 경력자 points-exemption for expired E-series contracts, whereas D-5/D-6/D-7 would use the ordinary points route.
  - corrected fix: Add `- to: D-10-1` to D-7, D-5 and D-6, but do NOT copy the standard note verbatim: the 80-point clause applies only to B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10 and G-1 holders, so for D-5/D-6/D-7 the note should read "Bachelor's degree and 60+ points; 1 year in total, 3 years with 80+ points." (The existing D-4-x edges carry the same inaccuracy, so this is a pre-existing file-wide wording issue, not one the fix introduces.)
  - citation: scratchpad/d10.txt lines 69-80 (허용대상 1-1) and 161-180 (제한대상), extracted from 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 구직(D-10) pp. 141-143; scratchpad/graph.txt D-10-1 in-edge list
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Both halves verified. ### [low] missing-edge: D-1 -> D-10-1

D-1's list is the shortest of any long-term D status in the graph while D-1 is not a restricted status for any target chapter I checked (it is explicitly named in the E-7 change 허용대상 and in the F-2-99 허용대상).
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) - 체류자격 변경허가, 허용대상 1-1) (pp. 141-142) - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-1 (line 1059), change `next: [E-7-1, F-2-99, F-6-1, F-2-3]` to a block list and add a `- to: D-10-1` entry carrying the standard D-10-1 note used elsewhere in the file.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - Same official text, independently checked in the repo's manual extract: the D-10-1 허용대상 is any 국내 합법 체류자 with a 전문학사 or higher and 60+ points, and the 제한대상 list for D-10-1 (scratchpad/d10.txt lines 161-180) does not mention D-1. D-1 (문화예술) is a long-term lawful status, so the route is legally open, and D-1's `next` in the file is [E-7-1, F-2-99, F-6-1, F-2-3] with no D-10-1, so the engine will never offer it. Severity is fairly judged low (few D-1 holders hold a degree in a job-market-relevant sense), but the finding is not wrong.

- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Verified. data/visamap.yaml line 1059 is `next: [E-7-1, F-2-99, F-6-1, F-2-3]` - 4 entries, no D-10-1. The 구직(D-10) 허용대상 1-1) text (inha.txt lines 71-80) requires only "학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자" with 60+ points, and the 고시국가 80-point list (B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1) does not include D-1; the D-10-1 제한대상 is only about an unexpired employment contract. ### [low] wrong-gives: D-7 -> E-7-1

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: D-7 has no `gives`, so a reader who explicitly answers "0 years' experience" is shown D-7 -> E-7-1 as BLOCKED, even though holding D-7 legally implies at least 1 year of prior employment with the overseas parent. The tool hides the main professional exit from D-7 from people who can in fact take it.
- **evidence**: E-7-1's `needs` is [{edu:4},{edu:3,experience:1},{experience:5},{koreanEdu:2},{edu:3,rank:1},{edu:3,stem:true,ageUnder:30,countries:asiaPilot}] (data/visamap.yaml lines ~790-795), so a bachelor's-degree reader needs experience >= 1 to pass. Decree table 1-2 row 10 주재(D-7) 가 requires "외국의 공공기관·단체 또는 회사의 본사, 지사, 그 밖의 사업소 등에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람" (and 나 likewise "1년 이상 근무한 사람"), i.e. everyone who completed a D-7 posting has >= 1 year of experience. Per engine.md, a `gives` level raises the reader's answered level, so `gives: {experience: 1}` fixes the false block without affecting readers who left the field blank.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, row 10 주재(D-7) 가/나 - https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-7 (line 1083), add a sibling key to `next`: `gives: {experience: 1}`.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - The mechanism checks out but the finding is overstated. Confirmed: 별표 1의2 row 10 주재(D-7) 가 and 나 both require '1년 이상 근무한 사람' (scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 47-62), and reading assets/js/visamap.js I confirmed factsAt() applies `gives` for path.slice(0, level), which includes the visa the step leaves from, so `gives: {experience: 1}` on D-7 would indeed raise an answered 0 to 1 on the D-7 -> E-7-1 step (LEVELS branch uses Math.max over the answered value). Two corrections, though. (a) The stated failure scenario is incomplete: with edu=3 answered and experience=0, the E-7-1 option {edu: 3, rank: 1} evaluates to 'maybe' while rank is unanswered, and checkNeeds prefers any 'maybe' option over 'no', so the step is NOT blocked. Blocking additionally requires the reader to answer the university-ranking question as 'Not in QS top 500' (and to be non-STEM, or 30+, or outside asiaPilot). (b) 'everyone who completed a D-7 posting has >= 1 year of experience' is not literally true: branch 가 carries an express carve-out - '국가기간산업 또는 국책사업에 종사하려는 경우나 그 밖에 법무부장관이 필요하다고 인정하는 경우에는 1년 이상의 근무요건을 적용하지 않는다'. Neither point defeats the fix, which is a simplification of the same kind the file already uses (D-2-x gives edu/koreanEdu).
  - corrected claim: D-7 implies 1 year of prior employment with the overseas entity in all but the statutory 국가기간산업/국책사업/MOJ-discretion carve-out, and the missing `gives` produces a false BLOCKED on D-7 -> E-7-1 for a reader who answers bachelor's (abroad), experience 'less than 1 year' AND university ranking 'not in QS top 500'.
  - corrected fix: Add `gives: {experience: 1}` to D-7 as proposed; blast radius is small (among D-7's targets only E-7-1 gates on experience). Optionally also give D-7 its own `needs: {experience: 1}`, since D-7 currently has no needs at all and so is offered from D-8-1 to a reader who answered 'less than 1 year' - the inverse of the same omission.
  - citation: 별표 1의2 row 10 주재(D-7), scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 47-62 (including the 1년 요건 waiver clause); assets/js/visamap.js lines 104-158 (profileFacts, factsAt, checkFact, checkNeeds) and 237-242 (stem/rank are only set when edu is answered); i18n/en.yaml vm_exp_0 = 'Less than 1 year', vm_rank_0 = 'Not in QS top 500'
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The decree quote and the engine mechanism both check out, but the failure scenario is overstated. Decree 별표 1의2 row 10 주재(D-7) 가 does say "...에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람" and 나 likewise, which I read verbatim in scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 47-62 - but 가 carries a bracketed carve-out the finding omits: "...국가기간산업 또는 국책사업에 종사하려는 경우나 그 밖에 법무부장관이 필요하다고 인정하는 경우에는 1년 이상의 근무요건을 적용하지 않는다", so not literally every D-7 holder has a year. Engine mechanism confirmed: render() calls assess(level, step) with parent = path[level-1] and factsAt(level) does path.slice(0, level), which includes the visa being left, so a `gives` on D-7 does affect D-7's own outbound steps. E-7-1's needs list is exactly as quoted (data/visamap.yaml lines 790-795). However, the false block is narrower than claimed: checkNeeds blocks only when every alternative is 'no', and for a reader with edu=3 and experience=0 the alternative {edu:3, rank:1} returns 'maybe' while "University ranking" is unanswered (rank is undefined unless the select is set), so the row still shows as open. The block needs the reader to have also answered the ranking question (e.g. "Not in QS top 500", value 0). And the fix helps only bachelor's holders - with edu=1 or 2 and no Korean schooling, experience:1 still fails every alternative.
  - corrected claim: D-7 has no `gives`, so a reader who answers bachelor's degree, "Less than 1 year" experience AND a university ranking (e.g. "Not in QS top 500") sees D-7 -> E-7-1 as BLOCKED, even though decree 별표 1의2 row 10 requires 1+ year of prior service with the overseas entity for D-7 (waived only for 국가기간산업/국책사업 or other MOJ-recognised cases). `gives: {experience: 1}` removes that false block for degree holders; it does not help readers below bachelor's level.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: add `gives: {experience: 1}` as a sibling of `next` under D-7 (data/visamap.yaml line 1083). Optionally mirror it on D-7's how text, which already states the 1-year rule.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 (개정 2026.9.15) row 10 주재(D-7) 가/나, scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 47-62; assets/js/visamap.js lines 117-122 (factsAt slices path[0..level-1]), 147-158 (checkNeeds: 'no' only if no alternative is yes/maybe), 397-399 (render passes level with parent = path[level-1]), 241 (rank only read from the extras select); data/visamap.yaml lines 790-795 (E-7-1 needs); layouts/visa-map.html lines 212-219 and i18n/en.yaml vm_rank_0 "Not in QS top 500", vm_exp_0 "Less than 1 year"

### [low] wrong-needs: NONE -> D-7

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: D-7 carries no `needs` at all, so the tool offers D-7 to a reader who has explicitly answered that they have no work experience, although 1 year of prior service with the overseas parent is a statutory entry condition.
- **evidence**: Decree table 1-2 row 10 주재(D-7): 가 "외국의 공공기관·단체 또는 회사의 본사, 지사, 그 밖의 사업소 등에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람으로서 ... 필수 전문인력으로 파견되어 근무하려는 사람"; 나 "... 해외 현지법인이나 해외지점에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람 ...". The engine already models this with the `experience` fact (used in E-7-1/E-7-2 needs), and per engine.md an unanswered `experience` never blocks, so adding the gate only filters readers who actively said 0. The decree's own waiver ("국가기간산업 또는 국책사업에 종사하려는 경우나 그 밖에 법무부장관이 필요하다고 인정하는 경우에는 1년 이상의 근무요건을 적용하지 않는다") is narrow enough that it belongs in a note, not in the absence of any gate.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, row 10 주재(D-7) - https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-7 (line 1083), add `needs: {experience: 1}` as a sibling of `next`.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - I confirmed the rule against the primary decree text myself. The local copy of 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 row 10 주재(D-7) in the scratchpad (byl_02.txt lines 47-62) reads verbatim: 가 "외국의 공공기관ㆍ단체 또는 회사의 본사, 지사, 그 밖의 사업소 등에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람으로서 ... 필수 전문인력으로 파견되어 근무하려는 사람" and 나 "... 해외 현지법인이나 해외지점에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람 ...". So 1년 이상 근무 is an entry condition on BOTH branches, and the waiver (국가기간산업/국책사업 또는 법무부장관 인정) sits only inside the 가 bracket. The engine reading is also right: D-7 in data/visamap.yaml (line 1083) has only name/how/next, no `needs`, so nothing gates it; its two in-edges (NONE -> D-7 at line 1526 and D-8-1 -> D-7 at line 1109) are both bare codes with no per-move `needs`, so a visa-level `needs` would apply to both, which is correct since both routes require the 1 year abroad. No visa in the file `gives` an `experience` level (gives appears only at lines 211, 338, 368, 407, 445, 1058, 1349-1423, and the only level-giving ones are edu/koreanEdu), so the gate cannot misfire via an inherited level. The gate is also unusually well calibrated to the UI: layouts/visa-map.html offers experience values 0/1/3/5/8 and i18n/en.yaml maps vm_exp_0 to "Less than 1 year" - so `experience: 1` blocks exactly and only the answer "less than 1 year", which is precisely the statutory 1년 이상 threshold, and per engine.md an unanswered value still never blocks. The MOJ waiver is not a reason to refute: a blocked option is still shown behind the "show N" button with a reason, and the 나 branch has no waiver at all. The fix is also conventional for this file (sibling `needs:` under the visa, exactly as D-8-1 line 553-ish `needs: {invest: 1}` and E-1/E-2 do).
  - corrected claim: Accurate, with one wording nit: the UI option the gate would filter is "Less than 1 year" (vm_exp_0), not "no work experience" - which makes the gate line up exactly with the decree's 1년 이상 threshold rather than over-reaching.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: add `needs: {experience: 1}` under D-7 (data/visamap.yaml line 1083) as a sibling of `next`. Optionally pair it with a note about the 국가기간산업/국책사업 waiver.

- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Verified against the current primary text, not just the 2019 copy the finding cites. /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/byl_02.txt is a law.go.kr extraction of 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 <개정 2026. 9. 15.>, and lines 47-62 (row 10 주재/D-7) read verbatim: 가 '외국의 공공기관ㆍ단체 또는 회사의 본사, 지사, 그 밖의 사업소 등에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람으로서 ... 필수 전문인력으로 파견되어 근무하려는 사람[기업투자(D-8) 체류자격에 해당하는 사람은 제외하며, 국가기간산업 또는 국책사업에 종사하려는 경우나 그 밖에 법무부장관이 필요하다고 인정하는 경우에는 1년 이상의 근무요건을 적용하지 않는다]' and 나 '... 해외 현지법인이나 해외지점에서 1년 이상 근무한 사람 ...'. Both limbs carry the 1-year floor, and the waiver is quoted accurately and is as narrow as the finding says. The code side checks out too: data/visamap.yaml:1083 D-7 has only name/how/next and no needs; NONE lists D-7 in next (data/visamap.yaml:1526), D-8-1 also offers D-7; `experience` is a real reader-answered select (assets/js/visamap.js:323, SELECTS) and per engine.md an unanswered fact never blocks, so the gate only filters readers who explicitly answered 0. D-7's own `how` string already says '1+ year with a company abroad', so today the prose and the gating disagree. I applied the proposed one-line change to a copy and it parses cleanly (D-7 needs = {experience: 1}, next still 8 items, no anchor side effects). Only caveat, which does not refute the finding: the engine's `experience` is generic work experience, not service with the sending entity, so the gate is a one-sided approximation (it blocks 0-year readers correctly but passes someone with 3 unrelated years).

  - corrected fix: In data/visamap.yaml under D-7 (line 1083), add `needs: {experience: 1}` as a sibling of `next`, and in the same change make the 1 year explicit about who it is with plus record the waiver, since `experience` is generic: extend D-7's `how` (or add a note) along the lines of '1+ year with the sending company abroad; waived for national key industries, state projects, or where the Minister of Justice so recognises.'

### [low] missing-edge: D-1 -> E-6-1

- **state**: REFUTED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.4)
- **claim**: D-1 (non-profit arts and culture) offers no route to E-6-1 (paid arts/performance), which is the natural occupational step once a D-1 artist takes a paid performance or recording contract.
- **evidence**: Decree table 1-2 row 4 문화예술(D-1) is limited to "수익을 목적으로 하지 않는 문화 또는 예술 관련 활동", so any paid activity forces a move to 예술흥행(E-6). The documented in-Korea 체류자격 변경 허용대상 for E-6-1/E-6-3 that I could find names only D-2 and D-10 holders (plus discretionary B-1/B-2/C-3 cases), so the correct edge is most likely the abroad route (visadocs-targets.txt shows E-6-1 has an `abroad` list). I could not find a source that either permits or forbids a direct D-1 -> E-6 in-Korea change, so the direction of the `abroad` flag here is unsourced; the existence of some route is not.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, row 4 문화예술(D-1) - https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf ; E-6 자격변경 허용대상 (D-2/D-10 only) - https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/e6-visa-art-performance-invitee-qualifications-requirements-documents-and-extension
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-1 (line 1059), add `- {to: E-6-1, abroad: true}` to `next` (drop the `abroad` flag if immigration confirms a direct in-Korea change from D-1).
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The omission is a deliberate, source-consistent design choice, and the proposed fix contradicts both the finding's own source and the file's convention. (1) E-6-1's in-edge set in the built graph is exactly {D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4, D-10-1, NONE} - i.e. precisely the D-2/D-10 change-allowed set the finding itself cites, plus the abroad route. E-6-3 has only NONE as an in-edge. So the author already applied the "change to E-6 only from D-2/D-10" rule when building these in-edges; D-1 -> E-6-1 is absent for the same reason every other status is absent, not by oversight. (2) I fetched the finding's own cited page and it does say in-Korea change to E-6 is for "job-seeking (D-10) status or student visa (D-2) status", with B-1/B-2/C-3 only "if there are unavoidable reasons or if necessary in the national interest" - which supports NOT adding a D-1 in-Korea edge. (3) The proposed `abroad: true` edge breaks the file's convention: every `abroad`/`abroadFor` edge in the data hangs off a status where an in-Korea change is barred (B-1, B-2, C-3-x, C-4, D-2-6, D-2-8, D-3, E-8, E-9, E-10, H-1). No long-term D status (D-1, D-5, D-6, D-7, D-8-x) carries a single `abroad` edge - the map simply does not model "leave Korea and apply from scratch" out of long-term statuses. Adding one only to D-1 -> E-6-1 would be arbitrary, and by the same logic one would have to add abroad edges to E-6-1 from D-5, D-6, D-7, E-7-x and so on. (4) The finding self-admits "I could not find a source that either permits or forbids a direct D-1 -> E-6 in-Korea change, so the direction of the abroad flag here is unsourced", and its only citation for the gating rule is a commercial visa-agency blog, not a primary source; I could not reach any primary source (law.go.kr/hikorea) to confirm it, and the web-search budget for this session was exhausted. Per the default-to-refuted rule, an unsourced flag direction plus a convention-breaking fix means refuted. Note also that D-1 already offers E-7-1 as its paid-work route, so a D-1 artist taking paid work is not left with no modeled option.
  - corrected claim: The decree's non-profit limit on D-1 is real (별표 1의2 row 4: 수익을 목적으로 하지 않는 문화 또는 예술 관련 활동), but the absence of a D-1 -> E-6-1 edge matches the documented E-6 change-of-status set (D-2/D-10 only), which the file already applies consistently across all of E-6-1 and E-6-3; D-1 already has E-7-1 for paid work.
  - corrected fix: No change. If anything is wanted, it is a note on D-1 saying paid performance work requires a new E-6 visa rather than a new `next` edge - and only once a primary source (hikorea/KIS 체류자격 변경 허용대상) is actually checked.
  - citation: data/visamap.yaml D-1 line 1059 (next: [E-7-1, F-2-99, F-6-1, F-2-3]) and E-6-1 line 709; full in-edge enumeration from /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/graph.txt (E-6-1 in-edges only D-2-1..4, D-10-1, NONE; E-6-3 only NONE; every abroad/abroadFor edge belongs to a short-term or change-barred status); finding's own source fetched and verified at https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/e6-visa-art-performance-invitee-qualifications-requirements-documents-and-extension (D-10/D-2, with B-1/B-2/C-3 only in exceptional cases)
- **verdict (sources)**: REFUTED - The asserted edge is contradicted by the repo's own primary extraction of the KIS manual. data/visadocs.yaml, under E-6-1 > change, cites source '외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 24. 예술흥행(E-6) › 체류자격 변경허가' (txt lines 8875-8912) and lists exactly two permitted cases: a D-2 / D-10 holder changing to E-6, and the F-3 spouse of an E-1~E-5 / E-6 (not E-6-2) / E-7 professional. D-1 is not in that list, so there is no documented in-Korea D-1 -> E-6 change, and the map already reflects this: E-6-1 appears in `next` for exactly D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4 (lines 336, 366, 405, 443), D-10-1 (520) and NONE (1514). The file follows these change lists strictly - D-1 -> E-7-1 exists precisely because the E-7-1 change list in data/visadocs.yaml names 'Registered D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6 ... holders'. The decree citation only establishes that D-1 is limited to '수익을 목적으로 하지 않는' activity (confirmed verbatim, byl_02.txt lines 18-21, 개정 2026.9.15); that says paid work needs some other status, but it supports no D-1 -> E-6 edge. The second citation is allvisakorea.com, a commercial blog, and what it says (D-2/D-10 only) argues against the finding. The `abroad: true` fallback is admitted by the author to be unsourced, and it is not the file's convention: every edge-level `abroad: true` in data/visamap.yaml sits on a status where in-Korea change is barred outright (B-1, B-2, C-3-x, C-4, D-3, E-8, E-9, E-10, H-1), never as a generic 'leave Korea and reapply' link off a long-term D status. Adding one for D-1 -> E-6 alone, with no source, would be an inconsistent special case; applied consistently it would mean an abroad edge from every status to every consular-issuable visa.
  - corrected claim: D-1 -> E-6-1 is correctly absent: the official E-6 체류자격 변경허가 list covers only D-2/D-10 holders and F-3 spouses of E-1~E-6/E-7 professionals. The checkable gap in this area is a different edge: F-3 is in that E-6 change list, yet F-3's `next` in data/visamap.yaml has no E-6-1 (it lists E-7-1, E-2, D-2-2, D-10-1, F-2-99, F-2-71, F-6-1, F-5-13, F-2-3, D-2-3, D-2-4).
  - corrected fix: No change to D-1. If the E-6 change list is to be followed through, the sourced edit is to add `E-6-1` (and E-6-3) to F-3's `next` in data/visamap.yaml, per the 'Spouse (F-3) of an E-1~E-5 / E-6 (not E-6-2) / E-7 professional' variant in data/visadocs.yaml - that should be raised and judged as its own finding, not folded into this one.
  - citation: data/visadocs.yaml E-6-1 > change, source '외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 24. 예술흥행(E-6) › 체류자격 변경허가' (variants: D-2/D-10 holder; F-3 spouse of E-1~E-5/E-6/E-7); data/visadocs.yaml E-7-1 > change (names D-1 explicitly); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 <개정 2026.9.15> row 4 문화예술(D-1) at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 18-21; data/visamap.yaml lines 1059, 336/366/405/443/520/1514

## graph / d10

### [high] missing-edge: D-10-T -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-10-T offers only E-7-T and F-2-T, so the tool dead-ends the exact profile the status was created for: a fresh top-100 master's graduate can legally change to an ordinary E-7-1 (or E-1/E-3/E-5) job in Korea, but the map can never suggest it.
- **evidence**: The sojourn manual's D-10 chapter defines the 해당자 for both 일반구직(D-10-1) and 최우수인재(D-10-T) as people doing 연수/구직활동 in order to take a job in 교수(E-1)·회화지도(E-2)·연구(E-3)·기술지도(E-4)·전문직업(E-5)·예술흥행(E-6)·특정활동(E-7) fields, and D-10-T is simply 'Top-Tier 학력 요건은 갖추었으나 취업 미확정' (p.140, p.143). The E-7 chapter's change-of-status list covers '등록된 D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10 자격 소지자' (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가; mirrored in this repo as visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change variant 2), and D-10-T is a D-10 sub-code, so the change is documented. Worse, the only work target the data offers (E-7-T) carries needs [{edu:4,rank:3,experience:8},{edu:5,rank:3,experience:5}], so any reader who answers 'no experience' - the normal D-10-T holder - sees E-7-T BLOCKED and has no open work option at all.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 구직(D-10) › 해당자 / 체류자격 변경허가 4) 최우수인재(D-10-T), pp.140-143 — https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10 holders)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, visas.D-10-T.next: change `next: [E-7-T, F-2-T]` to `next: [E-7-T, F-2-T, E-7-1, E-1, E-3, E-5, F-2-7S, F-6-1]` (keep E-7-T first). At minimum add `E-7-1`.

### [medium] wrong-age: D-10-3 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-10-3's age gate `age: [18, 34]` is too loose: the manual caps the high-tech intern at under 30 unless the applicant holds a master's or higher (then under 35), so the tool offers D-10-3 to 30-34 year-old bachelor's holders who cannot get it - and it contradicts the visa's own 'how' text.
- **evidence**: Manual, 구직(D-10) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3) 첨단기술인턴(D-10-3): '세계 우수 대학(본교만 해당) 첨단기술 분야 학사과정 이상 재학생 또는 학위취득일로부터 3년이 경과되지 않은 졸업생으로 만 30세(석사 이상의 경우 만 35세) 미만인 자' (p.143; identical wording in 해당자, p.140). The data's own how text already says 'under 30 (under 35 with a master's)', so the gate is inconsistent with the data itself. The conditional limit IS expressible with the engine's needs-list + ageUnder fact, and because an unanswered fact never blocks, a reader who leaves education empty still sees the option.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 구직(D-10) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3) 첨단기술인턴(D-10-3), p.143 — https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do (also yoonhjs.com D10 발급요건: '만 30세 미만인 자(석사 이상의 경우 만 35세)')
- **fix**: In visas.D-10-3, keep `age: [18, 34]` and replace `needs: {stem: true, rank: 1}` with `needs: [{stem: true, rank: 1, ageUnder: 30}, {stem: true, rank: 1, edu: 4, ageUnder: 35}]`.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-3 -> D-2-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: D-10-3 does not offer D-2-2/D-2-3/D-2-4, although the manual gives high-tech interns an explicit, nationality-blind privilege to change to a student status on the admission letter alone - a benefit D-10-1 and D-10-2 already carry in the data.
- **evidence**: Manual, 구직(D-10) › 첨단기술인턴 체류지원 특례 › '(유학활동 지원) 국적과 무관하게 국내 대학에서 유학하고자 하는 경우 표준입학허가서만으로 유학(D-2) 체류자격변경 허용' (pp.158-159). D-10-1 and D-10-2 both list D-2-2/D-2-3/D-2-4 in the data with the 'admission letter + proof of funds' note, so omitting them from D-10-3 - the one status where the funds proof is explicitly waived - is an oversight, not a simplification.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 구직(D-10) › 첨단기술인턴 체류지원 특례 › (유학활동 지원), pp.158-159 — https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: In visas.D-10-3.next add `D-2-2`, `D-2-3`, `D-2-4`, with a note along the lines of 'High-tech interns change to a student status on the standard admission letter alone, whatever your nationality' (do not copy D-10-1's proof-of-funds note).

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-2 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-10-2, D-10-3 and D-10-T offer no family route at all (no F-6-1, no F-2-3), although D-10-1 does; marriage to a Korean national permits a change to F-6-1 from any lawful status, including every D-10 sub-code.
- **evidence**: F-6-1 has a documented in-Korea change list (visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend') that is keyed to being the spouse of a Korean national, not to the current status, and the data already offers F-6-1 from 45 statuses including B-1/B-2, D-4, E-9, E-10 and D-10-1. There is no rule that singles out D-10-2/D-10-3/D-10-T, so the omission is an internal inconsistency that hides the most common status change in Korea from readers standing on those three nodes.
- **citation**: data/visadocs.yaml / visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend' (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 결혼이민(F-6) › 체류자격 변경허가); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-2 (F-6-1 국민의 배우자)
- **fix**: Add `F-6-1` and `F-2-3` to visas.D-10-2.next, visas.D-10-3.next and visas.D-10-T.next, exactly as they appear in visas.D-10-1.next.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-2 -> D-9-5

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-10-2 (start-up preparation) does not offer D-9-5, even though the sole-proprietor route out of D-10-2 is documented in the manual and is the natural non-corporate exit for exactly this status; D-10-1 offers it but D-10-2 does not.
- **evidence**: The D-9 chapter documents a change of status for '외국인 개인사업자': a D-2 or D-10 holder with a Korean master's degree (or a bachelor's plus 30 start-up-immigration points) who invests at least KRW 100 million, and its document list includes the OASIS training certificate - the defining credential of D-10-2 (repo data/visadocs.yaml targets['D-9-5'].change, source '외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 17. 무역경영(D-9) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3. 외국인 개인사업자에 대한 체류자격 변경허가'; visadocs-targets.txt shows D-9-5: change,extend). D-10-2's activity scope is '창업과 관련된 제반 준비활동' (manual p.140), i.e. founders in preparation, so the person who ends up as a sole proprietor rather than a corporation can only go to D-9-5.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 17. 무역경영(D-9) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3. 외국인 개인사업자에 대한 체류자격 변경허가 (as captured in data/visadocs.yaml targets['D-9-5'].change)
- **fix**: In visas.D-10-2.next add `D-9-5` (after D-8-4).

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-1 -> F-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: No D-10 status offers F-4, so an ethnic Korean job seeker (a large share of this site's Uzbek, Kazakh and Chinese readers) can never be shown the single easiest status change available to them; in the whole graph F-4 is only reachable from short-term visas and H-2.
- **evidence**: A change of status to 재외동포(F-4) is documented for any 외국국적동포 lawfully staying in Korea, with no requirement on the current status and no income, education or career requirement once Korean descent is proven (visadocs-targets.txt lists F-4: change,abroad,extend, i.e. the manual documents an in-Korea change to F-4). The engine already has the `korean` fact and F-4 carries needs {korean: true}, so adding the edge cannot leak the option to non-Koreans. D-10-1 is the second-busiest node in the graph, and F-4 strictly dominates D-10 for anyone eligible (free employment, 3-year stays), so its absence is a real recommendation failure rather than a cosmetic one.
- **citation**: data/visadocs.yaml / visadocs-targets.txt 'F-4: change,abroad,extend' (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 재외동포(F-4) › 체류자격 변경허가); 재외동포(F-4) 자격 통합 안내 — https://www.mofa.go.kr/us-ko/brd/m_4478/view.do?seq=1110182
- **fix**: Add `F-4` to visas.D-10-1.next (and, for consistency, to D-10-2.next, D-10-3.next and D-10-T.next). No extra needs are required: F-4's own `needs: {korean: true}` gates it.

### [medium] wrong-note: D-10-1 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The D-10-1 -> F-2-R note states '... and 5 years in a depopulating area' as if five years of past residence were an entry requirement; the real rule is a forward-looking pledge to live and work in the recommended area for five years, which nobody on D-10 could ever satisfy retrospectively.
- **evidence**: The 지역특화형 비자 (우수인재형) requirement is that the applicant actually reside in the recommending municipality at the time of application and undertake to keep residing/working in the area: '신청일 기준 해당 지자체에 실거주하며, 해당 지자체에 5년 이상 계속 거주할 것을 확약' and '허가일 기준 최초 2년은 추천지역에 실거주해야 하며, 2년 경과 시 허용지역으로 이전 가능'. The 2026 update likewise describes it as a commitment: holders 'must commit to residing in the recommended region for a minimum of 5 years while maintaining employment or operating a business.' The rest of the note (local-government recommendation, Korean associate degree or regional-wage income, TOPIK 3 / KIIP 3, and the pre-D-10 status exclusion list D-3/D-4/E-6-2/E-8/E-9/E-10/G-1/H-1) is correct, including the TOPIK 3 relaxation effective 2026-08-03 - only the five-year clause is inverted. The same wording also sits in visas['F-2-R'].how.
- **citation**: 지역특화형 우수인재 F-2-R 요건 정리 — https://yoonhjs.com/korea_visa/korea_visa_f_type/%EC%A7%80%EC%97%AD%ED%8A%B9%ED%99%94%ED%98%95-%EC%9A%B0%EC%88%98%EC%9D%B8%EB%8C%80-f2-r-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EC%B4%9D%EC%A0%95%EB%A6%AC/ ; 2026 regional-visa update (TOPIK 3, 5-year commitment) — https://www.jobploy.kr/en/content/new-visas-2026-e74r-f2r-f4r ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ. 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2)
- **fix**: In the D-10-1 -> F-2-R note (and in visas['F-2-R'].how) replace 'and 5 years in a depopulating area' with wording such as 'you must already live in the recommending municipality and undertake to live and work in that depopulating area for 5 years (the first 2 years in the recommended area itself)'.

### [low] missing-edge: D-10-1 -> E-6-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: D-10-1 offers E-6-1 but not E-6-3, although the D-10 chapter explicitly admits the sports branch of E-6 as a job-search target and excludes only E-6-2.
- **evidence**: Manual, 구직(D-10) › 해당자: D-10 is for people doing 연수/구직활동 to take up work in E-1~E-7 fields, with the footnote '단, 예술흥행(E-6) 자격 중 유흥업소 등의 흥행활동(E-6-2)은 제외하고, 순수예술 및 스포츠분야만 허용' (p.140). 순수예술 = E-6-1, 스포츠 = E-6-3 (professional athlete/coach). The E-6 chapter's change-of-status list has a dedicated 'D-2 / D-10 holder changing to E-6' document set (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-6-3'].change exists per visadocs-targets.txt 'E-6-3: change,abroad,extend'), so the route is documented for E-6-3 as well.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 구직(D-10) › 해당자, p.140 — https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 24. 예술흥행(E-6) › 체류자격 변경허가 (D-2/D-10 → E-6)
- **fix**: Add `E-6-3` to visas.D-10-1.next, next to E-6-1.

### [low] wrong-note: D-10-3 -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: The D-10-3 -> E-7-1 note says that after 1+ year of internship 'education and experience requirements are waived', but drops the two conditions the manual attaches: the job must be formal employment in the field of the internship AND the wage must reach the previous year's per-capita GNI, which is above the ordinary E-7-1 salary floor the data quotes (KRW 31.1M = 80% of GNI).
- **evidence**: Manual, 구직(D-10) › 첨단기술인턴 체류지원 특례: '(취업 지원) 첨단기술인턴(D-10-3) 자격으로 국내 기업 등에서 1년 이상 인턴활동을 한 자로서, 인턴 활동 분야에 정식 취업하고, 임금이 전년도 1인당 국민총소득(GNI) 이상일 경우, 특정활동(E-7) 체류자격변경 시 학력, 경력 요건 면제' (pp.158-159). As written, a reader who takes the waiver route at the data's advertised 31.1M floor would be refused.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 (2026) › 구직(D-10) › 첨단기술인턴 체류지원 특례 › (취업 지원), pp.158-159 — https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: Rewrite the note on the D-10-3 -> E-7-1 entry to: 'After 1+ year of internship, if you are formally hired in the field of your internship at a salary of at least the previous year's per-capita GNI, the education and experience requirements are waived.'

### [low] wrong-needs: D-10-1 -> F-2-7

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: The D-10-1 -> F-2-7 move carries no gating at all (and F-2-7 itself has no visa-level needs), so the tool shows F-2-7 as an OPEN next step to every D-10 reader, including one who answered 'foreign bachelor's only', for whom no F-2-7 track from D-10 exists.
- **evidence**: From a D-10 status the only workable F-2-7 track is the Korean-degree track: '국내에서 정규과정 석사 이상의 학위를 취득하고 5년 이내에 전문직 직종에 취업이 확정되었거나 취업 중인 자' (with D-2/D-10 legal stay counted); the professional track requires 1+ year already held on E-1~E-7-1 or D-5~D-9, which a D-10 holder by definition does not have. The data's own note on this edge states the Korean master's condition, but because the engine only blocks on explicitly answered facts, nothing is enforced. Adding koreanEdu to the move's needs filters only readers who said they have less, which is the intended behaviour per engine.md.
- **citation**: F-2-7 점수제 우수인재 신청 대상 (국내 석사 이상 학위 취득 후 5년 이내 전문직 취업; 전문직 트랙은 E-1~E-7/D-5~D-9 1년 이상 체류자) — https://hanvisa.net/f-2-7-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EC%A0%90%EC%88%98%EC%A0%9C-%EC%9A%B0%EC%88%98%EC%9D%B8%EC%9E%AC-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A3%BC-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90/ and https://k-labor.co.kr (정봉수, 전문인력의 점수제 거주비자(F-2-7): D-2·D-10 1년 이상 체류자)
- **fix**: On the D-10-1 -> F-2-7 entry add `needs: {koreanEdu: 4}` (Korean master's or higher). Leave the existing note, which already states the track. Note the listed-company track has no degree rule of its own, so if that case must stay open use `needs: [{koreanEdu: 4}, {edu: 4}]` instead.

## graph / d2

### [high] wrong-abroad: D-2-8 -> D-2-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.88)
- **claim**: The D-2-8 (visiting / short-term student) moves to D-2-2 and D-2-3 are flagged `abroad: true`, but the manual explicitly allows a D-2-8 holder to change to a regular degree course (D-2-1~D-2-6) inside Korea.
- **evidence**: MOJ 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, Ⅴ.3.라 '자격변경의 특례 ❍ (단기유학 체류자)': "단기유학(D-2-8)자격으로 체류 중 정규과정(D-2-1~D-2-6)자격으로 변경하고자 하는 경우 체류자격변경 및 체류기간연장 허가 시 제출서류를 징구하고 변경허용". The same guideline's 체류기간 연장의 특례 1)① confirms the in-Korea route for a 단기유학생 progressing to a degree course: "등록금 납입자(일부 예치자 포함): 유학자격(D-2-1내지 D-2-7) 자격으로 변경".
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅴ.3.라 자격변경의 특례 (단기유학 체류자) and Ⅴ.4.다 체류기간 연장의 특례 1)① - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `D-2-8: next:`, delete `abroad: true` from both the `to: D-2-2` and the `to: D-2-3` entries and reword the note (it also wrongly says "after the exchange ends" - D-2-8 is not an exchange).

### [high] wrong-abroad: D-2-6 -> D-2-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.88)
- **claim**: The D-2-6 (exchange student) moves to D-2-2 and D-2-3 are flagged `abroad: true`, telling exchange students they must leave Korea and get a new visa, but immigration explicitly allows an exchange student who finishes the exchange to change to a regular degree course inside Korea.
- **evidence**: MOJ 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, Ⅴ.3.라 '자격변경의 특례 ❍ (교환학생)': "교환학생(D-2-6)이 교환과정을 종료하고 정규과정으로 변경하고자 하는 경우, 체류자격변경 및 체류기간연장 허가 시 제출하는 서류를 징구하고 변경허용". The 2026 sojourn manual's 유학(D-2) 체류자격 변경허가 › 가. 자격변경 대상 is open to any "외국인등록 후 국내에 체류하고 있는 자" with only C-3-2, C-3-3, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 excluded, so a D-2-6 registrant is an eligible in-Korea applicant. visadocs-targets.txt also shows D-2-2 and D-2-3 both have a 'change' list, i.e. the manual documents an in-Korea change to them.
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅴ.3.라 자격변경의 특례 (교환학생) - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1. 유학(D-2) 체류자격 변경허가 › 가. 자격변경 대상 - https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217 (pp. 27-29)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `D-2-6: next:`, delete `abroad: true` from both the `to: D-2-2` and the `to: D-2-3` entries, and replace the note text with something like "After the exchange ends you can change status in Korea with a standard admission letter and proof of funds."

### [high] wrong-gives: D-2-7 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: D-2-7 has no `gives` at all, even though it is a regular Korean degree course (전문학사 이상). Because the engine applies `gives` of every visa up to and including the one the reader stands on, a D-2-7 reader who truthfully answered that their highest completed education is high school gets every useful option in the list BLOCKED.
- **evidence**: engine.md plus assets/js/visamap.js: `factsAt(level)` uses `path.slice(0, level)` with `level = path.length`, so the current visa's `gives` are applied. With `edu: 1` answered, `koreanEdu` is forced to 0/1, so E-7-1 (needs any of {edu:4}, {edu:3,experience:1}, {experience:5}, {koreanEdu:2}, {edu:3,rank:1}, {...}) and D-10-1 (needs any of {edu:3}, {koreanEdu:2}, {koreanEdu:1,ageUnder:25}) both evaluate to 'no'. Yet D-2-7 is by definition a degree course: 유학생 지침 I. 세부약호 ⑦ "D-2-7(일-학습연계): 정부초청 장학생 등으로 선발되어 국내 대학 전문학사 이상의 정규 학위과정에서 유학하고자 하거나 재학중인 유학생", and the same guideline's 【참고】D-2-7 소지자의 체류우대 is premised on "선발될 당시 해당 학위과정을 정상적으로 졸업한 경우". D-2-1~D-2-4 all carry `gives`; D-2-7 is the only degree code without it.
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅰ 유학 세부약호 ⑦ D-2-7, and 【참고】D-2-7(일-학습연계 비자) 소지자의 체류우대 내용 - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to `D-2-7:` a `gives: {edu: 3, koreanEdu: 3}` (mirroring D-2-2, since the GKS/foreign-government/university STEM scholarship tracks are bachelor's level and above); use `{edu: 2, koreanEdu: 2}` if you want the 전문학사 floor instead. Also add `needs: {edu: 1}` for consistency with the other D-2 codes.

### [medium] wrong-needs: D-2-5 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: D-2-5 (research student) has `needs: [{edu: 4}, {stem: true}]`. Because a list of needs means ANY ONE is enough, answering "STEM: yes" with no degree at all opens D-2-5, but 연구과정 유학 requires a master's or doctorate already held.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-2 유학(D-2) is 정규과정 교육 또는 특정 연구; the manual defines the subcode as 유학생 지침 I ⑤ "D-2-5(연구과정): 대학 및 연구기관에서 석·박사 학위를 소지하고 전문분야의 연구를 수행하면서 실험실습 및 논문작성을 위하여 연구하는 유학생", and the 2026 sojourn manual's 유학(D-2) 제출서류 for 특정 연구과정(D-2-5) requires "⑤ 최종학력 입증서류(석사학위 이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)". There is no STEM-only route and no route without a degree.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 다. 제출서류 › 특정 연구과정(D-2-5) - https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217 (p. 29); 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅰ 세부약호 ⑤ - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `D-2-5: needs:` from the two-item list to a single map: `needs: {edu: 4}` (drop the `- {stem: true}` alternative).

### [medium] wrong-note: D-2-5 -> D-10-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.82)
- **claim**: The note on D-2-5 -> D-10-1 says "60+ points (80+ for citizens of the 21 notified countries, including Uzbekistan)". The 80-point rule does not apply to anyone changing from a 유학(D-2) status; it applies only to 고시국가 nationals who currently hold B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10 or G-1. An Uzbek D-2-5 holder needs 60 points, so the tool over-states the bar for exactly the nationality group it names.
- **evidence**: 체류안내매뉴얼 구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 가. 허용대상 › 1-1) 일반구직(D-10-1): 점수제 적용 대상자: "학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자로 구직 점수표에서 총 190점 중 기본항목이 20점 이상으로 총 득점이 60점 이상인자 - 단, 법무부장관 고시국가 국민 중 B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 자격에 대해서는 총 득점이 80점 이상인 경우에만 체류자격 변경 허용". D-2 is absent from that list.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 가. 허용대상 1-1) (pp. 141-142) - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `D-2-5: next: - to: D-10-1`, replace the note with: "Master's or doctorate holder with 60+ points on the D-10 points table (minimum 20 points on the basic items); the 80-point rule for the 21 notified countries does not apply to changes from a student status. 60-79 points gets 6 months at a time, 80+ gets 1 year." The identical wrong sentence also sits on D-4-1 -> D-10-1 (outside this slice).

### [medium] wrong-note: D-2-1 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: F-2-R's Korean-language threshold is stated as TOPIK 3 / KIIP 3 (in `F-2-R: lang: {topik: 3, kiip: 3}`, in its `how`, and in the D-10-1 -> F-2-R note). Since 2025 the requirement is 사회통합프로그램 4단계 or TOPIK 4급. Every D-2 code offers F-2-R with no note of its own, so this understated threshold is the only language information a student reader sees.
- **evidence**: 법무부 보도자료 「2025년 지역특화형 비자 운영계획 시행」 (2025.2.20.): "한국어 능력 기준은 기존 사회통합프로그램 3단계[또는 한국어능력시험(TOPIK) 3급] 이상에서 4단계[또는 한국어능력시험(TOPIK) 4급] 이상으로 상향". The academic route itself (국내 전문학사 이상 학위취득자/졸업예정자, 지자체 추천, 인구감소지역 거주·취업) is unchanged and is open to D-2 holders, so the edges themselves are right - only the level is wrong.
- **citation**: 법무부 보도자료 「2025년 지역특화형 비자 운영계획 시행」, 2025.2.20. - https://www.mrtc.re.kr/community/05.php?admin_mode=read&no=27679&table_name=trend03_1 (original: https://www.moj.go.kr/bbs/moj/182/477721/download.do)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `F-2-R: lang:` to `{topik: 4, kiip: 4}`, update `F-2-R: how` from "KIIP 3 or TOPIK 3" to "KIIP 4 or TOPIK 4", and update the same phrase in the `D-10-1 -> F-2-R` note. Note this field is shared with other slices.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-2-1 -> D-10-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-10-2 (start-up preparation) is offered from D-2-2 and D-2-3 but not from D-2-1 or D-2-4, although the manual's degree requirement for D-10-2 is "학사(국내 전문학사) 이상", which an associate-degree graduate meets and a doctoral graduate obviously meets.
- **evidence**: 체류안내매뉴얼 구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 가. 허용대상 › 2) 기술창업준비(D-10-2): "학사(국내 전문학사) 이상의 학위를 가진 사람으로서, 아래 어느 하나에 해당하는 자 - 대한민국 특허권·실용신안권·디자인권을 보유하고 있거나 출원 중인 사람 / OASIS 교육과정 참여 중이거나 최근 3년 이내 이수 / K-Startup 그랜드챌린지 참여자 / 해외(OECD) 지식재산권 보유자". The visamap's own `D-10-2: needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` already encodes this, so the gating would admit both groups - only the `next` entry is absent.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 가. 허용대상 2) 기술창업준비(D-10-2) (p. 143) - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- D-10-2` to `D-2-1: next:` and to `D-2-4: next:` (both currently omit it, while D-2-2 and D-2-3 have it).

### [medium] missing-edge: D-2-4 -> D-8-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.78)
- **claim**: D-8-4 (tech start-up founder) is offered from D-2-1, D-2-2 and D-2-3 but not from D-2-4, so a doctoral graduate can never be shown the start-up route even though they are the best-qualified group for it.
- **evidence**: 법무부/출입국·이민정책청 「비자 내비게이터」, 유형3 '유학생이 창업, 무역경영 후 영주 자격 취득', 1단계 (유학·연수 → 기술창업·무역경영): "(D-8, 기술창업) 국내 전문학사 이상 취득, 국외 학사 이상 취득 또는 중앙행정기관의 장이 추천한 사람으로서 점수제 등 요건을 충족하거나, K-startup 그랜드챌린지 참여자로서 요건 충족" - the route is defined from 유학(D-2) with 전문학사 이상, so a 박사과정 student is covered. visamap's `D-8-4: needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` is satisfied by D-2-4's `gives`.
- **citation**: 출입국·외국인정책본부 「비자 내비게이터 - 국내 체류 외국인을 위한 맞춤형 체류 길잡이」, PART 02, 1. 유학·연수(D-2, D-4), 유형3 1단계 - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- D-8-4` to `D-2-4: next:`.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-2-4 -> D-9-5

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: D-9-5 (graduate entrepreneur: Korean master's or higher plus KRW 100M invested) is offered from D-2-3 but not from D-2-4, although the rule is "석사 이상", which includes doctoral graduates.
- **evidence**: 법무부/출입국·이민정책청 「비자 내비게이터」, 유형3 1단계: "(D-9, 유학무역경영) 국내 석사 이상 취득(예정)자로 1억원 이상을 투자하여 국내에서 회사 경영". visamap's `D-9-5: needs: {koreanEdu: 3, invest: 1}` is satisfied by D-2-4's `gives: {edu: 5, koreanEdu: 5}`, so the only thing missing is the `next` entry.
- **citation**: 출입국·외국인정책본부 「비자 내비게이터」, PART 02, 1. 유학·연수(D-2, D-4), 유형3 1단계 (D-9, 유학무역경영) - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- D-9-5` to `D-2-4: next:`.

### [medium] wrong-note: D-2-7 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: D-2-7's name ("Work-study student") and `how` ("Degree program linked with work in Korea, including government scholarship (GKS) students") describe the wrong category. D-2-7 is the full-scholarship student code: GKS recipients, foreign-government full-tuition scholarship holders and university-selected full-tuition STEM scholarship holders. Nothing about it is "linked with work in Korea", and GKS is the core case rather than an aside.
- **evidence**: 법무부 유학생 지침 Ⅰ 세부약호 ⑦: "D-2-7(일-학습연계, 2016.5.16. 신설): 정부초청 장학생 등으로 선발되어 국내 대학 전문학사 이상의 정규 학위과정에서 유학하고자 하거나 재학중인 유학생 - ❶(정부초청장학생) 정부초청 외국인장학생 사업(GKS) 대상자로 선정된 학생 ❷(외국정부선발 장학생) 외국정부에서 등록금 전액 장학금을 지급하는 학생 ❸(대학선발 이공계 우수 장학생) 입학 시 또는 입학 후 교비전액 장학생으로 선발되어 등록금 전액을 장학금으로 지원 받는 학생". The 자격변경 특례 is likewise about scholarship selection, not employment.
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅰ 유학(D-2) 세부약호 ⑦ D-2-7, and Ⅴ.3.나 일-학습연계(D-2-7) 자격변경 특례 - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `D-2-7: name.en` to "Scholarship student" (or "Full-scholarship / GKS student") and `how.en` to "A regular Korean degree course (associate to doctorate) on a full scholarship: GKS government scholarship, a foreign government's full-tuition scholarship, or a university full-tuition STEM scholarship."

### [medium] missing-edge: D-2-7 -> F-2-7

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: D-2-7's `next` has only 7 entries (E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3, D-10-1, F-2-R, F-6-1, F-2-3) even though D-2-7 is a full Korean degree course from 전문학사 to 박사. The scholarship-student routes the manual singles out as *advantaged* - points-based F-2-7, tech start-up D-8-4/D-10-2, research E-3 - are all unreachable from it.
- **evidence**: 법무부 유학생 지침 【참고】D-2-7(일-학습연계 비자) 소지자의 체류우대 내용: "(거주자격 취득) 점수제에 의한 거주자격(F-2-7) 취득 시 가점(10점) 부여" and "(E-7 취업자격 등) 고용계약만 체결하면, 업체의 국민고용 비율적용 면제". The F-2-7 유학인재 track is "국내 정규과정 석사 이상의 학위를 취득한 합법 체류 외국인"으로 학위 취득 5년 내 E-1~E-7-1/D-5~D-9 직종 취업 확정 또는 취업 중 - a GKS master's/doctoral graduate on D-2-7 is exactly that, and the Visa Navigator states "점수제 우수인재의 경우 요건 충족 시 [유학] 자격에서 [거주] 자격으로 바로가기 가능".
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 【참고】D-2-7 소지자의 체류우대 내용 - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 5. 점수제 우수인재 (유학인재) - https://yoonhjs.com/f-type-visa-%EB%8F%99%EA%B1%B0%EA%B1%B0%EC%A3%BC%EB%8F%99%EB%B0%98%EC%98%81%EC%A3%BC%EA%B2%B0%ED%98%BC/?mod=document&uid=449 ; 비자 내비게이터 유형1 2단계 - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml extend `D-2-7: next:` to mirror the degree codes: add `- {to: F-2-7, note: 'Korean master's degree or higher within 5 years and a confirmed job; 80+ points (D-2-7 scholarship graduates get a 10-point bonus).'}`, `- D-8-4`, `- D-10-2` and `- E-3`.

### [medium] wrong-countries: D-2-2 -> E-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: E-2 is offered from D-2-2, D-2-3 and D-2-4 gated only by `needs: {edu: 3}` with no `countries` on E-2, so a Korean bachelor's degree alone is presented as qualifying any nationality. In fact E-2 requires the applicant to be a national of a country where the language taught is the mother tongue - for English, only the 7 designated countries - so Indian, Nigerian, Filipino, Ghanaian, Pakistani etc. D-2 graduates are shown an option they can never get.
- **evidence**: 사증발급/체류 안내매뉴얼 회화지도(E-2) 해당자: "해당 외국어를 모국어로 하는 국가의 국민으로서 해당 외국어를 모국어로 하는 국가에서 대학 이상의 학교를 졸업하고, 학사 이상의 학위를 소지한 자"; "영어 모국어 국가(7개국): 미국, 영국, 캐나다, 남아공, 뉴질랜드, 호주, 아일랜드". The only concession for a Korean degree is the exception "해당 외국어를 모국어로 하는 국가에서 고등학교 또는 전문대학을 졸업하고 국내의 대학에서 학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 경우 자격 인정" - which still presupposes the matching nationality/schooling. For languages other than English/Chinese/Japanese there is a separate route (공인 교원자격 또는 해당 외국어 관련 학과 학사 + 임금 GNI 80% 이상), again tied to the applicant's own language. The visa's `how` text mentions the 7 countries in prose, but the gating never blocks, so the option renders as OPEN for every nationality.
- **citation**: 외국인체류/사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 회화지도(E-2) › 활동범위·해당자 - https://yoonhjs.com/?kboard_content_redirect=292 ; corroborated at https://www.bangduty.com/status/hoehwajido-e-2/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a `groups:` entry (e.g. `e2Native: US,GB,CA,ZA,NZ,AU,IE,CN,TW,HK,JP,FR,DE,ES,RU,VN,...`) and set `countries: "e2Native"` on `E-2:`; or, if you prefer to keep E-2 open, change each D-2 -> E-2 entry to `- {to: E-2, needs: {edu: 3, countries: "e2Native"}, note: 'You may only teach your own mother tongue; English teaching is limited to citizens of the US, UK, Canada, South Africa, New Zealand, Australia and Ireland, and the degree must be from that country unless you finished high school or junior college there.'}` in D-2-2, D-2-3 and D-2-4.

### [low] missing-edge: D-2-2 -> D-2-7

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: No visa anywhere in the graph offers D-2-7 as a next step (its only in-edge is from NONE), yet the manual documents a dedicated in-Korea change from D-2-1~D-2-6 to D-2-7 for students who win a scholarship while already studying. D-2-7 is therefore only reachable as a starting point.
- **evidence**: 법무부 유학생 지침 Ⅴ.3.나 '일-학습연계(D-2-7) 자격변경 특례': "(정부초청장학생) 일반유학생(D-2-1~D-2-6)자격으로 체류 중 정부초청장학생 자격을 취득한 경우 체류자격 변경허가" and "(외국정부 선발 장학생 및 대학선발 이공계 우수 장학생) 일반유학생(D-2-1~D-2-6)자격으로 체류 중 아래의 요건을 모두 충족한 경우 자격변경" (학사 6학기 / 석사 3학기 / 박사·석박사통합 4학기 종료 후, 등록금 전액 장학금, 대학 총장 추천).
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅴ.3.나 일-학습연계(D-2-7) 자격변경 특례 - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to `D-2-1`, `D-2-2`, `D-2-3`, `D-2-4`, `D-2-5` and `D-2-6` a next entry `- {to: D-2-7, note: 'If you are selected for a GKS, foreign-government or university full-tuition scholarship while already studying, you can change to D-2-7 in Korea (bachelor''s: after 6 semesters; master''s: after 3; doctorate: after 4, with the president''s recommendation).'}`

### [low] missing-edge: D-2-6 -> D-2-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: D-2-6 and D-2-8 only offer D-2-2 and D-2-3 as degree-course targets, so an exchange or visiting student heading for an associate degree or a doctorate is given no route at all.
- **evidence**: 법무부 유학생 지침 Ⅴ.3.라: for 단기유학 the permitted target is the whole band "정규과정(D-2-1~D-2-6)", and for 교환학생 it is "정규과정" without restriction; the 체류기간 연장 특례 for a 단기유학생 who progresses says "유학자격(D-2-1내지 D-2-7) 자격으로 변경".
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅴ.3.라 자격변경의 특례 (교환학생) / (단기유학 체류자) and Ⅴ.4.다 1)① - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- D-2-1` and `- D-2-4` to both `D-2-6: next:` and `D-2-8: next:` (as in-Korea changes, with the same reworded note as the D-2-2/D-2-3 entries).

### [low] wrong-gives: D-2-5 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: D-2-5 has no `gives`, although every D-2-5 holder already holds a master's or doctorate by definition. A reader who answered a lower education level and still lands on D-2-5 is then blocked from E-3 (needs edu 4), which is the main route out of it.
- **evidence**: 유학생 지침 Ⅰ 세부약호 ⑤ ("석·박사 학위를 소지하고 ... 연구하는 유학생") and the 2026 sojourn manual's D-2-5 document list ("최종학력 입증서류(석사학위 이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)") establish that the degree is a precondition. Per engine.md a `gives` level acts as a minimum when the reader left the field empty and raises it when they answered lower, which is exactly the right behaviour here. The degree may be foreign, so only `edu` should be set, not `koreanEdu`.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 다. 제출서류 › 특정 연구과정(D-2-5) - https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217 (p. 29)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `gives: {edu: 4}` to `D-2-5:`.

### [low] missing-edge: D-2-2 -> D-4-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: No D-2 code offers D-4-1 (Korean language course). A student who takes leave or withdraws from a degree programme and enrols in a separate language course is explicitly a change-of-status case, so the tool can never suggest the single most common fallback for a student whose degree programme ends.
- **evidence**: 법무부 유학생 지침 Ⅴ.3.라 '자격변경의 특례 ❍ (유학자격 소지자의 한국어 연수)': "유학자격 소지자가 유학의 일환으로 1년 범위 내에서 어학연수(D-4-1, D-4-7)를 받고자 하는 때에는 체류자격 변경 불필요 ※ ... 즉, 휴학 또는 등록금 반환 후 별도의 어학연수과정에 등록하는 경우는 체류자격변경허가 대상임". The same guideline's 어학연수(D-4-1, D-4-7) 자격 › 허가대상 is "외국인등록 후 국내에 체류하고 있는 자" with only D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 excluded, so D-2 holders qualify; visadocs-targets.txt shows D-4-1 has a 'change' list.
- **citation**: 법무부 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅴ.3.다 어학연수(D-4-1, D-4-7) 자격 허가대상 and Ⅴ.3.라 자격변경의 특례 (유학자격 소지자의 한국어 연수) - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: D-4-1, note: 'Only if you leave the degree programme (leave of absence or withdrawal) and enrol in a separate language course; language study inside your own degree programme needs no change of status.'}` to `D-2-1`, `D-2-2`, `D-2-3` and `D-2-4`.

### [low] missing-edge: D-2-2 -> D-2-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: Downward intra-D-2 moves to D-2-1 are missing: D-2-3 and D-2-4 offer D-2-2 but nothing offers D-2-1, and D-2-2 offers no downward move at all, although the repo's own manual-derived 'within' table documents D-2-2>D-2-1, D-2-3>D-2-1 and D-2-3>D-2-2.
- **evidence**: visadocs-targets.txt 'within' section lists `D-2-2>D-2-1: use=report`, `D-2-3>D-2-1: use=report`, `D-2-3>D-2-2: use=report`, `D-2-4>D-2-2: use=report`, `D-2-4>D-2-3: use=report`. The data implements only the last three; the two moves into D-2-1 are absent, so D-2-1 is unreachable from any higher student course.
- **citation**: in-repo ground truth: data/visadocs.yaml `within` (D-2-2>D-2-1, D-2-3>D-2-1), derived from 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 유학(D-2); the underlying mechanism is 등록사항 변경신고 (법 제35조, 시행규칙 제49조의2 - 학교 변경) per https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: D-2-1, note: <same 'moving down' note already used on D-2-3 -> D-2-2>}` to `D-2-2: next:` and to `D-2-3: next:`.

## graph / d4

### [high] wrong-note: D-4-1 -> D-10-1

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/1 (auditor confidence 0.92)
- **claim**: The D-4-* -> D-10-1 note says citizens of the 21 notified countries (incl. Uzbekistan) need 80+ points, but from a D-4 status 60 points is enough for any nationality; the 80-point bar only applies to 고시국가 nationals who hold B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10 or G-1.
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) chapter, 체류자격 변경허가 > 가. 허용대상 > 1-1) 일반구직(D-10-1): 점수제 적용 대상자 (p.141-142): "학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자로 구직 점수표에서 총 190점 중 기본항목이 20점 이상으로 총 득점이 60점 이상인자 - 단, 법무부장관 고시국가 국민 중 B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 자격에 대해서는 총 득점이 80점 이상인 경우에만 체류자격변경 허용". D-4 (일반연수) is not in that enumerated list, so an Uzbek D-4-1/D-4-2/D-4-6/D-4-7 holder with a degree changes at 60 points. The same manual (p.155) confirms the rest of the note: 체류기간 상한 1년 (1회 6개월) at 60-79 points, 3년 (1회 1년) at 80+. I extracted the chapter text from the PDF with PyMuPDF; the 80-point clause is correct on the B/C/D-3/E-9/E-10 edges elsewhere in the file, just not on the D-4 ones.
- **citation**: https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10), pp.141-142 and p.155)

Note the repo's visadocs-targets.txt lists only 'extend' for D-4-2, so the document lists for a D-4-2 step would need filling too.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-4-6 -> F-6-1

- **state**: REFUTED 1/1 (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-4-6 is the only long-term adult status in the graph with no route to F-6-1 (and none to F-2-3), so a private-institute trainee who marries a Korean citizen is told there is no path, while every other D-4 sub-status offers it.

The graph itself reflects this: D-4-1, D-4-2 and D-4-7 all list F-6-1 and F-2-3 (lines 267-268, 283-284, 313+), and the B/C statuses list F-6-1 with abroad: true. D-4-6's next (lines 293-300) simply stops after D-10-1. F-2-3 (spouse/minor child of an F-5 holder) is likewise a documented in-Korea change available from any lawful status.
- **citation**: https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration/47/416137/download.do (법무부, 단기체류자의 결혼이민(F-6) 체류자격 변경 신청 일시 허용 종료 알림 - restriction is on 단기체류자, not on 일반연수); data/visadocs.yaml F-6-1: change,abroad,extend and F-2-3: change,abroad,extend
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml: after line 300 (the D-10-1 entry in D-4-6's next, before `age: [18, 30]`) add `- F-6-1` and `- F-2-3`.
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The load-bearing premise is false. I recomputed which visas with a non-empty `next` omit F-6-1: D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, H-2, D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4, D-9-5, plus most F-2-* nodes. The identical list omits F-2-3. So D-4-6 is not 'the only long-term adult status in the graph with no route to F-6-1' - every D-8 investor/founder status, every D-9 trade status and the other three D-10 sub-codes are in the same position, and 'every other D-4 sub-status offers it' is also wrong, since D-4-3 does not. This is a file-wide pattern, not a D-4-6-specific oversight, which removes both the evidence and the medium-severity rationale. The cited source does not help either: the immigration.go.kr notice says only that the temporary allowance for 단기체류자 has ended; it affirmatively says nothing about 일반연수(D-4) being permitted, so the claim rests on absence of prohibition. Engine-wise the gap is also much smaller than implied: F-6-1's node `needs` is `{spouse: korean}` and F-2-3's is `[{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}]`, so these steps are only ever surfaced to a reader who answered that way, and per engine.md an unanswered fact never blocks.
  - corrected claim: data/visamap.yaml is internally inconsistent about where the marriage/F-5-family routes are drawn: D-4-1, D-4-2 and D-4-7 list F-6-1 and F-2-3 while D-4-6, D-4-3, D-3, E-8, all four D-8 sub-codes, all three D-9 sub-codes and D-10-2/D-10-3/D-10-T do not. D-4-6 is one of about fifteen such nodes, not a unique omission, and no source I checked speaks to D-4 specifically.
  - corrected fix: Do not treat this as a two-line D-4-6 patch. Run one consistency pass that decides a single rule for which long-term statuses carry F-6-1 and F-2-3, then apply it to the whole set (D-4-6, D-3, E-8, D-8-*, D-9-*, D-10-2/3/T) in one change; adding the two entries to D-4-6 alone is not wrong but leaves the same inconsistency on a dozen other nodes while implying D-8 and D-9 holders have no marriage route.

### [medium] wrong-gives: D-4-3 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: D-4-3 has no 'gives', so the engine never credits a reader who completed Korean K-12 with a high-school education; readers who answered "no education / below high school" are then blocked from D-2-1, D-2-2 (needs edu:1) and from the koreanEdu-based D-10-1 and E-7-Y routes that exist only for Korean school graduates.
- **evidence**: engine.md: "gives: on visa X = facts that holding X implies for LATER steps ... a gives must represent what a person has after COMPLETING that visa" and "needs only ever filters readers who explicitly answered". D-2-1/D-2-2 carry needs {edu: 1} (lines 276, 300 of their blocks), and D-10-1's third needs branch is {koreanEdu: 1, ageUnder: 25}, E-7-Y's is {koreanEdu: 1} - exactly the 국내 초·중·고 졸업 fact that completing D-4-3 establishes (구직(D-10) 매뉴얼 p.142, 국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년). Compare D-2-1/D-2-2, which do declare gives {edu: n, koreanEdu: n}.
- **citation**: https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do p.142 (국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년: 국내 초·중·고교를 모두 졸업); engine.md (gives semantics)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml: add `gives: {edu: 1, koreanEdu: 1}` to D-4-3 (after `age: [0, 19]`, line 289). If crediting high school to a reader who is still in elementary/middle school is a concern, use `gives: {koreanEdu: 1}` and put `needs: {edu: 1}` on the individual D-2-1/D-2-2 moves instead.
