All three have a documented in-Korea change list in the repo ground truth (D-8-1: change,abroad,extend; D-8-2: change,abroad,extend; D-8-3: change,abroad,extend) and the manual's change-list cases carry no from-status restriction ('Investor in a Korean corporation', 'Venture-business founder', 'Investor in a company run by a Korean national'). A D-10 or D-2 holder who sets up a Korean corporation and brings in KRW 100M+ changes to D-8-1 in Korea - a very common route.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1. 법인에 투자(D-8-1) / 2. 벤처 투자(D-8-2) / 3. 개인기업 투자(D-8-3) (data/visadocs.yaml) ; https://immikorea.com/d-8-1-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EA%B8%B0%EC%97%85%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EC%B7%A8%EB%93%9D%EC%9D%84-%EC%9C%84%ED%95%9C-%EC%9A%94%EA%B1%B4-%EC%A0%88%EC%B0%A8-%EC%84%9C%EB%A5%98-%EA%B8%B0/
- **fix**: Add `- D-8-1` (and `- D-8-3` where a Korean-owned sole proprietorship is the target) to the `next` lists of D-10-1, D-2-3, D-2-4, E-7-1, D-9-1 and D-9-4 in data/visamap.yaml; add `- D-8-2` to D-10-1 and D-10-2 (venture/pre-venture founders usually come off the job-seeker or OASIS track).
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The substantive gap is real but the headline is overstated and part of the proposed fix is contradicted by the decree. Verified true: D-8-2 and D-8-3 have exactly one in-edge each, NONE (i.e. from-abroad only), so the tool can never offer them to anyone already in Korea; and all three of D-8-1/2/3 do have documented in-Korea change lists with no prior-status condition - data/visadocs.yaml targets.D-8-1.change (source 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1. 법인에 투자(D-8-1)), .D-8-2.change (› 2. 벤처 투자(D-8-2)), .D-8-3.change (› 3. 개인기업 투자(D-8-3)), each a pure document list keyed on the investment type, and visadocs-targets.txt gives all three "change,abroad,extend". Verified false: the claim "D-8-1, D-8-2 and D-8-3 have no in-Korea in-edge at all" contradicts its own parenthetical - D-7 -> D-8-1 at data/visamap.yaml line 1094 is a bare code, hence an in-Korea change - and "the only D-8 it can ever recommend to a resident is D-8-4" is therefore wrong. The D-10-2 -> D-8-2 half of the fix is refuted by 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 13.구직(D-10) 나목, "기업투자(D-8) 다목에 해당하는 법인의 창업 준비 등을 하려는 사람": D-10-2 (기술창업준비) exists to feed D-8 다목, which is D-8-4 (기술창업), not 나목/D-8-2 (벤처). visamap already has D-10-2 -> D-8-4, which is the correct edge. Likewise the D-2 -> D-8-1 half is not supported by the Visa Navigator's curated student route: PART 02 유형3, p.12, sends 유학 D-2/D-4 only to 기술창업(D-8-4) and 무역경영(D-9, incl. 유학무역경영 D-9-5), both of which visamap already has.
  - corrected claim: D-8-2 and D-8-3 are reachable only from NONE, and D-8-1 has a single in-Korea in-edge (from D-7 alone), so the ordinary corporate and sole-proprietor investor routes are effectively invisible to residents who are not already on D-7. The manual documents an in-Korea 체류자격 변경허가 for all three sub-codes with no prior-status restriction, so this is an under-connected graph rather than a deliberate rule. The claims that D-8-1 has no in-Korea in-edge, and that D-8-4 is the only D-8 ever offered to a resident, are incorrect.
  - corrected fix: Add `- D-8-1` to the `next` lists of D-10-1, E-7-1, D-9-1 and D-9-4 (a D-9 trader who makes a FIPA-registered investment must move to D-8: 별표 1의2 12.무역경영(D-9) expressly excludes "기업투자(D-8) 체류자격에 해당하는 사람"), and `- D-8-3` to the same four where the target is a Korean-run individual enterprise. D-8-1 already carries `needs: {invest: 1}` (₩100M), which gates it correctly. Do NOT add D-10-2 -> D-8-2 (별표 1의2 13.나목 ties 기술창업준비 to D-8 다목 = D-8-4, already present). Drop D-2-3/D-2-4 -> D-8-1 unless a source is found; the Navigator's student route (p.12 유형3) goes to D-8-4 and D-9 only, both already in the file. D-8-2 is best left fed from NONE plus, at most, D-10-1, since 벤처기업 확인 under 벤처기업육성법 제25조 presupposes an already-established company.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 11.기업투자(D-8) 가/나/다목, 12.무역경영(D-9) and 13.구직(D-10) 가/나목, verbatim at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/byl12.txt lines 51-76; KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › 1.유학 › 유형3 p.12, at scratchpad/navigator.txt lines 279-300; /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visadocs.yaml targets.D-8-1/D-8-2/D-8-3 .change; data/visamap.yaml line 1094 (D-7 -> D-8-1)
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The headline claim is false for D-8-1 and the finding contradicts itself. D-7 -> D-8-1 is a plain (no `abroad` flag) entry at data/visamap.yaml:1094, and D-7 itself has no visa-level `abroad: true`, so per engine.md that IS an in-Korea change of status. The finding's own parenthetical ('D-8-1 only from D-7') concedes this while the headline says 'no in-Korea in-edge at all', and the conclusion 'the only D-8 it can ever recommend to a resident is D-8-4' is simply wrong - a reader standing on D-7 is offered D-8-1 in Korea. Part of the proposed fix is also unsupported: adding `- D-8-1` to D-9-1 and D-9-4 is not something the finding sourced (its citation is the D-8 change-of-status document list, which proves a change route exists, not that D-9 is a sensible origin), and the map deliberately routes the student/job-seeker founder population (D-2-1/2/3, D-10-1/2/3, E-7-1) to D-8-4, which is the OASIS tech-startup route the KIS Visa Navigator presents separately as 유형3 for exactly those people. What does survive: D-8-2 and D-8-3 genuinely have NONE as their only in-edge, so they are unreachable in Korea, and I verified both have documented in-Korea change lists with no prior-status condition (visadocs.yaml D-8-2.change sourced to '16. 기업투자(D-8) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 2. 벤처 투자(D-8-2)', case 'Venture-business founder'; D-8-3.change › '3. 개인기업 투자(D-8-3)', case 'Investor in a company run by a Korean national'). So the finding is right in a much narrower form than stated, and at lower severity.
  - corrected claim: D-8-2 and D-8-3 have no in-Korea in-edge (only NONE, which the engine treats as from abroad), so the tool can never offer the venture-founder or Korean-sole-proprietorship investor route to someone already in Korea, even though the manual documents an in-Korea change to both with no prior-status condition. D-8-1 is not affected: it is reachable in Korea from D-7.
  - corrected fix: Add `- D-8-2` to D-10-1 and D-10-2 (the startup-prep origins), and `- D-8-3` to D-10-1 and the D-2 graduate codes. Leave D-8-1's in-edges alone unless a source for a specific origin is produced; do not add D-8-1/D-8-3 to D-9-1 or D-9-4 on the strength of a document list alone.
  - citation: /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visamap.yaml:1083-1096 (D-7 next contains plain `- D-8-1`, D-7 has no abroad flag); data/visadocs.yaml:6253-6256 and 6316-6320 (D-8-2 / D-8-3 change sections); /private/tmp/.../scratchpad/engine.md ('No flag = change of status inside Korea'); /private/tmp/.../scratchpad/navi.txt:420-430 (Navigator 유형3 기술창업 D-8 route)

### [medium] wrong-note: D-8-4 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: D-8-4's `how` text states '60+ start-up points'; the 기술창업 점수제 pass mark is 80 points (out of 368, with at least one mandatory item satisfied), not 60.
- **evidence**: The 기술창업(D-8-4) points system requires '총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점(필수항목은 반드시 1개 이상 충족)'. An English restatement of the same table gives 80 points minimum out of 448 (313 essential + 135 optional) after the latest revision. 60 is the pass mark of a different scheme - the D-10-1 일반구직 points table (60 of 190) - so the two have been conflated. A reader who scores 65 start-up points is told they qualify when they do not.
- **citation**: 정봉수, 「점수제 기술창업비자(D-8-4)」, 한국유학신문: https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=306 ; https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/d8-visa-d84-technology-start-up-points-based-entrepreneur-visa-eligibility-required-documents-s ; 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 4. 기술창업
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, D-8-4 `how`: change '60+ start-up points' to '80+ start-up points' (en), and the matching ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings ('60+ баллов' -> '80+ баллов', '60+ ball' -> '80+ ball', '60+ балл' -> '80+ балл').
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed. The cited 한국유학신문 article yields the verbatim KIS-manual sentence '총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점(필수항목은 반드시 1개 이상 충족)', and the independent English restatement (allvisakorea) gives '80 points or more out of a total of 448 points' after the revision. Both agree the pass mark is 80; only the denominator changed between revisions, which the finding itself flags. The finding's secondary claim that 60 belongs to a different scheme is also confirmed from a primary document already in the scratchpad: the KIS 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 D-10 chapter (/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/d10.txt, lines 68-70) reads '구직 점수표에서 총 190점 중 기본항목이 20점 이상으로 총 득점이 60점 이상인 자' for 일반구직(D-10-1). A third threshold exists and is not 60 either: the same manual (d10.txt line ~672) sets 기술창업준비(D-10-2) at '창업이민 점수제 평가점수가 35점 이상', with the note that the scoring table lives in the 기업투자(D-8) 지침. So no D-8/D-10 points scheme uses 60 except 일반구직. '60+ start-up points' in D-8-4's `how` is wrong and understates the bar by 20 points. Proposed fix is exactly right.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: in /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visamap.yaml, D-8-4 `how`, '60+ start-up points' -> '80+ start-up points', and ru '60+ баллов' -> '80+ баллов', uz '60+ ball' -> '80+ ball', uz-cyrl '60+ балл' -> '80+ балл'. Do not touch the 80+ points figure in the F-2-7 note on the same visa, which is a different (F-2-7 점수제) table that genuinely is 80.
  - citation: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 기업투자(D-8) › 기술창업, quoted verbatim at https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=306 ('총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점(필수항목은 반드시 1개 이상 충족)'); https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/d8-visa-d84-technology-start-up-points-based-entrepreneur-visa-eligibility-required-documents-s ('80 points or more out of a total of 448 points'); KIS 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 D-10 chapter, scratchpad/d10.txt lines 68-70 (60 of 190 = D-10-1) and line ~672 (35 = D-10-2)
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - Could not refute. Both sources I fetched myself give 80 as the pass mark, and nothing anywhere gives 60 for D-8-4. 정봉수's column quotes the manual verbatim: '기술창업 점수제에 따라 총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점(필수항목은 반드시 1개 이상 충족)'. The English restatement gives 80 of 448 (313 essential + 135 optional) after the revision. The two sources disagree on the TOTAL (368 vs 448, i.e. edition drift) but agree on the 80 pass mark, and the `how` string quotes no total, so only the number has to change. The conflation story is also credible: 60 is the D-10-1 일반구직 pass mark (60 of 190), a different table. Engine-wise this is purely display text (`how` is never gated on; D-8-4's actual gate is needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]), so the error misinforms but does not misroute - 'medium' is about right for a wrong number in prose. Fix is correct as written, including the three translations.
  - citation: https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=306 (fetched: '총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점(필수항목은 반드시 1개 이상 충족)'); https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/d8-visa-d84-technology-start-up-points-based-entrepreneur-visa-eligibility-required-documents-s (fetched: '80 points or more out of a total of 448'); in-repo /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visadocs.yaml:6448 D-8-4 'points-system route' variant confirms the 점수제 route exists but carries no score

### [medium] missing-edge: D-8-4 -> F-5-5

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.78)
- **claim**: D-8-4 does not offer F-5-5 (large investor PR) although D-8-1, D-8-2 and D-8-3 all do, and the F-5-5 requirement contains no D-8 sub-code restriction.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 (고액투자자) and the KIS Visa Navigator both state the requirement as '미화 50만 달러 이상을 투자한 외국인투자가로서 5명 이상의 국민 고용' - the test is the investment amount plus five Korean employees, with no condition on which D-8 sub-code the applicant holds. The Visa Navigator's 유형2 diagram is 투자(D-8) -> 거주(F-2) -> 영주(F-5) for the whole D-8 status. The tool therefore hides the route from exactly the founders most likely to raise USD 500K+.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제3호 (고액투자자); KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › 2. 주재·투자·경영 (D-7~D-9) › 유형2, p.16: https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do
- **fix**: Add `- F-5-5` to the `next` list of D-8-4 in data/visamap.yaml.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The rule as stated checks out against both cited primary sources, though the finding's motivating rationale is wrong. 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제3호 reads "「외국인투자촉진법」에 따라 미화 50만 달러를 투자한 외국인투자가로서 5명 이상의 국민을 고용하고 있는 사람" - investment amount plus five Korean employees, with no condition on the applicant's current status at all, not even that it be D-8. The KIS Visa Navigator p.16 유형2 2단계 states it as "(F-5, 고액 투자자) 미화 50만 달러 이상을 투자한 외국인투자가로서 5명 이상의 국민 고용" and its 유형2 flow diagram is indeed drawn as 투자 D-8 -> 거주 F-2 -> 영주 F-5 against the undifferentiated "D-8" label, exactly as the finding says. visamap's own F-5-5 entry ("no minimum stay", needs {invest: 3}) and visadocs-targets.txt "F-5-5: change,abroad" agree there is no prior-status gate. And the file is already internally inconsistent: F-5-5 is offered from D-8-2 (venture founder), who is no more a FIPA 외국인투자가 than a D-8-4 holder is, so excluding only D-8-4 is arbitrary.
  - corrected claim: The rule claim is correct, but the finding's reasoning ("hides the route from exactly the founders most likely to raise USD 500K+") conflates raising investment with making one. F-5-5 requires the applicant personally to be the 외국인투자가 who invested USD 500K under the Foreign Investment Promotion Act; a D-8-4 founder who raises capital from investors is covered instead by 별표 1의3 제15호 ("기업투자(D-8) 다목 ... 3억원 이상의 투자금을 유치하고 2명 이상의 국민을 고용"), which is F-5-24 and is already D-8-4's first `next` entry. The correct justification for the edge is simply that 제3호 imposes no status or sub-code condition. Also, the decree text says "미화 50만 달러를 투자한"; the "이상" in the finding's quote comes from the Navigator's restatement, not from the decree.
  - corrected fix: Add `- F-5-5` to the `next` list of D-8-4 in data/visamap.yaml. Its existing `needs: {invest: 3}` (₩700M ~ USD 500K) gates it, so a points-route D-8-4 founder who answered a low invest level is correctly blocked. For symmetry also consider D-9-4 (₩300M+ imported capital), whose holders can equally satisfy 제3호; do not change F-5-24, which is the right 제15호 route for D-8-4 and is already present.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제3호 and 제15호, verbatim at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/byl13.txt (PAGE 1 item 3; PAGE 2-3 item 15); KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › 2.주재·투자·경영 › 유형2 2단계 and 유형3, p.16, at scratchpad/navigator.txt lines 405-432 (source PDF https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do)
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The decree text checks out but the inference does not, and the finding's own Navigator citation argues against it. 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제3호 reads '「외국인투자 촉진법」에 따라 미화 50만 달러를 투자한 외국인투자가로서 5명 이상의 국민을 고용하고 있는 사람' (I read the decree table myself; note it says 50만 달러, not 50만 달러 '이상' as quoted). The operative requirement is that the APPLICANT personally be a FIPA 외국인투자가 who invested the money. The finding's rationale - 'hides the route from exactly the founders most likely to raise USD 500K+' - conflates raising investment with being an investor. A D-8-4 holder is by construction on the non-capital route: 별표 1의2 기업투자(D-8) 다목 is the 지식재산권/기술창업 법인 창업자 clause, whereas FIPA-based investment is 가목 (the D-8-1/D-8-3 basis). A founder who personally puts USD 500K of foreign capital into their own corporation falls under 가목, i.e. D-8-1, which already has F-5-5. The founder who RAISES ₩300M+ and employs 2+ Koreans has a dedicated, strictly easier PR route, F-5-24, which the map already gives D-8-4 - and which is precisely the Navigator page the finding cites: p.16's 유형3 '기술창업자의 영주 자격 취득' routes 기술창업(D-8) straight to '(F-5, 기술창업 투자자)', while 유형2 (the one quoted) is the 고액투자자 track. So the claim that 유형2 covers 'the whole D-8 status' misreads a document that explicitly splits 기술창업 out. Nothing I found says a D-8-4 holder is barred from F-5-5, but the omission is a defensible curation choice rather than a data error, and the real inconsistency runs the other way: D-8-2 (벤처, 나목) is also not a FIPA-investment route yet the map already offers it F-5-5.
  - corrected claim: F-5-5's requirement names no D-8 sub-code, so a D-8-4 holder who personally made a FIPA investment of USD 500K and employs 5+ Koreans is not formally excluded. But that person falls under D-8 가목 (D-8-1), and the D-8-4 population the finding describes - founders who raise capital - is served by F-5-24, which the map already offers. At most this is a low-severity consistency nit, not a hidden route.
  - corrected fix: No change required. If consistency across the D-8 sub-codes is wanted, the question to settle is whether D-8-2 should keep F-5-5 (it is equally non-FIPA), not whether D-8-4 should gain it.

### [medium] wrong-needs: D-8-2, D-8-4 -> F-2-99

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: The D-8-2 -> F-2-99 and D-8-4 -> F-2-99 moves carry no investment condition, but the F-2-99 rule admits D-8 holders only if the investment still held on the application date is at least the Foreign Investment Promotion Act threshold of KRW 100M.

D-8-2 (venture founder) and D-8-4 (tech start-up) have no visa-level `needs`, and F-2-99 has none either, so per engine.md the move is unconditionally OPEN for them - the tool offers 5-year long-term residence to venture and start-up founders whose paid-in foreign investment is below KRW 100M, who are expressly excluded.
- **citation**: KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › ❹ 장기체류자(F-2-99) › 자격 요건, p.35: https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 9. 기타 장기체류자에 대한 거주 자격 변경허가
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, change the bare `- F-2-99` entry in D-8-2's and D-8-4's `next` lists to `- {to: F-2-99, needs: {invest: 1}}`.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed verbatim against the primary source, which is already in the scratchpad. The KIS 비자 내비게이터, PART 02, ❹ 장기체류자(F-2-99), 자격 요건, p.35 (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 900-903) reads: '신청 대상 체류자격 : 문화예술(D-1), 취재(D-5), 종교(D-6), 주재(D-7), 기업투자(D-8)*, 무역경영(D-9), 교수(E-1), ...' with the footnote '* 기업투자(D-8) 자격자는 신청 당시 투자금액이 현행 「외국인투자촉진법」 상의 투자기준금액(1억원) 이상인 자에 한함'. The page number cited (p.35) matches the page marker in the extracted text. The footnote covers 기업투자(D-8) as a whole, so it reaches D-8-2 and D-8-4. Neither of those two statuses carries any investment requirement in its own right - 별표 1의2 기업투자(D-8) 나목 (venture company founder) and 다목 (IP-holding corporation founder) are defined by 지식재산권/우수한 기술력 and a degree, with no capital threshold (scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 69-80) - so the gap is real and not hypothetical. I verified the data: D-8-2 and D-8-4 have no visa-level `needs` touching investment (D-8-4's is `[{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]`), and F-2-99 itself has `needs: None`, so per engine.md the move is unconditionally OPEN. D-8-1 and D-8-3 escape the same defect only incidentally, because their own entry gate `needs: {invest: 1}` already filters the reader upstream - which is why the finding is right to name exactly D-8-2 and D-8-4.

Leave D-8-1 and D-8-3 alone - their visa-level `needs: {invest: 1}` already enforces the same thing upstream - and do not put the condition on F-2-99 itself, since the footnote restricts only D-8 holders and a visa-level `needs` there would wrongly block the D-1/D-5/D-6/D-7/D-9/E-1~E-7/F-1 applicants the same list admits.

I downloaded the KIS 비자 내비게이터 PDF from the cited immigration.go.kr URL and extracted p.35, which reads exactly: '신청 대상 체류자격 : 문화예술(D-1), 취재(D-5), 종교(D-6), 주재(D-7), 기업투자(D-8)*, 무역경영(D-9), ...' with '* 기업투자(D-8) 자격자는 신청 당시 투자금액이 현행 「외국인투자촉진법」 상의 투자기준금액(1억원) 이상인 자에 한함'. Case ① says 'D-8 체류자격' without a sub-code; case ③ names no status at all. The repo's own F-2-5 change list reproduces the same three cases verbatim.

Case 1) says 기업투자(D-8) with no sub-code, and case 3) names no status. The Navigator p.16 유형2 1단계 restates it word for word as the finding quotes, and data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-2-5.change (source 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 4. 고액투자자에 대한 거주자격 변경허가) carries exactly those three cases. It rests on 'case ① says D-8 체류자격 without a sub-code; case ③ names no status at all', but in 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 라목 the three numbered cases sit under an umbrella clause: '라. 「외국인투자 촉진법」에 따른 외국투자가 등으로 다음의 어느 하나에 해당하는 사람', followed by 1) USD 500K + 3 years on 기업투자(D-8), 2) dispatched officer of a USD 500K foreign corporate investor, 3) USD 300K + 2 Korean employees. FIPA-registered investment is the 기업투자(D-8) 가목 basis, i.e. D-8-1 and D-8-3, which are exactly the two the map already routes to F-2-5. D-8-2 is 나목 (지식재산권 + 벤처기업 확인) and D-8-4 is 다목 (기술창업 법인 창업자); neither is predicated on investment capital, and D-8-4's own F-2 route in the map is F-2-7 (points), with its PR route F-5-24, matching the Navigator's separate 유형3 for 기술창업. ### [medium] wrong-needs: D-8-1 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-8-1 carries `needs: {invest: 1}` for the whole visa, which blocks the essential-professional-staff half of D-8-1 - people dispatched to a foreign-invested Korean company who invest nothing personally - and with it makes F-5-26 unreachable for the only group that can use it.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 기업투자(D-8) 가목 covers '「외국인투자 촉진법」에 따른 외국인투자기업의 경영·관리 또는 생산·기술 분야에 종사하려는 필수전문인력' - i.e. the KRW 100M/10% test applies to the investor, not to the dispatched executive or R&D specialist. The visa's own `how` says so: '... or essential staff of such a company'. F-5-26 ('R&D staff at a foreign-invested firm', requirement: D-8 가목 for 3+ years) is offered only from D-8-1, so a reader who answers that they have made no investment is blocked out of D-8-1 and can never be shown F-5-26 at all. Per engine.md a visa-level `needs` gates every move into that visa, and an answered fact does block.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 기업투자(D-8) 가목 ; 주 고베 대한민국 총영사관, 【D-8-1】 국내기업 투자: https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/jp-kobe-ko/brd/m_44/view.do?seq=1025258
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, replace D-8-1's `needs: {invest: 1}` with an alternatives list that also admits dispatched staff, e.g. `needs: [{invest: 1}, {edu: 3}]`, and keep the KRW 100M condition as a per-move `needs: {invest: 1}` on the investor entries (NONE -> D-8-1, D-10-1 -> D-8-1 etc.) rather than on the visa.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Directionally confirmed on the primary source, with one overstatement to trim. 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제11호 기업투자(D-8) 가목 reads, verbatim (scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 64-68): '「외국인투자 촉진법」에 따른 외국인투자기업의 경영ㆍ관리 또는 생산ㆍ기술 분야에 종사하려는 필수전문인력으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람[외국인이 경영하는 기업(법인은 제외한다)에 투자한 사람 및 국내에서 채용된 사람은 제외한다]'. 가목 is defined entirely in terms of 필수전문인력 of a foreign-invested enterprise; the KRW 100M/10% FIPA test is what makes the *company* an 외국인투자기업, not a test on the individual applicant, so a dispatched executive or production/technology specialist who personally invests nothing is squarely within 가목. The decree corroborates this a second time: 별표 1의3 제17호 (= F-5-26, scratchpad/byl_03.txt lines 75-80) grants 영주 to someone holding '기업투자(D-8) 가목' who serves as '연구개발시설의 필수전문인력' for 3+ years - a staff route, not an investor route. I also confirmed the mechanism is live, not theoretical: layouts/visa-map.html lines 264-268 build the investment select over `seq 0 6`, and i18n/en.yaml line 197 defines vm_invest_0 as 'None', so a reader can explicitly answer zero investment, which per engine.md is an answered fact and does block. Trim: 'the only group that can use it' is too strong (an investor who is himself the R&D 필수전문인력 is not excluded, and a reader who leaves the field blank is UNKNOWN and not blocked), and the proposed `{edu: 3}` alternative is not grounded in any rule - the decree imposes no degree requirement on 필수전문인력.
  - corrected claim: D-8-1's visa-level `needs: {invest: 1}` wrongly blocks the 필수전문인력 half of 별표 1의2 기업투자(D-8) 가목 - dispatched management/production/technology staff of a foreign-invested company, who make no personal investment - for any reader who explicitly answers 'None' to the investment question. Because F-5-26 is offered only from D-8-1, those readers can never see F-5-26, which is itself a staff-only route (별표 1의3 제17호: 가목 + 연구개발시설의 필수전문인력). Readers who leave the investment field blank are unaffected, and an investor who is also the R&D specialist is not excluded, so the group shut out is 'readers who answered no investment', not literally everyone eligible.
  - corrected fix: Drop D-8-1's visa-level `needs: {invest: 1}` entirely and move it onto the investor-shaped moves into D-8-1 (NONE -> D-8-1, D-10-1 -> D-8-1, and the other entries listed at data/visamap.yaml lines 328/358/392/521/549/567/778/1094), as `needs: {invest: 1}`. Do not add `{edu: 3}` as a D-8-1 alternative: no rule requires a degree for 필수전문인력, so it would introduce a fresh false block. The degree requirement belongs on F-5-26 instead (see finding 8). With no visa-level needs, a reader who answers 'None' is correctly shown D-8-1 as the staff route and can reach F-5-26.

- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - Could not refute the substance. ### [low] wrong-needs: D-8-1 -> F-5-26

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: F-5-26 has no `needs` at all, so the R&D-staff PR route is offered to readers with no degree, although a bachelor's degree or higher is a hard requirement.

The visa's own `how` in visamap.yaml already states "bachelor's degree", but there is no `needs` block, so a reader who answered edu: 1 (high school) is shown F-5-26 as an OPEN option.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 32. 영주(F-5) › 17. 외국인투자기업의 연구개발 인력〔F-5-26〕 ; https://yoonhjs.com/f-type-visa-%EB%8F%99%EA%B1%B0%EA%B1%B0%EC%A3%BC%EB%8F%99%EB%B0%98%EC%98%81%EC%A3%BC%EA%B2%B0%ED%98%BC/?mod=document&uid=406
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, add `needs: {edu: 3}` to the F-5-26 visa block.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed. The degree requirement is not in the decree - 별표 1의3 제17호 (scratchpad/byl_03.txt lines 75-80) says only '기업투자(D-8) 가목 체류자격을 가지고 「외국인투자촉진법 시행령」 제25조제1항제4호에 따른 연구개발시설의 필수전문인력으로 대한민국에 3년 이상 계속 체류하고 있는 사람' - so I checked the guideline level the finding actually cites, and the cited page confirms the operative text '국내․외 학사학위 이상 소지할 것' as a listed F-5-26 requirement, alongside 3+ years continuous D-8 가목 stay, 3+ years at the designated R&D facility, and the means test. The data defect is as described: F-5-26's block in data/visamap.yaml (line 1455) has only `name` and `how` keys, no `needs`, so a reader who answered edu: 1 and reached D-8-1 is shown it as OPEN. The finding is correctly scoped and correctly rated low severity. One incidental inaccuracy in the finding's own evidence, which does not affect the verdict: the means test is not income-only - the guideline allows 자산 : 전년도 평균 순자산 1.5배 이상 as an alternative to 소득 : 전년도 1인당 GNI 2배 이상, so visamap's `how` ('income of 2× GNI') is itself slightly incomplete.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: add `needs: {edu: 3}` to the F-5-26 block in data/visamap.yaml (line 1455). Worth doing in the same pass: F-5-26's `how` currently gives only 'income of 2× GNI', but the guideline accepts 자산 전년도 평균 순자산 1.5배 이상 as an alternative, so the English/ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings would be more accurate as 'income of 2× GNI (or assets of 1.5× the average)'.

### [low] missing-edge: D-8-2, D-8-4 -> F-5-25

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-5-25 (conditional large investor, KRW 3bn public-business fund deposit) is offered from D-8-1 and D-8-3 but not from D-8-2 or D-8-4, even though the route depends only on the deposit and not on the status held.
- **evidence**: F-5-25 조건부 고액투자자 is granted to anyone (plus spouse and unmarried children) who deposits KRW 3bn or more in the Korea Development Bank 공익사업 투자이민 fund and pledges to keep it 5 years; income and KIIP requirements are waived. No current status is part of the test, and visamap already offers it from B-1, B-2 and the C-3 statuses, which makes the gap at D-8-2/D-8-4 an inconsistency rather than a rule.
- **citation**: 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부, 공익사업 투자이민제: https://www.immigration.go.kr/immigration/1523/subview.do ; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 (조건부 고액투자자)
- **fix**: Add `- F-5-25` to the `next` lists of D-8-2 and D-8-4 in data/visamap.yaml.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The facts check out, but the 'inconsistency' framing is only half-supported, so this is weaker than stated. Confirmed: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제16호 (scratchpad/byl_03.txt lines 72-74) reads '5년 이상 투자 상태를 유지할 것을 조건으로 법무부장관이 정하여 고시하는 금액 이상을 투자한 사람과 그 배우자 및 자녀로서 법무부장관이 정하는 요건을 갖춘 사람' - no 체류자격 appears in the test, which supports the finding's central point. The amount is also right: the 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부 공익사업 투자이민제 page gives 30억 원 이상 for the 고액투자 tier granting 영주(F-5) directly (cancelled if the funds are withdrawn inside 5 years), against 15억 원 이상 for the 일반 tier leading to 거주(F-2) then F-5 - matching visamap's '₩3bn' and `needs: {invest: 6}`. Where the finding overreaches: the data's handling of this programme is not an otherwise-consistent pattern with a hole at D-8-2/D-8-4. F-5-25 is listed from exactly B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9, D-8-1 and D-8-3, and the sibling routes F-2-12 (the same 공익사업 투자이민 programme, 15억 tier, `needs: {invest: 5}`) and F-2-8 are listed from only B-1, B-2 and the three C-3 statuses - not from D-8-1 or D-8-3 either. So the file deliberately routes investment immigration through entry statuses and has added F-5-25 only to the two D-8 sub-codes that already require invest: 1. The D-8-2/D-8-4 omission is a plausible authorial choice (those two need no capital), and the slice the fix addresses is arbitrary: the same route is equally absent from ~107 other statuses. The fix is harmless because F-5-25's own `needs: {invest: 6}` still gates it, but it does not resolve the underlying patchiness.
  - corrected claim: 별표 1의3 제16호 (조건부 고액투자자, F-5-25) imposes no requirement as to the status currently held, and the ₩3bn / 5-year-maintenance figures in visamap are correct, so nothing in the rules prevents offering F-5-25 from D-8-2 and D-8-4. But this is not a hole in an otherwise consistent pattern: the sibling routes F-2-12 and F-2-8 are offered only from B-1, B-2 and C-3-1/4/9 and are absent from D-8-1 and D-8-3 as well, so the file models investment immigration as an entry-status route generally, and F-5-25's presence on D-8-1/D-8-3 (which already require invest: 1) is the exception rather than the rule. Treat this as optional completeness, not a defect.
  - corrected fix: If applied at all, do not stop at `- F-5-25` on D-8-2 and D-8-4: decide the policy once and apply it to the whole 공익사업 투자이민 family, i.e. either (a) add both `- F-2-12` and `- F-5-25` to all four D-8 sub-codes, since 별표 1의3 제16호 and the F-2-12 rule are equally status-blind, or (b) leave F-5-25 only where the holder is already an investor (D-8-1, D-8-3) and change nothing. Option (b) is the smaller, defensible change; `needs: {invest: 6}` on F-5-25 means either option stays safe for readers who answer a low investment figure.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제16호 (scratchpad/byl_03.txt lines 72-74); 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부 공익사업 투자이민제, https://www.immigration.go.kr/immigration/1523/subview.do (일반 15억 원 이상 → F-2, 고액 30억 원 이상 → F-5 직접); data/visamap.yaml F-5-25 (line 1451), F-2-12 edge sources (lines 98/133/163/192/231)
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - Refuted as a defect. I could not confirm the positive claim that status is irrelevant from the source cited: the immigration.go.kr 공익사업 투자이민제 page I fetched describes the 15억 and 30억 tiers and shows both 단기체류자 and 등록외국인 in the 변경 flow, but it is silent on prior status - absence of a restriction is not a statement that every status qualifies, and I could not reach 별표 1의3 (law.go.kr returned error pages for both 별표 1의2 and 별표 1의3). The repo's own ground truth is silent too: visadocs-targets.txt records only 'F-5-25: change', with no source-status matrix. On top of that the finding's own consistency argument proves too much (see corrected_claim), and the impact is minimal because F-5-25 carries needs: {invest: 6}, so it is only ever shown to a reader who explicitly answered ₩3bn+. Low severity and 0.6 confidence are, if anything, generous.
  - corrected claim: The underlying rule claim is fine - the 30억 공익사업 고액투자 route turns on the deposit and the immigration.go.kr page states no prior-status condition - but the inferred data defect does not follow. visamap offers F-5-25 from 7 of ~100 statuses (B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9, D-8-1, D-8-3), i.e. visitor statuses plus the two D-8 sub-codes that themselves carry needs: {invest: 1}. If 'the route depends only on the deposit' were the editorial rule, the edge would be missing from E-7-*, D-9-4, D-10-*, D-2-*, F-2-7 and dozens more, and F-2-12 (the 15억 sibling) would be missing from D-8-1 and D-8-3, where it is in fact absent. So this is a curation policy (offer the investor-immigration route where the reader is already a capital investor or a visitor sizing up Korea), not an inconsistency isolated to D-8-2/D-8-4 - and the proposed two-line patch would not make the map consistent, it would just move the boundary.
  - corrected fix: No change, or else a deliberate map-wide decision about which statuses should offer F-5-25/F-2-12 (made once, for every long-stay status, not for two of them). Note D-8-2 and D-8-4 are also the two D-8 sub-codes with no capital test at all - the repo's own ground truth for D-8-2 (data/visadocs.yaml:6253) asks only for a 벤처/예비벤처 확인서 and IP proof - so they are the least natural statuses from which to offer a ₩3bn deposit route.
  - citation: https://www.immigration.go.kr/immigration/1523/subview.do (fetched: 15억/30억 tiers, F-5 direct on 30억 with 5-year hold, no prior-status condition stated); law.go.kr 별표 1의2 and 별표 1의3 unreachable (error pages); /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-tillo-gurufin-Desktop-code-self-kvppartners-blog/9e3eada8-01bc-4c8d-b5b1-1d2cc07e27c8/scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt ('F-5-25: change'); data/visamap.yaml F-5-25 offered from B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9, D-8-1, D-8-3 only (enumerated from all `next` lists)

### [low] wrong-needs: D-8-4 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: D-8-4's `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` omits the third eligibility route - recommendation by the head of a relevant central government agency - so applicants on the Startup Korea special visa (D-8-4S) track who have no degree are blocked.
- **evidence**: The eligibility clause has three alternatives: '국내 전문학사 이상 취득, 국외 학사 이상 취득 또는 중앙행정기관의 장이 추천한 사람으로서 점수제 등 요건을 충족하거나, K-startup 그랜드챌린지 참여자로서 요건 충족'. The repo's own D-8-4 change list carries the matching points-exempt cases ('K-Startup Grand Challenge participant', 'government startup-support beneficiary', 'Startup Korea special visa (D-8-4S)'). Under engine.md an answered edu/koreanEdu below the threshold blocks, so a ministry-recommended founder who answered 'high school' is hidden from the one visa built for them.
- **citation**: KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › 3. 구직(D-10) › 유형2 1단계 (D-8, 기술창업), p.20: https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 4. 기술창업(D-8-4)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, drop the hard degree gate on D-8-4 (remove the `needs:` block) and state the degree-or-recommendation condition in the `how` text instead; alternatively keep `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` only on the student/job-seeker entries (D-2-*, D-10-* -> D-8-4) and leave the visa itself ungated.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - I tried to refute this three ways and all three failed.

(1) The rule. The primary source confirms the three-way alternative verbatim. 출입국관리법 시행령 [별표 1의2] <개정 2026. 9. 15.>, item 11 기업투자(D-8), 다목: '다음의 어느 하나에 해당하는 사람으로서 지식재산권을 보유하거나 이에 준하는 기술력 등을 가진 사람 중 법무부장관이 인정한 법인 창업자 / 1) 국내에서 전문학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람 / 2) 외국에서 학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람 / 3) 관계 중앙행정기관의 장이 지식재산권 보유 등 우수한 기술력을 보유한 사람으로 인정하여 추천한 사람'. Route 3 carries no degree requirement at all, so `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` is a strictly narrower gate than the law. The KIS navigator wording the finding quotes is also accurate (scratchpad navigator.txt lines 288, 507, 540).

(2) The engine reading. This is where I expected the finding to die (an unanswered fact never blocks, and `needs` as a list means any one alternative suffices, so I expected edu=1 + koreanEdu unknown to yield 'maybe'). It does not: assets/js/visamap.js lines 109-110 derive koreanEdu from the same single answer rather than asking it separately - `if (person.edu !== undefined) { known.koreanEdu = person.eduKorea ? person.edu : 0; }`. So a reader who answers 'high school' gets edu=1 and koreanEdu=0 (or 1 with the Korean-school box ticked), both alternatives return 'no' in checkNeeds, and the step is BLOCKED. The finding's engine claim is exactly right.

(3) Reachability. All 8 edges into D-8-4 (NONE, D-2-1/2/3, D-10-1/2/3, E-7-1) are bare string entries with no move-level `needs` override, so the visa-level gate applies to every one of them, including the first-step NONE -> D-8-4 edge. The D-2-* edges are harmless in practice because D-2-2's `gives` raises edu to 3, but NONE, D-10-* and E-7-1 are not.

The repo's own data corroborates rather than contradicts: data/visadocs.yaml document key `copyOfDegreeCertificateOrRecommendationF` is named 'Copy of degree certificate or recommendation from the relevant ministry', i.e. the manual itself treats the ministry recommendation as a substitute for the diploma, and D-8-4.change carries the three points-exempt variants the finding lists (K-Startup Grand Challenge participant, government startup-support beneficiary, Startup Korea special visa D-8-4S).
  - corrected claim: Confirmed as written. One refinement: the block does not bite on the D-2-1/D-2-2/D-2-3 edges, because those visas' `gives` raise the reader's edu/koreanEdu above the threshold before the step is assessed. It bites on NONE -> D-8-4, D-10-1/2/3 -> D-8-4 and E-7-1 -> D-8-4, for any reader who answered education at 'high school' or below.
  - corrected fix: Take the first option, not the second. In data/visamap.yaml remove the `needs:` block from D-8-4 and fold the condition into `how`, e.g. 'A degree (or a recommendation from the relevant ministry) plus intellectual property or technology, 60+ start-up points and your own Korean corporation.' The author's fallback - keeping `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` on the D-2-*/D-10-* edges only - is itself wrong: route 3 of 다목 is open to a D-10 holder just as much as to anyone else, so that variant would still wrongly block exactly the readers the finding is about (and it would be inert on the D-2-* edges anyway, per the `gives` point above).

- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed at the primary source. 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, item 11 기업투자(D-8), 다목 reads: "다음의 어느 하나에 해당하는 사람으로서 지식재산권을 보유하거나 이에 준하는 기술력 등을 가진 사람 중 법무부장관이 인정한 법인 창업자 - 1) 국내에서 전문학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람 2) 외국에서 학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람 3) 관계 중앙행정기관의 장이 지식재산권 보유 등 우수한 기술력을 보유한 사람으로 인정하여 추천한 사람". Three alternative routes, the third being exactly the central-agency recommendation the finding names, and visamap.yaml:1145-1147 encodes only the first two ({edu: 3} = foreign bachelor, {koreanEdu: 2} = domestic 전문학사). The blocking mechanism is real and worse than 'maybe': assets/js/visamap.js:110 does `known.koreanEdu = person.eduKorea ? person.edu : 0`, so answering edu at all also fixes koreanEdu. A reader who answers 'high school' without Korean schooling gets edu=1, koreanEdu=0, both needs options return 'no', and checkNeeds -> 'no' -> BLOCKED (not 'maybe'). The gate bites on the NONE, E-7-1 and D-10-1/2/3 entry edges (the D-2-* sources pass anyway via `gives: {koreanEdu: 3}`). The finding's own citation (KIS 비자 내비게이터 PART 02 › 3. 구직(D-10), p.20) is a weak pointer for a D-8 rule, but the decree and the repo's own D-8-4 change list (data/visadocs.yaml:6480-6526, points-exempt cases 'K-Startup Grand Challenge participant', 'government startup-support beneficiary', 'Startup Korea special visa (D-8-4S)') carry the claim.

One imprecision in the finding: the recommendation route substitutes for the *degree* requirement, not for the points test. Points-exemption is a separate list (K-Startup Grand Challenge, government startup-support beneficiary, Startup Korea D-8-4S); a Startup Korea D-8-4S applicant is still typically reaching D-8-4 via route 3 because the recommending body is 중소벤처기업부, so the practical effect the finding describes holds.

If you want to keep filtering the graduate routes, attach `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` as a per-move override on the D-10-1/D-10-2/D-10-3 and E-7-1 -> D-8-4 steps only, and leave the NONE -> D-8-4 and D-2-* -> D-8-4 steps ungated (D-2-* already satisfy it through `gives`).

### [low] missing-edge: D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4, D-9-5 -> F-2-3

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-2-3 (spouse or minor child of a permanent resident) is offered from 46 statuses but from none of the D-8/D-9 statuses, so a D-8 or D-9 holder married to an F-5 holder is never shown the route.
- **evidence**: F-2-3's change list has two cases, 'Spouse of an F-5 (permanent resident) holder' and 'Minor child of an F-5 holder', with no condition on the status currently held; the visa is already gated in visamap by `needs: [{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}]`, so adding the edge cannot mislead anyone. D-5, D-6, D-7 and every E status list it; only D-8-* and D-9-* do not.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 (영주자의 배우자·미성년 자녀) (data/visadocs.yaml F-2-3.change)
- **fix**: Add `- F-2-3` to the `next` list of D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4 and D-9-5 in data/visamap.yaml.
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - The core claim survives, though two supporting statements in the evidence are false and I am docking the finding for them rather than refuting it.

The rule holds. 출입국관리법 시행령 [별표 1의2] <개정 2026. 9. 15.>, item 24 거주(F-2), 가목: '국민의 미성년 외국인 자녀 또는 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 체류자격을 가지고 있는 사람의 배우자 및 그의 미성년 자녀'. There is no condition on the status currently held, and data/visadocs.yaml F-2-3.change (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 2) likewise attaches no prior-status condition to either variant. D-8 and D-9 are not among the statuses subject to change-of-status restrictions, so a D-8/D-9 holder married to an F-5 holder can change to F-2-3.

The data defect holds too. I enumerated the `next` lists: 46 statuses offer F-2-3 and none of D-8-1/2/3/4 or D-9-1/4/5 do. Per engine.md, anything not in `next` is unreachable, and there is no other mechanism that would surface it, so the route is never shown. Confirmed.

Where the finding is wrong: (a) 'only D-8-* and D-9-* do not' is false. Excluding F-5 goals and the F-2-*/F-6-* statuses where the route is moot, the following also lack F-2-3: D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, H-2, F-4, F-4-R, G-1-5, G-1-6. (b) 'every E status lists it' is false - E-8 does not (and H-1 does while H-2 does not). (c) 'adding the edge cannot mislead anyone' overstates: `needs: [{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}]` yields 'maybe' for any adult reader who leaves the spouse question blank (parent is forced to 'none' for readers 19+ at visamap.js:113), so the option would show as an unconfirmed maybe to every D-8/D-9 reader, exactly as it already does for the existing 46 sources. That is consistent with the rest of the map, so it is not a reason to reject the edge, but the evidence's framing is wrong.

The proposed fix is directionally right and would not introduce anything incorrect; it is just incomplete.
  - corrected claim: F-2-3 is offered from 46 statuses but from none of D-8-1/2/3/4 or D-9-1/4/5, so a D-8 or D-9 holder married to an F-5 holder is never shown the route. The gap is wider than the finding states: D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, H-2, F-4, F-4-R, G-1-5 and G-1-6 also omit it, so 'only D-8-* and D-9-* do not' and 'every E status lists it' are both incorrect. Adding the edge is not risk-free in the way claimed either: F-2-3 shows as an unconfirmed 'maybe' to any reader who leaves the spouse question blank - the same as on the 46 statuses that already offer it.
  - corrected fix: Add `- F-2-3` to the `next` lists of D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4 and D-9-5 as proposed, and at the same time decide deliberately about the other omissions rather than leaving an arbitrary split: D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, H-2 and G-1-5/G-1-6 are all statuses from which 별표 1의2 24 가목 permits the change. If the map's rule is 'any long-term status the holder could be sitting in', apply it uniformly; if it is 'only statuses where the manual documents the change', note that the manual documents no prior-status condition at all, which argues for the uniform treatment.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 [별표 1의2] <개정 2026. 9. 15.>, 24. 거주(F-2) 가목 (scratchpad/byl_02.txt lines 181-183; same text in b12.txt line 150); /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-3'].change (two variants, no prior-status condition); /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visamap.yaml F-2-3 needs/next and the 46 source `next` lists; engine.md 'next' section; assets/js/visamap.js:113
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The substantive claim is confirmed, two supporting details in the evidence are wrong. Primary source: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, item 24 거주(F-2) 가목 reads "국민의 미성년 외국인 자녀 또는 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 체류자격을 가지고 있는 사람의 배우자 및 그의 미성년 자녀" - no condition at all on the status currently held, so a D-8 or D-9 holder married to an F-5 holder qualifies on the merits. The repo's own ground truth agrees: visadocs-targets.txt lists 'F-2-3: change,abroad,extend' and data/visadocs.yaml:9770-9804 has only the two cases 'Spouse of an F-5 (permanent resident) holder' and 'Minor child of an F-5 holder' with no prior-status restriction. I verified the count: exactly 46 statuses list F-2-3 in `next`, and none of D-8-1/2/3/4 or D-9-1/4/5 do. Two errors in the evidence: (a) 'every E status lists it' is false - E-8 does not; (b) 'only D-8-* and D-9-* do not' is false - D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, G-1-5, G-1-6 and H-2 also omit it. The 'cannot mislead anyone' argument is also slightly loose: for an adult who leaves spouse blank, {spouse: f5} evaluates 'maybe' while {parent: f5} is 'no' (visamap.js:113 forces parent='none' at 19+), and checkNeeds picks the 'maybe' option, so the row shows as open - but that is the identical behaviour on all 46 existing edges, so it introduces nothing new.
  - corrected claim: F-2-3 is listed as a next step from 46 statuses but from none of D-8-1/2/3/4 or D-9-1/4/5, even though 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 거주(F-2) 가목 puts no condition on the status held, so a D-8/D-9 holder married to an F-5 holder is never shown the route. The evidence's exclusivity claim is wrong: D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, E-8, G-1-5, G-1-6 and H-2 also omit F-2-3, and E-8 is an E status that omits it. The real shape of the gap is that the D-8/D-9 blocks carry no family routes at all: the same seven statuses are also the only non-F work statuses missing F-6-1 (also offered from exactly 46 statuses), which has the same 'no condition on prior status' basis (별표 1의2 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) 가목 '국민의 배우자').
  - corrected fix: In data/visamap.yaml, add `- F-2-3` to the `next` list of D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4 and D-9-5 (as proposed), and in the same pass add `- F-6-1` to those seven lists, since it is missing from exactly the same statuses on the same grounds. Both targets are already gated at the visa level (F-2-3 by `needs: [{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}]`, F-6-1 by its own spouse need), so no per-move `needs` is required. Leave D-3, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-10-2/3/T, E-8, G-1-5, G-1-6 and H-2 out of scope for this finding rather than implying D-8/D-9 are the only omissions.
  - citation: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 거주(F-2) 가목 and 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) 가목, current text as of 2026-10-02 (법령일련번호 290501), fetched from law.go.kr: https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML . ### [high] wrong-needs: E-3 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: E-3's gating is `needs: {edu: 4}` (a master's, nothing else), which offers E-3 to any foreign master's holder with no research experience; the manual requires a doctorate, or a master's plus 3 years' experience (waived only for a Korean master's, a master's from a TIMES top-200 / QS top-500 university, or an SCI(E)/SSCI/A&HCI-indexed publication). E-3's own `how` text states the correct rule, so the gating contradicts the data's own description.
- **evidence**: KIS 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 21. 연구(E-3) › 자격요건: '박사 학위 소지자(취득예정자 포함)' 또는 '석사 학위 소지자로서 3년 이상 경력자', with the 3-year requirement exempted for 국내 석사학위 소지자, 석사 from a TIMES top-200/QS top-500 university, and authors of SCIE/A&HCI/SCI/SSCI papers. The same 자격요건 (박사 / 석사+3년, 국내석사 면제) appears verbatim in the university-level E-3 manual derived from the KIS manual. Field is irrelevant: 시행령 별표 1의2 제16호 covers 자연과학 as well as 사회과학·인문학·예체능 research, so no `stem` condition is needed either.
- **citation**: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 21. 연구(E-3) › 해당자·자격요건 (quoted at https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=281); 외국인 연구원 비자(E-3) 관리 매뉴얼, Ⅰ. 연구(E-3) 비자의 신청 자격 › 자격요건 (https://sanhak.jnu.ac.kr/sites/sanhak/files/video_download04.pdf); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제16호 연구(E-3) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=152014589&bylClsCd=110201)
