### [high] wrong-needs: E-1 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: E-1's `needs` list is wrong in every branch: it accepts a bachelor's with only 2 years and a junior-college diploma with 3 years (both below the statutory faculty standard), and it makes the doctorate and master's routes conditional on `stem: true`, which has no legal basis and hides E-1 from every non-science doctorate holder.
- **evidence**: 시행령 별표 1의2 제14호 defines 교수(E-1) as a foreigner meeting the 고등교육법 제14조①②/제17조 qualification, i.e. the 대학교원 자격기준 등에 관한 규정 별표: 조교수 needs 연구실적 2년 + 교육경력 2년 = 계 4년 for a 대학졸업자 and 3 + 4 = 계 7년 for a 전문대학졸업자 (and the two year-counts are interchangeable). The standard conversion table credits a 박사학위 소지자 with 4 years and a 석사학위 소지자 with 2 years, so a doctorate alone meets 조교수, a master's needs 2 further years and a bachelor's 4. No field restriction exists anywhere: the KIS manual's E-1 해당자 is simply '고등교육법에 의한 자격요건을 갖춘 외국인… 전임강사 이상의 교수', with '고급과학기술인력 (교육부장관 고용추천)' listed as an extra category, not a limit. The visamap engine makes this concrete: layouts/visa-map.html sets `stem` from a checkbox and assets/js/visamap.js does `details.stem = extras && f.stem.checked`, so stem is explicitly false (never unknown) for anyone who entered a degree - a humanities/social-science PhD with 0 years of experience is therefore BLOCKED from E-1, while a bachelor's holder with 2 years is shown as OPEN.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제14호 교수(E-1) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=152014589&bylClsCd=110201); 대학교원 자격기준 등에 관한 규정 [별표] 교원 및 조교의 자격기준 (https://www.ptu.ac.kr/bbs/www/310/20121/download.do ; https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/lsInfoP.do?lsiSeq=123603); 경력 환산표 박사 4년/석사 2년 (https://www.skku.edu/app/common/attachDownload.do?articleNo=73885&attachNo=58463 ; KAIST E-1 visa page https://io.kaist.ac.kr/m/visa/visa_prof.do); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 19. 교수(E-1) › 해당자 (quoted at https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=293)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, replace E-1's `needs` block (lines 605-609) with:
needs:
- {edu: 5}
- {edu: 4, experience: 2}
- {edu: 3, experience: 4}
- {edu: 2, experience: 7}
i.e. drop `stem: true` from both branches, drop the 3-year master's requirement, raise the bachelor's branch from 2 to 4 years and the junior-college branch from 3 to 7 years.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-1 -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: E-1's `next` list has no E-7-1 entry, so a professor leaving a university for a company job (or for an E-7 university-lecturer post, occupation 2612) is never offered that move, although the E-7 chapter explicitly lists E-1 holders as change-eligible.
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 covers 'Registered D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6 (not E-6-2) holders … changing to E-7' (already copied into data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change), and the E-7 occupation list includes 대학강사(2612) and 외국법자문사(261), posts E-1 holders routinely move into. The map already offers E-1 → E-3 but not E-1 → E-7-1, while E-2 and E-4 do offer E-7-1, so the omission is a data gap rather than a legal restriction.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (in-repo: data/visadocs.yaml targets.E-7-1.change); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 직종별 세부기준 대학강사(2612)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- E-7-1` to E-1's `next` list (insert after `- E-3` on line 579). No `abroad` flag, no `needs` override.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-5 -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: E-5's `next` list contains no work-visa move at all (no E-7-1, no other E status), so a licensed professional - e.g. a foreign-law practitioner or a doctor moving into a non-licensed specialist role - has no onward employment option except D-10-1 job search, even though the E-7 chapter lists E-5 holders as change-eligible.
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 covers 'Registered D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6 (not E-6-2) holders … changing to E-7' (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change), and E-7's occupation list contains 외국법 전문가/외국법자문사(261) - the very group that used to sit in E-5 - plus 의료 관련 전문가 posts. E-5's own 해당자 list (pilots, licensed ship officers, doctors/medical trainees) is defined by the licence, not by any bar on changing status.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (in-repo: data/visadocs.yaml targets.E-7-1.change); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제18호 전문직업(E-5) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=152014589&bylClsCd=110201); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 23. 전문직업(E-5) › 해당자 (quoted at https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=265)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- E-7-1` as the first item of E-5's `next` list (after line 690). No `abroad` flag, no `needs` override.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-2 -> F-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: No E status offers F-4: F-4 is reachable only from B-1, B-2, C-3-*, H-2 and NONE. An ethnic-Korean E-2 teacher (Korean-American/Korean-Canadian nationals are a large share of E-2 holders) is therefore never shown the in-Korea change to 재외동포(F-4) - the standard route that frees them from employer restrictions and leads to F-5-6 after 2 years.
- **evidence**: F-4 status is granted to 외국국적동포 as such (former Korean nationals, F-4-41, and their descendants, F-4-42), not by reference to the status they currently hold, and the manual documents an in-Korea change procedure for it ('다. 재외동포(F-4) 자격 변경 절차'). visadocs-targets.txt confirms F-4 has a `change` list, so an in-Korea change is documented and no `abroad` flag is needed. The target already carries `needs: {korean: true}`, so adding the edge only exposes it to readers who answered that they are ethnic Korean.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 36. 외국국적동포 관련 (알기쉬운 외국국적동포 업무매뉴얼 2026.2) › Ⅱ.4 재외동포(F-4) 자격부여 › 다. 재외동포(F-4) 자격 변경 절차 (in-repo: data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-4.change); 재외동포의 출입국과 법적 지위에 관한 법률 제2조제2호
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-4` to E-2's `next` list (after line 611's `- E-7-1`), and for the same reason to the `next` lists of E-1 (line 579), E-3 (line 638), E-4 (line 669) and E-5 (line 691). No `abroad` flag and no `needs` override - F-4's own `needs: {korean: true}` does the gating.

### [medium] wrong-countries: E-2 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: E-2 carries no `countries` gate and only `needs: {edu: 3}`, so the tool shows 'E-2 language instructor' as an OPEN option to a reader of any nationality who holds any bachelor's from anywhere - while the 법무부 자격요건 requires the applicant to be a national of a country where the taught language is the mother tongue (for English: US, UK, Canada, South Africa, New Zealand, Australia, Ireland) AND to hold the bachelor's from a university in that same country.
- **evidence**: 시행령 별표 1의2 제15호 makes E-2 conditional on '법무부장관이 정하는 자격요건'. The manual's 자격요건 is: a national of a country where the target language is the native/official language, holding a bachelor's degree or higher obtained at a university in that country (alternatively: home-country high school plus a Korean bachelor's); for English instruction the eligible nationalities are the seven designated countries. E-2's own `how` text in this file states the seven-country rule, so the gating contradicts the data's own description, and the engine never blocks on a rule that is only in prose (engine.md: only `countries`/`needs` can block).
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제15호 회화지도(E-2) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=152014589&bylClsCd=110201); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 20. 회화지도(E-2) › 자격요건 (quoted at https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=292)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a nationality group under `groups:` - `e2: US,GB,CA,ZA,NZ,AU,IE,CN,TW,JP,FR,DE,ES,RU,VN,MN,TH,ID,SA` (the seven English-designated countries plus the mother-tongue countries of the other languages Korean institutes hire for) - and add `countries: "e2"` to the E-2 visa entry (next to `needs: {edu: 3}` on line 633). Keep `needs: {edu: 3}` and extend E-2's `how`/add a note stating the degree must be from that same country (or home-country high school plus a Korean bachelor's).

### [medium] wrong-needs: E-1 -> F-5-11

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-5-11 ('Outstanding ability') has no `needs`, no `age`, no `lang` and the E-1 and E-3 edges to it carry no note, so the engine shows permanent residence via 특정분야 능력 소유자 as an unconditional, always-OPEN option to every professor and researcher, although it is an exceptional route requiring a Ministry-of-Justice finding of world-class ability measured by a points table.
- **evidence**: 시행령 별표 1의3 제9호 limits this route to '과학·경영·교육·문화예술·체육 등 특정 분야에서 탁월한 능력이 있는 사람 중 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람'. The KIS criteria are a points table in which the applicant must satisfy at least one 필수항목 (Nobel/Pulitzer-class award, world-ranked research record with SCI/SSCI output, Olympic/Asian Games medal, major patents, etc.), score 30+ on 필수항목 and 50+ in total. Per engine.md a visa with no `needs` is never blocked, so this is gating that is too loose in the strongest sense - the option is offered to everyone standing on E-1/E-3 with no qualification shown at all.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제9호 (https://law.go.kr/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=53439633 - '과학·경영·교육·문화예술·체육 등 특정 분야에서 탁월한 능력이 있는 사람 중 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람'); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 32. 영주(F-5) › 8. 특정분야 능력 소유자〔F-5-11〕 (in-repo: data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-5-11.change); F-5-11 필수/선택 점수표 요약 (https://immikorea.com/f-5-11/)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml give F-5-11 (lines 1412-1414) `needs: {edu: 5}` (the practical 필수항목 for the academic/professional tracks that reach it from E-1/E-3/E-7) and add a note to the F-5-11 entries in E-1's and E-3's `next` lists stating 'Only for exceptional achievement: at least one mandatory item and 50+ points on the Ministry of Justice table; no minimum stay.'

### [low] missing-edge: E-4 -> F-5-10

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-4 is the only one of E-1~E-5 with no talent-based permanent-residence route at all: its `next` list offers F-5-1 only, while E-1, E-2, E-3 and E-5 all offer F-5-10. The F-5-10 route is defined by the applicant's degree or technical qualification plus 3 years' residence, not by the status held, and E-4 holders (industrial-technology instructors) are precisely the 기술자격증 group it targets.
- **evidence**: 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호: '법무부장관이 정하는 분야의 학사 학위 이상의 학위증 또는 법무부장관이 정하는 기술자격증이 있는 사람으로서 국내 체류기간이 3년 이상이고, 영주자격(F-5) 신청 시 국내기업에 고용되어 법무부장관이 정하는 금액 이상의 임금을 받는 사람' - no status precondition. The same is true of 별표 1의3 제7호 가·나 (F-5-9 / F-5-15) and 제9호 (F-5-11). F-5-10 in visadocs-targets.txt has a `change` list, so no `abroad` flag is needed, and the target's own `needs` ([{edu: 3, stem: true}, {koreanEdu: 4}] plus KIIP 5) already filters readers who cannot qualify.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호 (https://law.go.kr/flDownload.do?gubun=&flSeq=53439633); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 32. 영주(F-5) › 7. 학사·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자〔F-5-10〕 (in-repo: data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-5-10.change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-5-10` to E-4's `next` list (after the F-5-1 entry, around line 676), matching E-5's entry; optionally also add F-5-9, F-5-11 and F-5-15 there, since all three are status-agnostic routes gated by their own `needs`.

## graph / e6-e10

### [medium] unreachable: E-6-2 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-6-2 (hotel/venue performer) is a dead node: no visa anywhere in the graph lists it in 'next', and NONE's next list omits it, so the tool can never route anyone into E-6-2 even though an E-6-2 visa issued abroad is the ONLY way anyone ever holds that status.
- **evidence**: grep over data/visamap.yaml shows E-6-2 appears only as its own node definition (line 732) and inside the D-10-1 F-2-R notAfter list (line 528); NONE's next (lines 1481-1540) lists E-6-1 and E-6-3 but not E-6-2. Legally E-6-2 exists as a sub-code of 예술흥행(E-6) in 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-2 (관광진흥법에 따른 호텔업 시설·유흥업소 등에서의 공연/연예활동) and is issued abroad by 사증발급인정서 with a 공연추천서 (영상물등급위원회). Every manual source I checked treats E-6-2 as a status people hold and then restricts what they can do next (the E-7 변경 chapter excludes 'E-6-2' by name; the D-10 점수제 면제 list says 'E-1~E-7 (E-6-2 제외)'), which is only meaningful if E-6-2 is reachable. There is no documented in-Korea change TO E-6-2 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-6-2'] has only an 'extend' list), so the abroad route is the only entry point and omitting it makes the status unreachable.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-2 제29호 예술흥행(E-6); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 24. 예술흥행(E-6) › 사증발급인정서 발급대상 (as recorded in data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-6-1'].abroad, variant '공연법에 의한 공연'); https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=219
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, add `- E-6-2` to the NONE visa's `next:` list (immediately after `- E-6-1`, around line 1515). Moves out of NONE are already treated as from abroad by the engine, so no `abroad: true` is needed.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-9 -> E-9

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-9 offers no way to continue on E-9: the 재입국취업 특례 (the 'sincere/committed worker' re-entry scheme) is the single most common next step for an EPS worker who reaches the 4-year-10-month ceiling, and the tool can never suggest it.
- **evidence**: E-9's total stay is capped at 3 years + 1 year 10 months re-employment = 4 years 10 months. 외국인근로자의 고용 등에 관한 법률 제18조의4 (재입국 취업의 제한의 특례) lets a worker who finished that period with the same employer leave Korea and re-enter on a fresh E-9 after one month, for another 4 years 10 months; the age ceiling and a new EPS-TOPIK are not re-applied. The engine explicitly supports a target that already appears on the path (`again:` replaces `note:`), so this is expressible. Without it the graph's only continuations from E-9 are E-7-4/E-7-4R/D-10-1/F-6-1/F-2-3 plus two abroad moves, and a reader who cannot meet the E-7-4 points test is shown nothing at all.
- **citation**: 외국인근로자의 고용 등에 관한 법률 제18조의4 (재입국 취업의 제한의 특례); E-9 stay ceiling 3년 + 1년10개월 = 4년10개월 confirmed at https://www.bangduty.com/status/bijeonmuncwieob-gyejeolgeunro-seonweoncwieob/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, add to E-9's `next:` list (after `- E-7-4R`): `- {to: E-9, abroad: true, again: {en: 'Re-entry special case: after 4 years 10 months with the same employer you may leave Korea for one month and return on a new E-9 for up to another 4 years 10 months.'}}`.

### [low] missing-edge: E-10 -> D-2-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-10 (seafarer) is given no study route, although E-9 - which is treated identically by the D-2 change rules - is given `{to: D-2-2, abroad: true}`.
- **evidence**: The 유학(D-2) chapter's 체류자격 변경 restriction list names 기술연수(D-3), 계절근로(E-8), 비전문취업(E-9) and 선원취업(E-10) together as statuses that may not change to D-2 inside Korea - i.e. E-9 and E-10 are in exactly the same position, and for both the route is a new D-2 visa from a consulate. The data encodes that correctly for E-9 (data/visamap.yaml line 989: `- {to: D-2-2, abroad: true}`) but E-10's next list (lines 994-1004) is E-7-4, E-7-4R, D-10-1, F-6-1, E-7-1 (abroad), F-2-3 with no D-2 entry at all. data/visadocs.yaml confirms D-2-2 has an `abroad` list, so the move is expressible and documented.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 (변경 제한 대상: D-3, E-8, E-9, E-10), summarised at https://yoonhjs.com/dtype-visa%EB%AC%B8%ED%99%94%EC%9C%A0%ED%95%99%EC%97%B0%EC%88%98%EC%B7%A8%EC%9E%AC/?mod=document&uid=129
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, add `- {to: D-2-2, abroad: true}` to E-10's `next:` list (after `- {to: E-7-1, abroad: true}`, around line 1003), mirroring the E-9 entry at line 989.

### [low] wrong-note: E-9 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-9's `how` text states 'citizens of 17 partner countries'; since 14 August 2026 there are 18 designated EPS sending countries.
- **evidence**: On 2026-08-14 the government designated Kazakhstan as the 18th EPS 송출국 ('고용허가제 송출국은 ... 17개국이며, 카자흐스탄이 추가되면서 18번째 송출국이 됐다'), with workers arriving from 2028. The data already carries a correct KZ note ('Kazakhstan was named an EPS partner in August 2026; workers are expected from about 2028') and correctly keeps KZ out of the `eps` country group for now, so only the '17' in the `how` sentence is stale. Note the two statements now contradict each other on the same card.
- **citation**: https://www.ajunews.com/view/20260814104708034 ; https://biz.heraldcorp.com/article/10841162
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml E-9's `how` (line 979), change '17 partner countries' to '18 partner countries' (keep `countries: eps` at the current 17 codes and keep the KZ note explaining that Kazakh recruitment starts around 2028).

### [low] missing-edge: E-6-2 -> D-2-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: E-6-2's next list is only [F-6-1, F-2-3]; the in-Korea change to a student status (D-2-2/D-2-3/D-2-4) is withheld from E-6-2 although E-6-2 is not on the D-2 change-restriction list, while the otherwise identical E-6-1 and E-6-3 are given all three.
- **evidence**: The statuses barred from an in-Korea change to 유학(D-2) are 기술연수(D-3), 계절근로(E-8), 비전문취업(E-9) and 선원취업(E-10). E-6-2 is not among them. The exclusions that really do single out E-6-2 are elsewhere and are all already honoured by the data: E-7 변경 ('관광진흥법에 의한 호텔업시설, 유흥업소 등에서 공연하는 자(E-6-2)는 제외'), the D-10 점수제 면제 list ('E-1~E-7, E-6-2 제외') and F-2-7 ('예술흥행(E-6), 단 E-6-2는 제외'). None of them touches D-2. So the data is too strict here and an E-6-2 performer who wants to enrol in a Korean university is shown no route.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가, 변경 제한 대상 D-3/E-8/E-9/E-10 (https://yoonhjs.com/dtype-visa%EB%AC%B8%ED%99%94%EC%9C%A0%ED%95%99%EC%97%B0%EC%88%98%EC%B7%A8%EC%9E%AC/?mod=document&uid=129); E-6-2 exclusions listed only for E-7 (https://yoonhjs.com/e-visa-%ED%9A%8C%ED%99%94%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%A0%84%EB%AC%B8%EC%A7%81%EC%98%88%EC%88%A0%ED%9D%A5%ED%96%89%ED%8A%B9%EC%A0%95%ED%99%9C%EB%8F%99%EA%B3%84%EC%A0%88%EA%B7%BC%EB%A1%9C%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=219) and for D-10 (https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=150)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change E-6-2's `next: [F-6-1, F-2-3]` (line 735) to also carry the three study moves used by E-6-1, i.e. `next: [F-6-1, F-2-3, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4]` (optionally with the same 'Change of status in Korea with a university admission letter and proof of funds' note that E-6-1 uses).

### [low] missing-edge: E-9 -> G-1-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: The one status change out of E-9 that the law explicitly names - a change to 기타(G-1) for treatment of an industrial accident, with the right to recover E-9 afterwards - is absent from E-9's next list, and the graph has no G-1 industrial-accident node at all (only G-1-5 refugee applicant and G-1-6 humanitarian stay).
- **evidence**: 법제처 찾기쉬운 생활법령 (외국인근로자 체류 > 체류자격 변경), citing 출입국관리법 제24조, states that changes into E-9 are restricted in principle but allowed by exception for a worker who changed to 기타(G-1) for 산업재해 치료 and then applies to recover E-9 within the overall stay ceiling - i.e. E-9 -> G-1 -> E-9 is a documented route. data/visadocs.yaml also records an E-9 extension case for 'workers who cannot get or apply for a workplace-change permit because of work injury, illness, pregnancy, childbirth', the same situation. Neither leg exists in visamap.yaml, so an injured EPS worker is shown nothing.
- **citation**: https://easylaw.go.kr/CSP/CnpClsMain.laf?popMenu=ov&csmSeq=2042&ccfNo=4&cciNo=1&cnpClsNo=3 (외국인근로자 취업 > 체류자격 변경), 출입국관리법 제24조
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a node `G-1-4:` (name 'Industrial accident / medical treatment', how: 'Treatment of a work injury or illness while your employment permit is suspended') with `next: [{to: E-9, note: ...}]`, and add `- G-1-4` to E-9's `next:` list; do the same for E-10 if the seafarer chapter is confirmed.

### [low] wrong-age: NONE -> E-8

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.35)
- **claim**: The E-8 node carries no `age`, `countries` or `needs` gating at all, so the tool offers seasonal work to a reader of any age, including minors and people well past the programme's upper age limit.
- **evidence**: E-8 is the only work status in the slice with zero gating (data/visamap.yaml lines 972-976: name, how, next only). The programme's two recruitment routes, recorded in data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-8'].abroad ('Workers selected under a municipality MOU (E-8-1, E-8-3)' and 'Relatives within the 4th degree of a marriage migrant'), are both run under 법무부 계절근로자 프로그램 지침, which sets an age band (commonly cited as 만 30세~55세 for the MOU route and 만 19세~55세 for the marriage-migrant-relative route). I could not retrieve the guideline text itself - my web-search budget was exhausted and the 법무부/농식품부 PDFs I could reach cover E-7-4, not E-8 - so the exact band is UNSOURCED here; what is solidly established is only that some age floor and ceiling exist and that the data has none.
- **citation**: 법무부 외국인 계절근로자 프로그램 기본계획/지침 (연령 요건) - not retrieved; supporting route structure from 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 26. 계절근로(E-8) › 사증발급인정서, as recorded in data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-8'].abroad
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `age: [19, 55]` to the E-8 node (after `next:`, around line 976) after confirming the current band in the 법무부 계절근로자 프로그램 지침; if the MOU route's 만 30세 floor is to be reflected, express it as a move-level `needs` on the NONE -> E-8 entry rather than widening the node gate.

## graph / e7-main

### [high] wrong-needs: E-7-1 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: E-7-1's qualification list contains an option `{edu: 3, stem: true, ageUnder: 30, countries: asiaPilot}` that is not an E-7-1 route at all - it is the D-10 job-seeker points rule, copied onto the wrong visa, so the tool tells young STEM bachelors from 9 Asian countries that they qualify for E-7-1 with no experience and no related degree.
- **evidence**: The E-7 chapter of the visa manual states the only qualification routes: "(일반요건) ... 도입직종과 연관성이 있는 분야의 석사 이상 학위 소지 / 도입직종과 연관성이 있는 학사학위 소지 + 1년 이상의 해당분야 경력 / 도입직종과 연관성이 있는 분야에 5년 이상의 근무경력", plus named 특별요건 (세계 500대 기업 1년 경력, THE 200/QS 500 대학 학사, 국내 전문대학 이상 졸업자, 부처추천+GNI 1.5배, 고소득 GNI 3배). There is no nationality-limited or age-limited route. The exact rule the YAML encodes appears in the D-10 chapter instead: "(아시아권 QS 1,000위 이내 현지 대학 이공계 졸업생) ①29세 이하, ②한국어능력시험(TOPIK) 2급이상 소지자로 ③베트남, 중국, 태국, 일본, 인도, 인도네시아, 말레이시아, 싱가포르, 필리핀(총 9개국) 국적만 허용(20점) - '27.10.까지 시범운영" - i.e. a 20-point bonus / points-exemption for the D-10-1 구직 visa, valid only to Oct 2027. The repo's `asiaPilot` group (VN,CN,TH,JP,IN,ID,MY,SG,PH) matches that list exactly and is used nowhere else in visamap.yaml. Because E-7-1's `needs` is an ANY-of list, this one entry opens E-7-1 to a 25-year-old Indian STEM bachelor with 0 years of experience, whom the manual's 학사+1년 경력 rule excludes.
- **citation**: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 3. 도입직종별 자격요건 › 가. 자격요건 (일반요건/특별요건); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 구직(D-10) › 점수제 평가항목 3. 가점 ② 및 '세계 우수대학 졸업자' 각주 (local copies: scratchpad/e7.txt lines 85-120, scratchpad/d10.txt lines 418-547, 641-650)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, delete the last entry of E-7-1's `needs` list (line 796: `- {edu: 3, stem: true, ageUnder: 30, countries: asiaPilot}`). The remaining five options correctly model the manual. The `asiaPilot` group then becomes unused and belongs, if anywhere, on D-10-1 (where `needs: [{edu: 3}, ...]` already admits these graduates).

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-4R -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-7-4R offers no route to F-2-99, although it is the same 특정활동(E-7) status as E-7-4, which the graph does give F-2-99; as a result a regional skilled worker can never be shown the long-term-resident route.
- **evidence**: The 기타 장기체류자 (F-2-99) change-of-status chapter lists the eligible prior statuses as 문화예술(D-1), 취재(D-5), 종교(D-6), 주재(D-7), 기업투자(D-8), 무역경영(D-9), 교수(E-1)~전문직업(E-5), 예술흥행(E-6-1/3), 특정활동(E-7), 방문동거(F-1) - 특정활동(E-7) as a whole, with no carve-out for the E-7-4R sub-code; only 단기사증, G-1, H-1 and illegal stayers are excluded. The repo already encodes this for E-7-4 (`- {to: F-2-99, note: 'After 5 years on E-7 (E-9 years do not count).'}`), and E-7-4R is granted under the same E-7 status (data/visadocs.yaml lists E-7-4R's change list under 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅲ. 지역특화 숙련기능인력(E-7-4R)). The 5-year count is '신청 대상 체류자격으로 대한민국에 5년 이상 계속 체류', so E-7-4 and E-7-4R years count the same way.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 9. 기타 장기체류자에 대한 거주 자격 변경허가 (as reproduced at https://yoonhjs.com/f-type-visa-%EB%8F%99%EA%B1%B0%EA%B1%B0%EC%A3%BC%EB%8F%99%EB%B0%98%EC%98%81%EC%A3%BC%EA%B2%B0%ED%98%BC/?mod=document&uid=444 and https://kormoya.com/f-2-99-%EC%9E%A5%EA%B8%B0%EC%B2%B4%EB%A5%98%EC%9E%90-%EA%B1%B0%EC%A3%BC%EC%B2%B4%EB%A5%98%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9-%EB%B3%80%EA%B2%BD-%EC%9A%94%EA%B1%B4/)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `E-7-4R:` add to `next`: `- {to: F-2-99, note: {en: 'After 5 years on E-7 (E-9 years do not count).'}}` - i.e. the same entry E-7-4 already has.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-4 -> F-5-10

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-7-4 (and E-7-4R) offer no F-5-10 route, although 별표 1-3 제8호 grants it on a Korean technical licence after 3 years in Korea - exactly the licence that E-7-4's own points table rewards - while E-7-1, E-7-2 and E-7-3 do get the edge.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호: "법무부장관이 정하는 분야의 학사 학위 이상의 학위증 또는 법무부장관이 정하는 기술자격증이 있는 사람으로서 국내 체류기간이 3년 이상이고, 영주자격(F-5) 신청 시 국내기업에 고용되어 법무부장관이 정하는 금액 이상의 임금을 받는 사람" - the clause is status-agnostic and the 기술자격증 branch needs no degree. E-7-4's own points table scores "숙련도: 자격증 소지 / 기량검증 통과 / 기사 / 산업기사 / 기능사" (국가기술자격법 시행규칙 별표2 기술·기능분야), and the E-7-4 and E-7-4R document lists in data/visadocs.yaml both include `copyOfKoreanTechnicalLicence`, so licence-holding E-7-4 workers are the normal case, not an edge case. The graph nevertheless gives F-5-10 only from E-1, E-2, E-3, E-5, E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호 (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 특정활동(E-7) › 숙련기능인력(E-7-4) 점수제 평가 항목 '숙련도' (scratchpad/e7.txt lines 2255-2262); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 32. 영주(F-5) › 7. 학사․석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자〔F-5-10〕
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to `E-7-4:` next (and to `E-7-4R:` next) `- {to: F-5-10, needs: {}, note: {en: 'With a Korean engineer licence (or a degree in a designated field) after 3+ years in Korea, in a job paying the required wage.'}}`. The `needs: {}` override is needed because F-5-10's visa-level `needs: [{edu: 3, stem: true}, {koreanEdu: 4}]` has no way to express the licence branch of 별표 1-3 제8호 and would hide the option from exactly the licence holders it is for.

### [medium] wrong-needs: E-7-3 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-7-3's `needs` accepts `{experience: 1}` as a standalone qualification, but no E-7-3 skilled-trade occupation can be entered on one year of experience; the experience-only routes are 5 years, 10 years, or a licence plus 2 years and a skills test.
- **evidence**: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼, 특정활동(E-7) Ⅳ. 기능인력에 대한 세부기준, per occupation: 양식기술자 "(자격요건) 수산분야 학사이상 학위, 수산분야 전문학사학위 + 해당 양식기술 분야 경력"; 할랄도축원 "(자격요건) 고등학교 이상 졸업 + 5년 이상 할랄 도축 경력"; 악기제조 및 조율사 "(자격요건) 경력 10년 이상"; 조선용접기능공 "(자격요건) 중급이상 조선용접공 자격증 취득 후 2년 이상 경력 + 현지 기량검증"; 동물사육사 and 선박도장공 "(자격요건) 일반 요건 적용" = the E-7 general rule of 석사 / 학사+1년 경력 / 5년 경력. The shortest experience-only path in the chapter is 5 years, so a reader who answers 'no degree, 1 year of experience' is told E-7-3 is open when every occupation in it is closed to them.
- **citation**: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › Ⅳ. 기능인력에 대한 세부기준 (local copy: scratchpad/e7.txt lines 2005-2120, esp. 2036, 2058, 2066, 2081)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change E-7-3's `needs` third option from `- {experience: 1}` to `- {experience: 5}` (keeping `- {edu: 3}` and `- {koreanEdu: 2}`, which are supported by 양식기술자 and the 국내 전문대학 졸업자 경력면제 rule respectively).

### [medium] wrong-note: E-7-4 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: The Korean-language threshold stated for F-2-R is one level too low: the 지역특화형 우수인재 거주 requirement is TOPIK 4급 / KIIP 4단계, not level 3, so every E-7-x → F-2-R row understates what the reader must reach.
- **evidence**: Two independent practitioner write-ups of 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ. 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2) give the same requirement: "사회통합프로그램 4단계 이상 이수 또는 한국어능력시험(TOPIK) 4급 이상" and "TOPIK 4급 이상 또는 사회통합프로그램 4단계 이상 (no waiver available)". The repo's F-2-R says `how: '... KIIP 3 or TOPIK 3 ...'` with `lang: {topik: 3, kiip: 3}`. The other F-2-R conditions in the repo (local-government recommendation, 국내 전문학사 이상 학위 or 생활임금 이상 소득, 5-year regional commitment) match those same sources, so only the language level is off. Note this is also the gate that separates F-2-R from E-7-4R, whose own requirement really is TOPIK 2 / KIIP 2.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ. 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2) › (1) 체류자격 변경, as reproduced at https://yoonhjs.com/f2r-e74r-f4r-f5r-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90/?mod=document&uid=1851 and https://lawfirm-us.com/korea-visa-types-e74-e74r-f2r-f27-f299-f4r-faq/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `F-2-R:` change `lang: {topik: 3, kiip: 3}` to `lang: {topik: 4, kiip: 4}` and change the `how` text from 'KIIP 3 or TOPIK 3' to 'KIIP 4 or TOPIK 4' in all four languages.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-4R -> F-5-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-7-4R has no F-5-1 edge, although 별표 1-3 제1호 counts any 특정활동(E-7) time toward general permanent residence; E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3 and E-7-4 all have the edge, so E-7-4R is the only E-7 code cut off from it.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제1호: "대한민국 「민법」에 따른 성년으로서 별표 1의2 중 10. 주재(D-7)부터 20. 특정활동(E-7)까지의 체류자격이나 별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 체류자격으로 5년 이상 대한민국에 체류하고 있는 사람". E-7-4R is granted as 특정활동(E-7), so its years fall inside that range exactly as E-7-4's do. The repo already encodes the matching E-7 caveat (bachelor's degree) as a move-level `needs: {edu: 3}` on the other four E-7 codes.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제1호 (local copy: scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `E-7-4R:` add to `next`: `- {to: F-5-1, needs: {edu: 3}, note: {en: "E-7 holders need a bachelor's degree for this; the usual route is via F-2-99."}}` - copying the entry E-7-4 already has.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-1 -> E-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: E-7-1 offers no professional-to-professional switch at all: a researcher move to E-3 is documented 'from any status' in the E-3 chapter, yet the graph forces E-7-1 holders to detour through D-10-1 to reach E-3 (or E-1/E-5).
- **evidence**: The repo's own manual-derived list (data/visadocs.yaml, targets E-3 change) has the case 'Researchers joining special-law, defence or national/public research institutes, from any status' alongside the narrower 'D-2 / D-10 holder changing to E-3' case, so an in-Korea change from E-7 to E-3 for that group is documented. The graph gives E-3 only from D-2-3, D-2-4, D-2-5, D-10-1, E-1 and H-1. E-1 ↔ E-3 switching is already modelled in both directions, which shows the pattern is intended; E-7-1, the busiest professional status, is simply not wired into it.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 21. 연구(E-3) › 체류자격 변경허가 (in-repo copy: data/visadocs.yaml, targets › E-3 › change › variants)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to `E-7-1:` next `- {to: E-3, note: {en: 'For a research post at a special-law, defence or national/public research institute.'}}`. (E-1/E-4/E-5 should stay unlisted: their change lists document only D-2/D-10 holders and F-3 spouses as sources.)

## graph / e7-special

### [high] unreachable: E-7-Y -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.88)
- **claim**: E-7-Y has in-degree 0: no visa in the graph lists E-7-Y in its 'next', so the tool can never offer the status to the exact population it was created for.
- **evidence**: E-7-Y (국내성장인력) was created in April 2025 under 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 ch. 38 '국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업‧정주 체류제도'. It is for 18-24 year olds who lived in Korea 7+ years before turning 18 and finished Korean elementary/middle/high school (or 검정고시 / KIIP level 5), and it is obtainable ONLY by change of status inside Korea ('국내 체류자격 변경으로 신청 가능') - there is no abroad issuance. Such youths hold F-3 (dependent of a D/E/F-2 parent), F-1 (방문동거) or D-4 while they are at school. I grepped the whole visamap.yaml: the string 'E-7-Y' appears only at its own definition (line 956); no 'next' list anywhere contains it. The compact graph dump confirms in-degree 0. The visa's own gating (age [18,24], needs {koreanEdu: 1}) is already correct and would do the filtering, so the only thing missing is the inbound edges.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 38. 국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업‧정주 체류제도 › Ⅲ. 체류자격별 요건 및 제출서류 › 2. 국내성장인력(E-7-Y) (cited verbatim in data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-Y']); https://immikorea.com/e-7-y-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EA%B5%AD%EB%82%B4%EC%97%90%EC%84%9C-%EC%84%B1%EC%9E%A5%ED%95%9C-%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%AD%EC%9D%B8-%EC%B2%AD%EC%86%8C%EB%85%84-%EB%8C%80%EC%83%81-%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85%EB%B9%84/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a bare `- E-7-Y` entry to the `next:` list of F-3 (line ~1300s, the 'Dependent family' visa), F-1 and D-4-1. No per-move gating is needed: E-7-Y's own `age: [18, 24]` and `needs: {koreanEdu: 1}` already restrict it. Primary one is F-3; if only one edge is added, add it there.

### [medium] wrong-note: E-7-S1 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: E-7-S1's 'how' says 'A job with a very high income, regardless of degree, experience or occupation' - it names no income threshold at all and the 'regardless of occupation' part is wrong.
- **evidence**: The 우수인재/네거티브 방식 E-7-S1 requirement is verbatim: '소득이 전년도 1인당 국민총소득의 3배 이상이면서 단순노무, 선량한 풍속에 반하는 업종, 기타 외국인의 취업을 제한하는 직종에 취업하지 않으면' - at least 3x the previous year's per-capita GNI (roughly 140M KRW at the 2026 figure), and NOT in simple-labour jobs, jobs against public morals, or occupations otherwise closed to foreigners. The engine has no `income` fact, so this threshold can only live in the text; with the current wording a reader on a 50-60M KRW salary is told they qualify for E-7-S1 and for its 1-year points-exempt F-2-7 route, which they do not. 'Regardless of degree or experience' is correct, 'regardless of occupation' is not.
- **citation**: https://immikorea.com/e-7-s%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EA%B3%A0%EC%86%8C%EB%93%9D%EC%9E%90-%EC%B2%A8%EB%8B%A8%EC%82%B0%EC%97%85%EB%B6%84%EC%95%BC-%EC%A2%85%EC%82%AC-%EC%98%88%EC%A0%95%EC%9E%90/ ; https://hanvisa.net/e-7-s-visa-%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%AD%EC%9D%B8-%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%ED%95%9C%EA%B5%AD%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85-e7visa-%ED%95%9C%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90%ED%96%89%EC%A0%95%EC%82%AC%EC%82%AC%EB%AC%B4/ (사증발급 안내매뉴얼, 특정활동(E-7) › 우수인재(네거티브 방식 전문인력))
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change E-7-S1's `how.en` (line 889) to something like: 'A job paying at least 3x per-capita GNI (about 140M KRW in 2026), in any occupation except simple labour and occupations closed to foreigners; no degree or experience requirement.' and update the ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings to match.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-S2 -> F-2-T

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: E-7-S2 (and E-7-S1) do not offer F-2-T, although the Top-Tier change-of-status rule explicitly covers every E-7 sub-status employed 1+ year in a high-tech field, and E-7-S2 is by definition a high-tech-industry worker.
- **evidence**: The Top-Tier manual states the F-2-T 체류자격변경 대상 verbatim as: '주재(D-7) ~ 특정활동(E-7) 또는 거주(F-2) 자격으로 1년 이상 첨단분야에 취업한 사람 중 소득요건을 제외한 최우수인재 자격 기준을 모두 충족한 사람으로서, 1인당 GNI 2배 이상 소득을 유지하고, 관계 중앙행정기관의 추천을 받은 자'. E-7-S2 is 첨단산업분야 종사(예정)자 - it is the single E-7 sub-code that is guaranteed to be '첨단분야에 취업' - and E-7-S1 requires 3x per-capita GNI, i.e. more than the 2x income the change route asks for. visamap already gives this edge to E-7-1 ('If you meet the Top-Tier criteria') and to E-7-T, but not to E-7-S1/E-7-S2, so a high-tech E-7-S holder is never shown the fastest PR track (F-2-T then F-5-T after 3 years).
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 › Ⅰ. 첨단산업 분야 › 2 톱티어 거주(F-2-T) › 사증발급 및 체류자격 변경 (quoted at https://yoonhjs.com/living_in_korea/%EC%B2%A8%EB%8B%A8%EB%B6%84%EC%95%BC-%ED%95%B4%EC%99%B8-%EC%9D%B8%EC%9E%AC-%ED%83%91%ED%8B%B0%EC%96%B4top-tier-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EB%A7%A4%EB%89%B4%EC%96%BCf2d10e7-visa/ ; chapter also referenced in data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-T'].change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to the `next:` list of BOTH E-7-S1 and E-7-S2 (after the F-2-7 entry):
    - to: F-2-T
      note: {en: 'After 1+ year in a high-tech field with income of at least 2x GNI and a ministry recommendation.'}
(i.e. the same entry E-7-T already carries at line ~935).

### [medium] wrong-needs: E-7-T -> F-2-T

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: The F-2-T gate that applies to this move is a disjunction, `needs: [{edu: 4, rank: 3}, {experience: 5}]`, so bare `experience: 5` alone opens F-2-T; the real Top-Tier criteria require a top-100 degree AND long career together.
- **evidence**: Per engine.md a move with no own `needs` inherits the target visa's `needs`, and a LIST of maps means ANY ONE suffices. E-7-T -> F-2-T carries only a `note`, so F-2-T's list applies. F-2-T's real criteria (identical to E-7-T's, minus income) are: master's/doctorate from a world top-100 university PLUS 8+ years total experience including 3+ years at a global top-500 company, OR a doctorate plus 5+ years of research at a top university/institute. The current OR-list lets any reader who answered 'experience: 5' pass with no degree and no ranking at all, and F-2-T leads straight to the F-5-T goal, so the tool shows a world-class-talent PR track to ordinary 5-year workers. Note the data already encodes the correct conjunctions on E-7-T itself: `needs: [{edu: 4, rank: 3, experience: 8}, {edu: 5, rank: 3, experience: 5}]`.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 (E-7-T 요건: '소득요건을 제외하고 법무부장관이 정한 요건을 모두 갖춘 사람'); https://www.korea.kr/news/policyNewsView.do?newsId=148940244 ('세계순위 100위 이내 대학의 석박사 학위를 취득하고, 세계 500대 기업 3년 이상 근무를 포함한 8년 이상 경력자')
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, replace F-2-T's `needs:` (line ~1278) with the same two conjunctions E-7-T uses:
    needs:
    - {edu: 4, rank: 3, experience: 8}
    - {edu: 5, rank: 3, experience: 5}

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-T -> F-5-9

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: E-7-T offers no degree-based F-5 route at all (no F-5-9, F-5-15, F-5-10 or F-5-11), so its only PR path in the tool is F-2-T then F-5-T, about 4 years, when F-5-9 would give PR after 1 year.
- **evidence**: E-7-T holders by definition hold a master's or doctorate from a world top-100 university and work for a Korean high-tech company. F-5-9 (첨단분야 박사) is exactly 'foreign doctorate in a high-tech field + 1 year full-time at a Korean company, income 1x GNI'; F-5-10 (학사·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자) covers a high-tech bachelor's/Korean master's + 3 years' work; F-5-15 covers a Korean doctorate. visamap gives E-7-1 all four of these (F-5-9, F-5-10, F-5-11, F-5-15), and the F-5 chapters themselves contain these change-of-status lists (data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-9'].change, targets['F-5-10'].change). Nothing in the F-5-9/10/15 rules excludes an E-7-T holder, and the engine already gates them by `needs` ({edu: 5, stem: true} for F-5-9, {edu: 3, stem: true}|{koreanEdu: 4} for F-5-10, {koreanEdu: 5} for F-5-15), so adding the edges cannot over-offer. The same omission applies to E-7-S2 (high-tech industry worker, F-5-9/F-5-10) and E-7-S1 (F-5-10).
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 32. 영주(F-5) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 6. 첨단분야 박사〔F-5-9〕 및 일반분야 박사〔F-5-15〕 and › 7. 학사․석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자〔F-5-10〕 (both cited verbatim in data/visadocs.yaml)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml copy E-7-1's four PR entries into E-7-T's `next:` (line ~934): `- F-5-9` (with E-7-1's note 'If you hold a foreign doctorate in a high-tech field: after 1+ year at a Korean company in that field.'), `- F-5-10`, `- F-5-15` (with E-7-1's note) and `- F-5-11`. Also add `- F-5-10` to E-7-S1's `next` and `- F-5-9`, `- F-5-10`, `- F-5-15` to E-7-S2's `next`.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-Y -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-7-Y's 'next' contains no settlement route except F-2-R: no F-2-99 and no F-5 goal of any kind, even though the chapter that created it is titled 취업‧정주 (employment AND settlement) and E-7-Y years count as E-7 years.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제1호 grants 영주(F-5-1) to a person who has held a status between 주재(D-7) and 특정활동(E-7), or 거주(F-2), for 5+ years. E-7-Y is a 특정활동(E-7) sub-code, so its years count toward F-5-1, and 5 years of lawful residence also opens F-2-99. In visamap E-7-Y's only F-paths are F-2-R, F-6-1 and F-2-3, so a reader standing on E-7-Y is told the status is a dead end unless they move to a depopulating area or marry a Korean. Caveat: the F-5-1 entry used elsewhere in the data carries `needs: {edu: 3}` ('E-7 holders need a bachelor's degree'), which most E-7-Y youths will not satisfy, so F-2-99 is the route that actually matters here; I could not source whether the bachelor's rule is waived for E-7-Y.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제1호 (영주 F-5-1: D-7~E-7 또는 F-2 자격으로 5년 이상 체류); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 9. 기타 장기체류자에 대한 거주 자격 변경허가; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 38. 국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업‧정주 체류제도
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to E-7-Y's `next:` list (line ~958) a bare `- F-2-99`, and an `- to: F-5-1` entry with `needs: {edu: 3}` plus the standard 5-year note used by the other E-7 sub-codes.

### [low] wrong-note: E-7-S2 -> F-2-7

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The E-7-S2 -> F-2-7 note says 'points waived', but an E-7-S2 holder must still satisfy the E-7-S2 60-point test at the F-2-7 change; only the 80-point F-2-7 table is replaced.
- **evidence**: The requirement is verbatim: '국내에 1년 이상 네거티브 방식의 전문인력 자격으로 체류하면서 E-7-S1은 소득요건이 유지되고 E-7-S2는 점수요건 60점 이상을 충족하고 사회통합프로그램 3단계 이상 이수'. So for E-7-S1 'points waived + KIIP 3' is exactly right (income must be maintained), but for E-7-S2 the note drops the condition that still binds, i.e. the 60-point score. The two visas currently share a verbatim-identical note.
- **citation**: https://immikorea.com/e-7-s%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EA%B3%A0%EC%86%8C%EB%93%9D%EC%9E%90-%EC%B2%A8%EB%8B%A8%EC%82%B0%EC%97%85%EB%B6%84%EC%95%BC-%EC%A2%85%EC%82%AC-%EC%98%88%EC%A0%95%EC%9E%90/ ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 5. 점수제 우수인재 및 동반가족의 거주자격 변경허가
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change the note on E-7-S2's F-2-7 entry (line ~913) from 'After 1+ year of work: points waived, KIIP level 3 needed.' to 'After 1+ year of work: the 80-point F-2-7 test is replaced by the E-7-S2 60-point test; KIIP level 3 needed.' Leave E-7-S1's note as it is, or add 'income kept at 3x GNI'.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-T -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-7-T omits F-2-99 (기타 장기체류자) even though every other E-7 sub-code in the data offers it and the F-2-99 route counts years spent in any eligible long-term work status.
- **evidence**: F-2-99 corresponds to 거주(F-2) 바목 '5+ years in Korea with a life base here' and the manual's change-of-status chapter is written for long-term residents generally, not for named sub-codes. visamap offers F-2-99 from E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3, E-7-4, E-7-S1 and E-7-S2, but not from E-7-T, which is an arbitrary gap; an E-7-T holder who does not get the ministry recommendation for F-2-T is left with only F-2-7 and F-5-1 in the tool.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 9. 기타 장기체류자에 대한 거주 자격 변경허가 (cited in data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-99'].change, case 'Other long-term resident (F-2 item 바: 5+ years in Korea with a life base here)')
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a bare `- F-2-99` entry to E-7-T's `next:` list (line ~933), matching E-7-S1/E-7-S2.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-S1 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: E-7-S1, E-7-S2 and E-7-T are the only E-7 sub-codes that do not offer F-2-R, although the 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2-R) change of status is open to lawfully resident professional-status holders.
- **evidence**: F-2-R's own document list in the manual is headed 'Lawful resident in Korea applying for regional talent (F-2-R)' with no restriction to particular E-7 sub-codes, and visamap gives F-2-R to E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3, E-7-4, E-7-4R and E-7-Y. The 우수인재(E-7-S) and 유망톱티어(E-7-T) statuses are 전문인력 E-7 statuses, so the regional-talent track should be reachable from them too. I could not find a source that enumerates the eligible prior statuses sub-code by sub-code, so I am relying on the generic wording of the F-2-R chapter plus the internal inconsistency.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ. 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2) › (1) 체류자격 변경 › 4. 제출서류 (cited in data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-R'].change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a bare `- F-2-R` entry to the `next:` lists of E-7-S1 (line ~889), E-7-S2 (line ~911) and E-7-T (line ~933). F-2-R's `lang: {topik: 3, kiip: 3}` is only a hint, so no extra gating is needed.

### [low] wrong-note: E-7-Y -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.4)
- **claim**: The E-7-Y -> F-2-R move carries no note, so the reader sees F-2-R's generic 5-year/KIIP-3 description, while chapter 38 gives Korea-raised youth their own 지역특화 우수인재(F-2-R) track with a shorter qualifying period.
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 ch. 38 has a dedicated section 'Ⅲ. 체류자격별 요건 및 제출서류 › 3. 지역특화 우수인재(F-2-R)' for this population, separate from the general ch. 37 F-2-R chapter, and secondary guidance describes it as F-2-R after 4+ years of residence in a depopulating area while on D-10 or E-7-Y. The visamap F-2-R 'how' states 5 years plus a Korean associate degree or regional-wage income and KIIP 3/TOPIK 3, which E-7-Y youth (high-school educated) would read as excluding them. I could not retrieve the exact chapter-38 bullet, so the 4-year figure rests on a secondary source - treat the year count as unconfirmed; the fact that a separate track exists is confirmed by the manual's own table of contents.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 38. 국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업‧정주 체류제도 › Ⅲ. 체류자격별 요건 및 제출서류 › 3. 지역특화 우수인재(F-2-R) (cited in data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-R']); https://immikorea.com/e-7-y-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EA%B5%AD%EB%82%B4%EC%97%90%EC%84%9C-%EC%84%B1%EC%9E%A5%ED%95%9C-%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%AD%EC%9D%B8-%EC%B2%AD%EC%86%8C%EB%85%84-%EB%8C%80%EC%83%81-%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85%EB%B9%84/
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml turn E-7-Y's bare `- F-2-R` (line ~959) into a map with its own note, e.g. `- {to: F-2-R, note: {en: 'Youth who grew up in Korea have their own regional track: about 4 years in a depopulating area on E-7-Y or D-10, with a local-government recommendation. Confirm with immigration (1345).'}}`.

## graph / engine

### [medium] engine-bug: layouts/visa-map.html:67 (the $e := dict ... line that builds each visa's page JSON) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.95)
- **claim**: The layout never copies a visa's top-level `abroad` field into the embedded page data, so `visa.abroad` in `nextSteps` (assets/js/visamap.js:59) is permanently `undefined` and the documented "abroad: true on the whole visa = every move out of it is from abroad" feature is dead code. A maintainer who adds the flag in YAML gets no effect and no build warning.
- **evidence**: layouts/visa-map.html:67-73 builds `$e` with exactly name, how, next, age, ageByCountry, countries, needs, gives, lang, notes - `abroad` is not among them. Proved empirically: I copied the repo to /private/tmp/.../scratchpad/repo, added `abroad: true` under `NONE:` in data/visamap.yaml, ran `hugo`, and parsed the `#vm-data` JSON of en/visa-map/index.html: `NONE keys: ['how','name','next','notes']`, `abroad present on NONE? False`. The build emitted no warning (the build-time validator at lines 8-43 does not check this key either). I then injected `visas.NONE.abroad = true` straight into the `#vm-data` JSON in a jsdom harness and re-ran the real bundled visamap.js: all 56 first-visa options immediately gained the ✈ tag, confirming visamap.js:59 works and only the data never arrives.
- **citation**: layouts/visa-map.html:67 vs assets/js/visamap.js:59; scratchpad engine.md section "abroad / abroadFor" ("`abroad: true` on the whole visa = every move out of it is from abroad")
- **fix**: In layouts/visa-map.html, immediately after line 67 (`{{- $e := dict "name" ... "next" $next }}`) add: `{{- with $v.abroad }}{{ $e = merge $e (dict "abroad" .) }}{{ end }}`. Then `abroad: true` can be set on `NONE:` (and on B-2/C-3-9 etc.) in data/visamap.yaml and will actually take effect.

### [medium] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:59 (nextSteps), vs assets/js/visamap.js:76 (howTo) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.9)
- **claim**: `nextSteps` does not treat moves out of `NONE` as from abroad, although `howTo` on line 76 does. For the most common entry point ("I have no Korean visa yet") not one of the 56 offered first visas carries the ✈ marker, so every first visa is presented as an in-Korea change of status; the bug-report snapshot (visamap.js:755, 806) also records them all as not abroad.
- **evidence**: `nextSteps` computes `abroad: Boolean(step.abroad || visa.abroad || (step.abroadFor && isIn(step.abroadFor)))` - no `code === NONE` term - while `howTo` computes `fromAbroad = from === NONE || Boolean(step && step.abroad)`. The two disagree, and the disagreement is visible on the page: in a jsdom run of the real built bundle (nationality UZ, age 22, no current visa), `document.querySelectorAll('.vm-level[data-level="1"] .vm-tag-abroad').length === 0` across all 56 open options, yet the same D-2-2 node's tooltip already contains the embassy-only note that data/visamap.yaml marks `abroad: true` ("For Uzbek citizens (Korean Embassy in Tashkent, 2026): TOPIK 3 and USD 15,500 ..."), which `countryNotes` only shows when `fromAbroad` is true. So the engine simultaneously says "this is a consular application" (note) and "this is a change of status" (no plane). Injecting `abroad` into the data made all 56 show the plane, so the JS term is the missing half.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:59 and :76; scratchpad engine.md: "Moves out of NONE (no Korean visa yet) are always treated as from abroad."
- **fix**: In assets/js/visamap.js:59 change the `abroad` term to `abroad: Boolean(step.abroad || visa.abroad || code === NONE || (step.abroadFor && isIn(step.abroadFor))),` (`code` is already the `nextSteps` parameter). Alternatively land the layout fix above and add `abroad: true` to the `NONE:` visa in data/visamap.yaml - but the layout fix is required for that route to work.

### [medium] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:342 (openMap) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: `openMap` strips unknown codes out of the shared path with `.filter()` instead of rejecting the link, so the surviving codes shift up one position; a link whose first code is no longer in the data silently opens a map that starts from the second visa - a different current status and therefore different recommendations.
- **evidence**: `const codes = (params.get('path') || '').split('.').filter((code) => visas[code]);` then `search.pick(codes[0])`. In the harness, the link `#nationality=UZ&age=30&path=E-9.D-10-1.D-10-2.D-10-1.F-2-R` replays correctly, but changing only the first code to a code not in the data (`path=X-9-9.D-10-1...`) opens the board with the visa box showing "D-10-1 — Job seeker" and the on-path chain `D-10-1, D-10-2, D-10-1, F-2-R`: the reader's real starting status is gone, no error is shown, and F-2-R is now offered because the laundered `previous` is D-10-2 rather than E-9. Second effect of the same function: when a middle step is no longer possible the loop at :371-375 truncates the path and the following `render()` → `saveMap()` (:337, :408) rewrites the address bar with the truncated path (`#...path=E-9.D-10-1` replacing `#...path=E-9.D-10-1.F-2-7`), so an admin opening a bug-report link loses the original link on first paint and can never reproduce what the reader saw.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:340-377 (openMap), :337 (saveMap), :408 (render calls saveMap)
- **fix**: In assets/js/visamap.js:342 replace the filter with a validate-or-bail: `const codes = (params.get('path') || '').split('.').filter(Boolean); if (!codes.length || codes.some((code) => !visas[code])) return;`. Additionally give `render` a `keepLink` argument and skip `saveMap()` for the first render after `openMap` when `path.length < codes.length`, so a truncated replay does not overwrite the incoming link.

### [medium] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:110 (profileFacts) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: `known.koreanEdu = person.eduKorea ? person.edu : 0` conflates two different facts: it records the reader's HIGHEST education level as the level they obtained IN KOREA as soon as the single "studied in Korea" checkbox is ticked. That hands permanent-residence recommendations to people who do not qualify.
- **evidence**: A reader with a Korean bachelor's and a doctorate from abroad can only answer `edu = 5 (doctorate)` + tick "studied in Korea", which yields `koreanEdu = 5`. In the harness (UZ, 35, current visa E-7-1, edu=doctorate, eduKorea ticked, stem ticked, rank=top-100) F-5-15, whose requirement is `needs: {koreanEdu: 5}` (a Korean doctorate), renders `OPEN [✓ Matches your details]`. With the box unticked the same reader gets F-5-15 `BLOCKED [Education doesn't match]`, so the single checkbox flips a PR goal between "you match" and "you don't" with no way to express "my Korean degree was the bachelor's". The same conflation applies to `stem` (visamap.js:111) and `rank` (:241): both are read as properties of the reader's highest degree, so `needs: {edu: 3, rank: 1}` and `needs: {koreanEdu: 4, stem: true}` (F-2-7S) are judged against the wrong degree too. The mirror-image effect also violates engine.md's "an unanswered detail NEVER blocks": because the checkbox has no "not set" state, merely answering the education question sets `koreanEdu = 0` and `stem = false` as DEFINITE negatives, so a D-10-1 holder who states "master's" without ticking the boxes loses F-2-7S (`BLOCKED [Education doesn't match]`) while a reader who answers nothing at all keeps it open.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:104-115 (profileFacts), :237-243 (readDetails), layouts/visa-map.html:200-221 (one `edu` select plus two bare checkboxes, no "level obtained in Korea" field); data/visamap.yaml F-5-15 `needs: {koreanEdu: 5}`, F-2-7S `needs: {koreanEdu: 4, stem: true}`
- **fix**: Add a separate question to layouts/visa-map.html next to `#vm-edu`: `<select class="vm-input" id="vm-korean-edu" name="koreanEdu">` with the same `vm_edu_0..5` options plus "not set"; read it in `readDetails` as `koreanEdu: level('koreanEdu')` and add `'koreanEdu'` to the `SELECTS` list at visamap.js:323; then change visamap.js:110 to `known.koreanEdu = person.koreanEdu;` and drop the `eduKorea` checkbox (or keep it only as the "was it in Korea" hint for `stem`/`rank`). Keep `known.stem`/`known.rank` undefined when the reader did not tick/choose them, i.e. change visamap.js:111 to `if (person.stem) known.stem = true;` and visamap.js:241-242 to leave `stem`/`rank` undefined instead of `false`/`undefined`-by-reset.

### [medium] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:129 (factsAt, `previous`) and :136 (checkFact, notAfter) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: `notAfter` is evaluated against `path[level - 2]`, i.e. literally one step back, so a detour through a sibling sub-status of the same status launders the restriction: the engine offers F-2-R to an ex-E-9 holder as soon as they route D-10-1 → D-10-2 → D-10-1.
- **evidence**: In the real built bundle (UZ, age 30, current visa E-9) the direct route is correctly refused: at row 2 from D-10-1, F-2-R is `BLOCKED [Not possible right after your previous visa]`. I then clicked D-10-2 (D-10-1's own next list offers it) and D-10-1 again (D-10-2 → D-10-1 is also an edge); at row 4 the identical move is `OPEN` with no warning, because `facts.previous` is now D-10-2 instead of E-9. The data's own note on that move says the bar is about the status before the D-10 period: "Not possible if your visa before D-10 was D-3, D-4, E-6-2, E-8, E-9, E-10, G-1 or H-1" (data/visamap.yaml:527-528), so D-10-2 must not reset it. Secondary effect of the same line: at level 1 `previous` is `undefined` → `checkFact` returns 'maybe', so for the single most common case (the reader starts on the D-10-1 they already hold) the gate never fires at all and the tool asks nothing about the prior status.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:129, :136; data/visamap.yaml:521-528 (D-10-1 → F-2-R move, `needs: {notAfter: [D-3, D-4, E-6-2, E-8, E-9, E-10, G-1, H-1]}` and its note)
- **fix**: Import `statusOf` (already exported by assets/js/visa/shared.js:9) and replace the `previous` computation in `factsAt` (visamap.js:129) with the last earlier step whose two-part status differs from the status this move leaves from: `const from = path[level - 1]; let i = level - 2; while (i >= 0 && statusOf(path[i]) === statusOf(from)) i -= 1; return { known, min, previous: i >= 0 ? path[i] : undefined };`

### [medium] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:176-182 (assess, age gate) and :134 (checkFact, ageUnder) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: Every age gate - `visa.age`, `visa.ageByCountry` and the `ageUnder` fact - is tested against the reader's age TODAY at every depth of the path, as if the whole multi-year plan happened instantly. The result is that the tool's own goal is unreachable for every reader aged 14-18, and age-capped routes are offered to readers who will have aged out by the time they get there.
- **evidence**: `const age = (visa.ageByCountry && visa.ageByCountry[person.nationality]) || visa.age; ... if (person.age < min || (max && person.age > max))` uses `person.age` with no reference to `level`; `checkFact`'s `ageUnder` branch does the same. In the harness, a 17-year-old UZ reader with no visa who walks NONE → D-2-2 (a 4-year Korean bachelor's) → D-10-1 → E-7-1 is told at row 4 that F-5-1 is `BLOCKED [Ages 19+ only]`, even though that step is ~8 years away and the form accepts ages from 14. So no 14-18-year-old can ever reach F-5, which is the entire point of the map (`isGoal`, visamap.js:46 of engine.md). The converse over-offer: D-10-1's `{ageUnder: 25, koreanEdu: 1}` group and E-7-1's `{ageUnder: 30, countries: asiaPilot, edu: 3, stem: true}` group are evaluated at today's age, so a 24-year-old just starting a Korean bachelor's and a 29-year-old starting a master's are credited with tracks that will have expired at graduation (the 29-year-old VN STEM reader gets `OPEN [✓ Matches your details]` on E-7-1 at row 2).
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:176-182, :134; data/visamap.yaml F-5-1 `age: [19]`, H-1 `age: [18, 30]` + `ageByCountry`, D-10-1 `needs: [..., {ageUnder: 25, koreanEdu: 1}]`, E-7-1 `needs: [..., {ageUnder: 30, countries: asiaPilot, edu: 3, stem: true}]`; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-3 영주(F-5) (the 19-or-over requirement the data encodes)
- **fix**: Add a typical duration to each visa in data/visamap.yaml (`years: 4` on D-2-2, `years: 2` on D-2-3, `years: 1` on D-10-1, etc.), carry it through layouts/visa-map.html:68 style (`{{- with $v.years }}{{ $e = merge $e (dict "years" .) }}{{ end }}`), and in assets/js/visamap.js compute `const ageAt = (level) => person.age + path.slice(0, level).reduce((s, c) => s + ((visas[c] && visas[c].years) || 0), 0);`. Use `ageAt(level)` instead of `person.age` in `assess` (:179) and pass it into `factsAt`/`checkFact` for the `ageUnder` branch (:134). Minimal interim fix: for `level > 1`, do not push `T.ageOnly` as a blocking reason - add it as a non-blocking tag instead - so a future step is never hidden on the strength of today's age.

### [low] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:155-158 (checkNeeds, `best` / `keys`) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: When every alternative requirement group fails, `checkNeeds` reports the reasons of only one group (the first with the fewest misses), so a blocked option tells the reader the wrong thing about what is missing and hides the alternative route that would actually be easiest for them.
- **evidence**: `options.reduce((a, b) => (b.misses < a.misses ? b : a))` keeps the first group on a tie, and `keys` is then taken from that one group only. E-7-1's requirements are six independent groups: `[{edu:4},{edu:3,experience:1},{experience:5},{koreanEdu:2},{edu:3,rank:1},{ageUnder:30,countries:asiaPilot,edu:3,stem:true}]`. In the harness a UZ reader (35, no visa, edu = high school, experience = 0 years) sees E-7-1 `BLOCKED [Education doesn't match]` and nothing else, although `{experience: 5}` is a complete, education-free route to the same visa and is the one most such readers can actually reach. The block itself is right; the stated reason is not, and it is the only thing the reader is shown.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:148-159 (checkNeeds), :189 (assess pushes `T[WHY[key]]` for each reported key); data/visamap.yaml E-7-1 `needs:` list
- **fix**: In assets/js/visamap.js:157-158, when the overall result is 'no' report the union of every group's failing keys instead of one group's: `const keys = best.result === 'no' ? [...new Set(options.flatMap((o) => o.checks.filter((c) => c.result === 'no').map((c) => c.key)))] : best.checks.filter((c) => c.result === best.result).map((c) => c.key); return { result: best.result, keys };` (the `[...new Set(reasons)]` dedup already in `assess` keeps the tag list short).

### [low] engine-bug: assets/js/visamap.js:121-123 (factsAt, non-level `gives` branch) -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: For non-level facts (`spouse`, `koreanChild`, `korean`) a visa's `gives` can only fill a gap - `if (known[key] === undefined) known[key] = value` - never correct an answer that describes the reader's situation today. Because the path describes the future, this makes the family-based routes impossible to model for anyone who answers the family questions, and it silently neutralises the obvious data fix.
- **evidence**: The level branch on the next line uses `Math.max`, so a path-implied degree can raise a stated degree, but the non-level branch cannot. Consequence: F-6-1 → F-5-2 carries `needs: {spouse: korean}` (data/visamap.yaml) and no visa in the data has `gives: {spouse: ...}`, so the marriage → PR route depends entirely on the reader leaving `spouse` blank. If a maintainer adds the natural fix `gives: {spouse: korean}` to F-6-1 (and `{spouse: exKorean}` to F-6-3), it will still have no effect for any reader who answered the spouse question, because their answer wins forever. I could not produce a currently-visible wrong recommendation from this, since F-6-1's own `needs: {spouse: korean}` already blocks spouse=none readers one step earlier - so this is a latent defect that will defeat the data fix rather than a live misrecommendation.
- **citation**: assets/js/visamap.js:120-126 (factsAt); scratchpad engine.md section "gives"; data/visamap.yaml F-6-1 (`needs: {spouse: korean}`, no `gives`) and its `next: - {to: F-5-2, needs: {spouse: korean}}`
- **fix**: In assets/js/visamap.js add `const ESTABLISHES = ['spouse', 'koreanChild', 'korean'];` beside `LEVELS` (:90) and change the non-level branch at :122 to `if (known[key] === undefined || ESTABLISHES.includes(key)) known[key] = value;`, then add `gives: {spouse: korean}` to F-6-1 and `gives: {spouse: exKorean}` to F-6-3 in data/visamap.yaml.

## graph / f-family

### [high] wrong-note: F-3 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.9)
- **claim**: F-3's 'how' says the sponsor can be any 'D, E or F-2 status holder'. That is wrong in both directions: D-3, E-8, E-9, E-10 holders and F-2 '타목' family-category holders cannot bring F-3 dependants at all, while F-4 and H-2 holders can.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제25호 동반(F-3) reads: '문화예술(D-1), 유학(D-2), 일반연수(D-4)부터 특정활동(E-7)까지, 거주(F-2), 재외동포(F-4) 및 방문취업(H-2)의 체류자격에 해당하는 사람의 배우자 및 미성년 자녀로서 배우자가 없는 사람. 다만, 거주(F-2)의 체류자격 중 타목의 체류자격에 해당하는 사람은 제외한다.' In the table's own numbering D-4 is item 7 and E-7 is item 20, so D-3 (item 6, 기술연수), E-8 (21, 계절근로), E-9 (22, 비전문취업) and E-10 (23, 선원취업) all fall outside the range. The repo's own ground truth agrees: data/visadocs.yaml F-3 > abroad case 1 is 'Spouse or unmarried minor child of a D-1, D-2, D-4–E-7, F-2, F-4 or H-2 holder' (사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 31. 동반(F-3) › 공관장 재량). This is a recommendation error, not only a wording one: F-3 is offered from NONE on `needs: [{spouse: foreigner},{parent: foreigner}]` alone, so the spouse of an E-9 EPS worker (a very large share of the site's Uzbek/Asian audience) is shown F-3 as an open first visa and the 'how' text confirms it, although E-9 holders may not bring family.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제25호 동반(F-3), 대통령령 제36728호 시행 2026.10.2 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 31. 동반(F-3) › 공관장 재량으로 발급할 수 있는 사증
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml line 1300: replace the F-3 `how.en` text with 'Spouse or unmarried minor child of a holder of D-1, D-2, D-4 to E-7, F-2 (except the F-2 family sub-categories), F-4 or H-2. Holders of D-3, E-8, E-9 and E-10 cannot bring dependants.' and update the ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings to match.

### [high] missing-edge: E-9 / D-2-x / E-7-x / F-3 (every status that already offers F-6-1 in Korea) -> F-6-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: F-6-2 (parent raising a Korean child) is offered from only two places in the whole graph - NONE and F-6-1 - and the F-6-1 edge is always blocked for the very readers standing on F-6-1, so a foreigner already in Korea on any other status who has a Korean child can never be shown F-6-2.
- **evidence**: The KIS sojourn manual documents the F-6-2 change of status explicitly for people on OTHER statuses: data/visadocs.yaml F-6-2 > change has exactly one case, 'Parent on a status other than F-6 who is raising in Korea a minor child born of a (de facto) marriage with a Korean national', sourced to 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 33. 결혼이민(F-6) > 자녀 양육자에 대한 체류자격(F-6-2) 변경허가 > 다. 제출서류. The decree row imposes no prior-status condition either. visadocs-targets.txt lists F-6-2 as change,abroad,extend, so an in-Korea change is documented. In visamap.yaml the only in-edges are line 1325 (F-6-1 -> F-6-2) and line 1535 (NONE -> F-6-2). Worse, F-6-2's visa-level needs is {koreanChild: true, spouse: [exKorean, none]}, while F-6-1's needs is {spouse: korean}; per engine.md needs are ALL-must-hold and the F-6-1 -> F-6-2 move has no own needs, so any reader who answered 'Married to a Korean citizen' (the only way to be open on F-6-1) is evaluated as spouse='korean', checkFact returns 'no', and F-6-2 is pushed into the hidden 'blocked' bucket. Net effect: ~30 statuses offer F-6-1 (D-1, D-2-1..D-2-8, D-4-1/2/7, D-5, D-6, D-7, D-10-1, E-1..E-7-*, E-9, E-10, F-1, F-3) and none of them offer F-6-2.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 자녀 양육자에 대한 체류자격(F-6-2) 변경허가 › 다. 제출서류 (as recorded in data/visadocs.yaml F-6-2.change); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) 나목, 대통령령 제36728호 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, add `- {to: F-6-2, needs: {koreanChild: true}}` immediately after the existing `- F-6-1` entry in the `next:` list of every status that currently offers F-6-1 as an in-Korea change (D-1, D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4, D-2-5, D-2-6, D-2-7, D-2-8, D-4-1, D-4-2, D-4-7, D-5, D-6, D-7, D-10-1, E-1, E-2, E-3, E-4, E-5, E-6-1, E-6-2, E-6-3, E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3, E-7-4, E-7-4R, E-7-S1, E-7-S2, E-7-T, E-7-Y, E-9, E-10, F-1, F-3). The per-move `needs: {koreanChild: true}` is required so it REPLACES the visa-level needs and does not demand a dissolved marriage. Also change line 1325 from `- F-6-2` to `- {to: F-6-2, needs: {koreanChild: true}}` so it stops being auto-blocked for F-6-1 holders.

### [medium] wrong-note: F-6-2 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: F-6-2's 'how' states that the status is for after the marriage to a Korean citizen has ended. The decree requires no dissolution: it covers any parent raising a child born of a marriage OR de-facto relationship with a Korean national.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) 나목: '국민과 혼인관계(사실상의 혼인관계를 포함한다)에서 출생한 자녀를 양육하고 있는 부 또는 모로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람'. There is no 'after the marriage ended' element; the explicit inclusion of 사실상의 혼인관계 means a parent who was never legally married qualifies. The manual's own framing is prior-status based, not marriage-status based: data/visadocs.yaml F-6-2.change case = 'Parent on a status other than F-6 who is raising in Korea a minor child born of a (de facto) marriage with a Korean national'. The data's needs `spouse: [exKorean, none]` encodes the same incorrect assumption that the relationship must be over.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) 나목, 대통령령 제36728호 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 자녀 양육자에 대한 체류자격(F-6-2) 변경허가
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml line 1334: replace F-6-2 `how.en` with 'Parent raising in Korea a minor child born of a marriage, or of a de-facto relationship, with a Korean citizen. Open from any status other than F-6; the relationship does not have to have ended, but while you are legally married you normally stay on F-6-1.' (and the other languages). Keep `needs: {koreanChild: true}` at visa level and drop `spouse: [exKorean, none]`, or move the spouse condition onto the single F-6-1 -> F-6-2 move only.

### [medium] wrong-note: F-3 -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: The note on F-3 -> F-2-99 says 'For the spouse of an F-2-99 holder (via F-3-18)'. Being the spouse of an F-2-99 holder does not give F-2-99; it gives F-3-18. F-2-99 is earned in the applicant's own right after 5+ years of continuous residence.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 거주(F-2) 바목 (the row behind F-2-99): '외교(A-1)부터 협정(A-3)까지의 체류자격 외의 체류자격으로 대한민국에 5년 이상 계속 체류하여 생활 근거지가 국내에 있는 사람으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람' - a personal residence test, with no family-of-F-2-99 branch. The repo's ground truth matches: data/visadocs.yaml F-2-99.change has exactly one case, 'Other long-term resident (F-2 item 바: 5+ years in Korea with a life base here)', while the F-2-99 holder's family appears in F-3's change list as 'Spouse or minor child of an F-2-99 holder (F-3-18)' sourced to 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 9. 기타 장기체류자 › 라. 동반가족 › 체류자격 변경. So the manual sends the family to F-3-18 and keeps F-2-99 for the person's own 5 years. The edge itself is fine (an F-3 holder's own years count under 바목); only the note is wrong, and it misdirects readers into thinking the sponsor's status is what qualifies them.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 거주(F-2) 바목 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 9. 기타 장기체류자에 대한 거주 자격 변경허가 (+ 라. 동반가족)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml line 1308: replace the note on the F-3 -> F-2-99 entry with 'In your own right after 5+ years of continuous stay in Korea on any long-term status (A-1 to A-3 excluded), with your life base here plus the income and KIIP conditions. Being the spouse of an F-2-99 holder only gives F-3-18.' (all four languages).

### [medium] missing-edge: F-4-R -> F-5-6

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: F-4-R's only outgoing edge is F-5-6R (4 years in the region, income 70% of GNI, KIIP 5). The ordinary overseas-Korean PR route F-5-6 (2 years on F-4, no KIIP) is also open to an F-4-R holder and is far easier, but the tool can never offer it.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제4호: '별표 1의2 중 26. 재외동포(F-4) 체류자격으로 대한민국에 2년 이상 계속 체류하고 있는 사람으로서 대한민국에 계속 거주할 필요가 있다고 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람' - the test is the 재외동포(F-4) status, and F-4-R is a sub-code of that same 체류자격 (the repo's own within-list records 'F-4>F-4-R: use=change' and 'F-4-R>F-4: use=change', i.e. they are two sub-codes of one status that can be swapped by a status change). data/visadocs.yaml F-5-6 requires only 2 years plus one of income >= 1x GNI / assets / trade / investment / pension at 60+, and carries no KIIP document, whereas F-5-6R needs 4 years in the region and KIIP level 5. So routing every F-4-R holder exclusively to F-5-6R hides the quicker goal. The reverse move F-4-R -> F-4 is likewise documented in visadocs' within-list but absent from visamap.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제4호, 대통령령 제36728호 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 36. 외국국적동포 관련 › 5. 외국국적동포 영주자격(F-5) 부여 세부절차 (F-5-6); 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅲ (F-4-R)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml line 1353: change `next: [F-5-6R]` to `next: [F-5-6R, F-5-6, F-4]`, adding a note on the F-5-6 entry such as 'Your F-4-R years count as F-4 time: 2+ years plus the income option, no KIIP needed.'

### [medium] missing-edge: F-1 -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: F-1 does not offer F-2-99 although F-3 does, even though the decree row behind F-2-99 counts years on any long-term status other than A-1 to A-3, which includes F-1.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 거주(F-2) 바목 counts '외교(A-1)부터 협정(A-3)까지의 체류자격 외의 체류자격으로 대한민국에 5년 이상 계속 체류' - F-1 (방문동거, 별표 1의2 제23호) is such a status, exactly like F-3 (제25호). F-1 sub-codes are long-stay, repeatedly extendable statuses (visadocs F-1 > extend documents F-1-5 parents of marriage migrants, F-1-11 family of F-2-7 holders, accompanying parents of school-age students, etc.), so 5+ years on F-1 is a common real situation. The graph is internally inconsistent: F-3 -> F-2-99 exists (line 1307) but F-1 -> F-2-99 does not. Note the visa-level description of F-2-99 ('5+ years on eligible work or business statuses') contradicts the decree and would also invalidate the existing F-3 edge, so one of the two must be corrected.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제23호 방문동거(F-1) and 제24호 거주(F-2) 바목, 대통령령 제36728호 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=XML); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 9. 기타 장기체류자
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, F-1's `next:` (lines 1289-1297): add `- to: F-2-99` with the note 'After 5+ years of continuous stay in Korea, with your life base here plus the income and KIIP conditions.' Alternatively, if the stricter reading is intended, also drop the F-3 -> F-2-99 entry (line 1307) and fix F-2-99's `how` so the two agree.

### [low] wrong-note: F-4 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-4's 'how' defines the holder as a 'former Korean citizen or descendant'. The statutory definition is narrower: the nationality must have been held BY BIRTH, so a naturalised Korean who later gave up the nationality, and their descendants, are not overseas Koreans.
- **evidence**: 재외동포의 출입국과 법적 지위에 관한 법률 시행령 제3조 (전문개정 2019.7.2): '법 제2조제2호에서 "대통령령으로 정하는 자"란 다음 각 호의 어느 하나에 해당하는 사람을 말한다. 1. 출생에 의하여 대한민국의 국적을 보유했던 사람(대한민국정부 수립 전에 국외로 이주한 동포를 포함한다)으로서 외국국적을 취득한 사람 2. 제1호에 해당하는 사람의 직계비속으로서 외국국적을 취득한 사람'. The repo's own ground truth already uses the narrower wording: data/visadocs.yaml F-4 change/abroad cases are 'Former Korean national BY BIRTH who acquired foreign nationality (F-4-41)' and 'Descendant of a former Korean national ...' Because F-4's only gate is the self-reported `korean: true` fact, the loose definition lets the tool present F-4 (and the F-5-6/F-5-7 goals behind it) to people who are not 외국국적동포. The 41-years-old military-service sentence in the same note is correct (재외동포법 제5조제2항 단서: '41세가 되는 해 1월 1일부터').
- **citation**: 재외동포의 출입국과 법적 지위에 관한 법률 시행령 제3조 (https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=284843&type=XML); 재외동포법 제5조제2항
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml line 1346: change the opening of F-4 `how.en` from 'Ethnic Korean of any nationality: former Korean citizen or descendant.' to 'Ethnic Korean of any nationality: someone who held Korean nationality by birth and then took foreign nationality, or their direct descendant. Nationality gained by naturalisation does not count.' (and the ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings).

### [low] missing-edge: F-6-1 -> F-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: F-1 has in-degree 0 in the whole graph. The one real route into it that matters to this slice is missing: a marriage migrant whose marriage ends but who qualifies for neither F-6-2 nor F-6-3 is moved to 가사정리 방문동거 (F-1-6), and the tool never shows that.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml F-1 > extend includes the case 'Former marriage migrant settling household affairs after the marriage ended (F-1-6)', and its ref records that the same document list appears twice in the manual: '외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 29. 방문동거(F-1) › 체류기간 연장허가 › 5. 혼인단절 결혼이민자 가사정리를 위한 방문동거 체류기간 연장허가' and '33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 가사정리(F-1-6) 체류기간 연장허가'. Its presence inside the F-6 chapter is exactly the manual documenting F-1-6 as the onward status for a 혼인단절 F-6 holder. Also supported by 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제23호 방문동거(F-1) 가목, which lists '가사정리' as a purpose of the status. Today an F-6-1 reader whose marriage ended and who has no Korean child and cannot prove the spouse's fault sees only F-5-2/F-6-2/F-6-3, all blocked, and no onward status at all. (Caveat: visadocs-targets.txt gives F-1 only an 'extend' list, so the repo's convention would need the entry treated as a status grant rather than a documented 'change'.)
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 가사정리(F-1-6) 체류기간 연장허가, and › 29. 방문동거(F-1) › 체류기간 연장허가 › 5. 혼인단절 결혼이민자 가사정리; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제23호 방문동거(F-1) 가목
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, F-6-1 `next:` (after line 1326) and F-6-3 `next:` (line 1342): add `- to: F-1` with a note such as 'If the marriage ends and you qualify for neither F-6-2 nor F-6-3: short visiting status (F-1-6) to settle household affairs before leaving.'
