### [high] wrong-age: F-2-2 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.82)
- **claim**: F-2-2 is hard-gated to ages 0-18 with `needs: {parent: korean}`, which hides it from every adult, but one of the two F-2-2 grounds (a person born of a marriage, including a de facto marriage, with a Korean national) has no minority requirement at all.
- **evidence**: Decree table 1-2, item 24 거주(F-2) has two separate grounds: 가목 "국민의 미성년 외국인 자녀" (minor, feeds F-2-2 and F-5-3) and 나목 "국민과 혼인관계(사실상의 혼인관계를 포함한다)에서 출생한 사람으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람" - no age limit. The repo's own data carries both as F-2-2: visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-2'].change case is "(1) A Korean national's minor foreign child; (2) a child born of a marriage (incl. de facto) with a Korean national", and the abroad list has a separate, stronger variant for case (2) ("5-year multiple-entry F-2-2 visa, stay up to 1 year") versus case (1) ("single-entry, 90 days"); case (1)'s doc list requires 친권자 동의 while case (2)'s does not. In assets/js/visamap.js `assess()` the `age` array is a hard block (pushes a reason), and `profileFacts()` sets `known.parent = 'none'` for any reader aged 19+, so `needs: {parent: korean}` also resolves to 'no' (a hard block) rather than 'maybe' for adults. Result: F-2-2 is unreachable for anyone 19 or older, twice over.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 가목 and 나목 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML ; 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 공관장 재량으로 발급할 수 있는 사증 › 3.
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under `F-2-2:` delete `age: [0, 18]` and delete `needs: {parent: korean}` (keeping `parent: korean` would still hard-block adults because the engine fabricates `parent: 'none'` at age 19+). Move the minority condition into `how`/a note, e.g. "Minor foreign child of a Korean citizen, or any person born of a marriage (incl. de facto) with a Korean citizen - the second ground has no age limit". Also see the F-5-1 finding: an adult F-2-2 holder currently has no onward step at all, since F-5-3 is itself gated to 0-18.

### [medium] wrong-note: F-2-R -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: F-2-R's `how` lists "5 years in a depopulating area" as if it were an entry requirement; in the governing notice the five years is a forward-looking commitment that starts on the day the F-2-R status is granted, so the tool makes the regional programme look closed to people who actually qualify for it today.
- **evidence**: 법무부고시 제2026-248호 제4조제1항 lists the requirements: (1) a recommendation letter from the head of the 광역지방자치단체; (2) a Korean 전문학사 or higher degree, OR annual income at or above that metropolitan government's 생활임금; (3) "지역특화형 우수인재(F-2-R) 체류자격을 받은 날로부터 5년 이상 추천지역에서 거주하거나 취업 또는 창업활동을 하고자 할 것" - an intention to live/work/run a business in the recommended area for 5+ years FROM the date the status is granted; (4) KIIP stage 3+ (incl. a pre-test placement of stage 4+) or TOPIK level 3+. I read the full notice text from the attached PDF. Items (1), (2) and (4) in the visamap `how` are correct; only the 5-year clause is inverted. Also, 제2조제1호/제3조 make the target area 인구감소지역 AND 인구감소관심지역 inside participating metropolitan jurisdictions, not just "a depopulating area".
- **citation**: 법무부고시 제2026-248호, 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 파목에 관한 기준 고시, 제4조제1항제3호 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000283176&type=HTML (full text: http://law.go.kr/flDownload.do?flSeq=167486017)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml rewrite `F-2-R.how.en` to end with a commitment rather than a prerequisite, e.g. "Local-government recommendation, a Korean associate degree or income at the metropolitan living wage, KIIP 3 or TOPIK 3, and a commitment to live, work or run a business in the recommended depopulating area for 5 years after you get the status." Mirror the change in the ru / uz / uz-cyrl strings.

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-3 -> F-5-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: F-5-1 is offered only from F-2-5, F-2-7, F-2-7S, F-2-99 and F-2-R, but the decree counts time on ANY 거주(F-2) sub-code toward it, so the general 5-year PR route is invisible from 11 of the 16 F-2 sub-codes.
- **evidence**: 별표 1의3 제1호: "대한민국 「민법」에 따른 성년으로서 별표 1의2 중 10. 주재(D-7)부터 20. 특정활동(E-7)까지의 체류자격이나 별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 체류자격으로 5년 이상 대한민국에 체류하고 있는 사람". The reference is to item 24 거주(F-2) as a whole, with no sub-code restriction. The visamap already carries exactly this note ("After 5+ years in work or business statuses (D-7-E-7, F-2)...") on five F-2 nodes, so the omission on the rest is an inconsistency, not a deliberate simplification. It matters most for F-2-2 (adult 나목 holders, whose only other step F-5-3 is age-gated 0-18), F-2-8, F-2-12, F-2-71, F-2-T, F-2-13, F-2-81 and F-2-T1, several of which currently have exactly one onward step.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제1호 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add the existing `- to: F-5-1` entry (with the same shared note used on F-2-5/F-2-7/F-2-7S/F-2-99/F-2-R, e.g. a YAML anchor) to the `next:` lists of F-2-2, F-2-3, F-2-4, F-2-8, F-2-12, F-2-13, F-2-16, F-2-71, F-2-81, F-2-T and F-2-T1.

### [medium] wrong-needs: F-2-T -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: F-2-T's `needs` is a two-branch OR (`[{edu: 4, rank: 3}, {experience: 5}]`), so a bare top-100 master's, or 5 years of experience alone, opens Top-Tier; the notice's criteria are conjunctive and the career bar is 8 years for the advanced-industry track.
- **evidence**: 법무부고시 제2026-160호, [첨단산업 분야] 가. 자격 기준 requires ALL of: 1. a master's or doctorate from a university in the world top 100; 2. either (i) 8+ years of advanced-field work experience including 3+ years at a global-500 company, or (ii) 5+ years of post-doctoral research experience including 3+ years at a government-led institute / top-100 university institute / global-500 corporate institute; 3. annual income >= 3x per-capita GNI; 4. verification and recommendation by the Minister of Trade, Industry. 나. 대상자 only relaxes one leg at a time and raises the price: dropping the degree OR the career leg requires income >= 4x GNI; dropping income requires 1+ year of advanced-field employment in Korea with income >= 2x GNI. The science-and-technology track needs 5+ years plus a PI / assistant-professor-or-above or 책임급 position and a 과기정통부 recommendation. Under engine.md a list `needs` passes if ANY one group passes, so each visamap branch on its own is far below every published combination.
- **citation**: 법무부고시 제2026-160호, 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 자목에 해당하는 사람 중 첨단산업 및 과학기술 분야 최우수인재(Top-Tier) 자격 기준과 대상에 관한 고시 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000279596&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `F-2-T: needs:` to conjunctive groups, e.g. `needs: [{edu: 4, rank: 3, experience: 8}, {edu: 5, rank: 3, experience: 5}]` (top-100 master's plus 8 years, or top-100 doctorate plus 5 years of research). Keep the income and ministry-recommendation conditions in `how`, since there is no fact for either.

### [medium] wrong-needs: F-2-5 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: F-2-5 is gated at `invest: 2`, which the UI labels "₩300M+ (about US$220K)", below the statutory floor of US$300,000, so the tool shows the high-amount investor status as open (with a "matches your details" tick) to people who cannot get it.
- **evidence**: Decree table 1-2, item 24 거주(F-2) 라목 gives exactly three grounds: 1) US$500,000+ invested and 3+ continuous years on D-8; 2) an executive/employee posted by a foreign company that invested US$500,000+, 3+ continuous years; 3) US$300,000+ invested and employing 2+ Korean nationals. The lowest figure is US$300,000. i18n/en.yaml maps vm_invest_2 to "₩300M+ (about US$220K)" and vm_invest_3 to "₩700M+ (about US$500K)"; assets/js/visamap.js `checkFact` compares `have >= want` for level facts, so answering level 2 satisfies `{invest: 2}` and the option is rendered open plus a PERSONAL "matches" tag.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 라목 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `F-2-5: needs: {invest: 2}` to `needs: {invest: 3}` (₩700M ~ US$500K, the 라목 1)/2) threshold). US$300,000 falls between levels 2 and 3 and cannot be expressed, so note the cheaper 2-employee route in `how` instead of loosening the gate.

### [medium] unreachable: F-2-16 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: F-2-16 has no in-edges at all, so the special-contributor residence status and both of its PR goals (F-5-28, F-5-29) are dead nodes the tool can never show.
- **evidence**: F-2-16 is a real current category: Decree table 1-2, item 24 거주(F-2) 카목 is "법무부장관이 대한민국에 특별한 기여를 했거나 공익의 증진에 이바지했다고 인정하는 사람", and 별표 1의3 제19호 grants PR after "별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 카목에 해당하는 체류자격으로 2년 이상" - which is exactly the visamap's F-5-28 ("2+ years on F-2-16"). I computed in-degrees across the whole graph: F-2-16 is 0, and F-5-28 / F-5-29 are reachable only from F-2-16. 카목 is a discretionary Ministry of Justice grant with no source-status restriction, which is also why visadocs.yaml has no `change` list for it.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 카목 and 별표 1의3 제19호 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml either make F-2-16 reachable - add `- to: F-2-16` with a note such as "Granted at the Ministry of Justice's discretion for a special contribution; no fixed application route" to `NONE: next:` and to a few long-term statuses (E-1, E-3, E-7-1, F-2-99) - or delete the F-2-16, F-5-28 and F-5-29 nodes so the graph has no dead branch.

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-7S -> F-5-16

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.68)
- **claim**: F-2-7S and F-2-T lead only to their programme-specific PR codes (F-5-S1, F-5-T), but both are 자목 statuses, so the ordinary points-based PR route F-5-16 (3 years on 자목) is also open to them and the tool can never suggest it.
- **evidence**: 별표 1의3 제13호 grants PR to "별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 자목에 해당하는 체류자격으로 대한민국에서 3년 이상 체류하고 있는 사람" - i.e. any 자목 holder, not just the F-2-7 sub-code. 법무부고시 제2025-408호 제2조⑤ puts 잠재적 우수인재 (master's/doctorate from the 이공계 특성화 대학·연구기관 in 붙임 1, which is what the map calls F-2-7S) among the 자목 대상자, and the Top-Tier notice is titled "...별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2) 자목에 해당하는 사람 중 ... 최우수인재(Top-Tier)...", so F-2-T is 자목 too. F-5-16 is a genuine alternative rather than a duplicate: it needs 2x GNI income and KIIP 5, whereas F-5-S1 needs 90/200 K-STAR points and F-5-T needs 3x GNI income.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제13호 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML ; 법무부고시 제2025-408호 제2조⑤ - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000266296&type=HTML ; 법무부고시 제2026-160호 (title) - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000279596&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-5-16` to `F-2-7S: next:` and to `F-2-T: next:`, and extend F-5-16's `how` from "3+ years on F-2-7" to "3+ years on a points-based F-2 (F-2-7, F-2-7S or F-2-T)".

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-7 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: No F-2 sub-code offers any lateral move to F-2-R, F-2-7S or F-2-T even though the repo's own manual-derived data records these in-Korea changes from every F-2 sub-code.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml `within` (the manual's documented intra-status moves) contains F-2-2>F-2-R, F-2-3>F-2-R, F-2-4>F-2-R, F-2-5>F-2-R, F-2-7>F-2-R, F-2-7>F-2-7S, F-2-7S>F-2-R, F-2-8>F-2-R, F-2-12>F-2-R, F-2-71>F-2-R, F-2-81>F-2-R, F-2-99>F-2-R, F-2-R>F-2-7S, F-2-T>F-2-R, F-2-T1>F-2-R (and the matching >F-2-7S pairs), each with `use: change`. Only one of these, F-2-7>F-2-T, is present in visamap.yaml; the other 29 pairs exist in the document data but can never be reached because no F-2 node's `next` lists them. The legal basis is permissive: 법무부고시 제2026-248호 제4조 sets no condition on the applicant's current status (visadocs' case text is "Lawful resident in Korea applying for regional talent (F-2-R)"), and the 자목 notice likewise restricts tracks, not the prior F-2 sub-code.
- **citation**: data/visadocs.yaml `within` (derived from 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ › (1) 체류자격 변경 and › 41. K-STAR 비자트랙 › Ⅱ); 법무부고시 제2026-248호 제4조 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000283176&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-2-R` to the `next:` lists of F-2-3, F-2-7, F-2-7S, F-2-99 and F-2-T at minimum (the highest-traffic sources), and `- F-2-7S` to F-2-7's `next`. Guard F-2-R with the same `needs: {notAfter: [...]}` already used on the D-10-1 -> F-2-R edge if the regional manual excludes prior statuses.

### [low] missing-edge: F-2-12 -> F-5-25

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-5-25 (conditional large-investor PR) is offered from B-1, B-2, C-3 and D-8 but not from F-2-12, although it is the natural top-up route for someone already in the public-interest investment programme.
- **evidence**: 공익사업 투자이민제도 투자대상, 투자금액 등에 관한 기준 고시 (법무부고시 제2023-319호) Ⅲ.1 sets the 별표 1의3 제16호 target group as "공익사업 투자이민제도 투자대상 중 원금보장·무이자형에 30억 원 이상을 투자하고 5년 이상 투자 상태를 유지하고자 하는 자와 그 배우자 및 미혼자녀" - the same 투자대상 (Korea Development Bank principal-guaranteed deposit) that F-2-12 holders are already invested in under Ⅱ, at ₩1.5bn, with no restriction on current status. The notice's Ⅱ.2 even allows amounts in the two investment-immigration schemes to be added together. The visamap's F-2-12 `next` contains only F-5-21.
- **citation**: 법무부고시 제2023-319호, 공익사업 투자이민제도 투자대상, 투자금액 등에 관한 기준 고시, Ⅱ.2 and Ⅲ.1 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000225532&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- to: F-5-25` to `F-2-12: next:` with `note: {en: If you top the deposit up to ₩3bn and pledge to keep it for 5 years., ...}`. F-5-25 already carries `needs: {invest: 6}`, so the ₩3bn condition stays enforced.

### [low] missing-edge: F-2-99 -> F-5-10

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: F-5-10 is offered only from E-1/E-2/E-3/E-5/E-7-1/E-7-2/E-7-3, but its statutory ground counts total time in Korea on any status, so F-2-99 and F-2-7 holders are hidden from a PR route whose income bar is lower than the one they are shown.
- **evidence**: 별표 1의3 제8호: "법무부장관이 정하는 분야의 학사 학위 이상의 학위증 또는 법무부장관이 정하는 기술자격증이 있는 사람으로서 국내 체류기간이 3년 이상이고, 영주자격(F-5) 신청 시 국내기업에 고용되어 법무부장관이 정하는 금액 이상의 임금을 받는 사람" - the three-year clock is 국내 체류기간 (time in Korea), with no status restriction. The visamap's own F-5-10 `how` sets the income bar at 1x GNI, against 2x GNI for F-5-1 (the only route F-2-99 is given besides the age-60 pension route F-5-13) and 2x GNI for F-5-16, so this is a materially easier route, not a duplicate. I could not read the manual chapter for F-5-10, so the manual may narrow the eligible statuses even though the decree does not.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=law&MST=290501&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-5-10` to `F-2-99: next:` and to `F-2-7: next:` (F-5-10 already carries `needs: [{edu: 3, stem: true}, {koreanEdu: 4}]`, so the degree condition stays enforced).

### [low] wrong-note: F-2-7S -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: F-2-7S's `how` says the points test is "waived"; the governing notice applies the 80-point threshold to every 자목 applicant, including the 잠재적 우수인재 group that F-2-7S represents.
- **evidence**: 법무부고시 제2025-408호 제2조 lists five 자목 대상자 groups, the fifth being "⑤ 잠재적 우수인재 - 이공계 특성화 대학 및 연구기관 [붙임 1]의 석·박사를 취득한 외국인" (붙임 1 is KAIST/GIST/DGIST/UNIST, the 정부출연연구기관 and 기초과학연구원 - i.e. the map's "K-STAR university" list). 제3조 then says "거주(F-2) 자목 체류자격을 부여받고자 하는 외국인은 [붙임 2]에서 정하는 각 평가항목의 합산점수 80점 이상을 충족하여야 한다", with no carve-out for group ⑤. In practice a 붙임 1 doctorate clears 80 easily (학력 25 + 우수대학 박사 가점 30 + 나이 25 = 80 before income), so "effectively always passes" would be accurate while "waived" is not. Caveat: the K-STAR track is documented in a newer chapter (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 41. K-STAR 비자트랙) that I could not read, and it may state an explicit waiver that post-dates this 2025-10-24 notice - hence the low confidence. The rest of F-2-7S (Korean science/technology master's or doctorate from a specialised institution) matches 제2조⑤, and `needs: {koreanEdu: 4, stem: true}` is right in kind.
- **citation**: 법무부고시 제2025-408호, 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 중 24. 거주(F-2)자목에 해당하는 자의 연령, 학력, 소득 등에 관한 기준 고시, 제2조⑤ and 제3조 - https://www.law.go.kr/DRF/lawService.do?OC=test&target=admrul&ID=2100000266296&type=HTML
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `F-2-7S.how.en` from "...with the president's recommendation; points waived." to "...with the president's recommendation; the 80-point test still applies but this profile clears it." Mirror in ru / uz / uz-cyrl. Verify against 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 41. K-STAR 비자트랙 › Ⅱ before changing.

## graph / f5

### [high] missing-edge: F-4 -> F-5-14

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: F-4 does not offer F-5-14, so an ethnic-Korean long-serving worker who followed the tool's own advice to move from H-2 to F-4 loses the 방문취업 장기근속자 permanent-residence route entirely; the manual explicitly counts people who changed to F-4.
- **evidence**: The KIS 동포 manual chapter on granting F-5 to 외국국적동포 states the F-5-14 condition as "방문취업(H-2) 자격으로 제조업, 농·축산업, 어업, 간병인, 가사보조인으로 취업활동을 하고 있는 사람으로서 ... 동일업체에서 근무처를 변경하지 않고 4년 이상 계속 근무하고 있는 경우 (재외동포(F-4) 자격변경자 포함)" - the parenthesis explicitly includes those who have changed to F-4 (scratchpad/dongpo.txt lines 507-512). visamap.yaml F-4 `next: [F-5-6, F-4-R, F-5-7, F-5-13]` (line 1347) has no F-5-14, while F-5-14 is reachable only from H-2, whose own note in the data says "No new H-2 visas since 2026.02.12; holders can switch to F-4". So the data routes the whole affected population into a node from which F-5-14 can never be offered.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 / 동포 맞춤형 길라잡이, 5. 외국국적동포 영주자격(F-5) 부여 세부절차 > 나. > 방문취업(H-2) 4년 이상 근속자 (F-5-14): "동일업체에서 근무처를 변경하지 않고 4년 이상 계속 근무하고 있는 경우 (재외동포(F-4) 자격변경자 포함)"; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제12호
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, visa F-4 (line 1344) change `next: [F-5-6, F-4-R, F-5-7, F-5-13]` to `next: [F-5-6, F-4-R, F-5-7, F-5-13, {to: F-5-14, note: {en: 'If you worked 4+ years without changing employer in manufacturing, farming, fishing or care work on H-2 and then changed to F-4.'}}]`.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-1 -> F-5-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: No D-10 node offers F-5-1, although 구직(D-10) is inside the status range that qualifies for 일반영주 and the KIS manual's F-5-1 document list has documents specifically for applicants who currently hold D-10.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3, 영주(F-5) item 1, counts stay under "별표 1의2 중 10.주재(D-7)부터 20.특정활동(E-7)까지의 체류자격"; in 별표 1의2 the numbered entries 10-20 are D-7, D-8, D-9, D-10(13.구직), E-1...E-7, so D-10 is inside the range (local copy: scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt and scratchpad/byl12.txt). The MOJ 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12), ch. 3 구직(D-10) and ch. 8 거주(F-2), states the rule verbatim as "(F-5, 일반영주) D-7~D-10, E-1~E-7 체류자격이나 F-2 체류자격으로 5년 이상 국내 체류" (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 483, 841). In-repo manual data confirms a D-10 applicant is contemplated: data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-1'].change carries three documents gated on "Only if you currently hold D-10" (visadocs.yaml lines 11349-11355), including "Proof of meeting the criteria of the relevant E-1 to E-7 status", sourced to 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 체류자격 변경허가 > 1. 일반 영주자[F-5-1] > 다. 제출서류. In visamap.yaml none of D-10-1/D-10-2/D-10-3/D-10-T lists any F-5 target, so a reader standing on D-10 after years of E-7 work is never shown F-5-1.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제1호 (+ 별표 1의2 제10~20호); 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 3. 구직(D-10) 유형1 3단계 및 8. 거주(F-2) 유형1 1단계; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 체류자격 변경허가 > 1. 일반 영주자[F-5-1] > 다. 제출서류 (as copied into data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-1'])
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, visa D-10-1 (line 508) add to `next`: `- {to: F-5-1, needs: {edu: 3}, note: {en: 'Only if you already have 5+ years in D-7-E-7 or F-2 statuses; the degree certificate and employment contract are required for D-10 applicants.'}}`. Do the same for D-10-2 and D-10-T if they are meant to be kept parallel to D-10-1.

### [medium] wrong-note: F-5-10 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: F-5-10's `how` says "3+ years' work in Korea"; the rule is 3+ years of *residence* in Korea plus 1+ year of full-time regular employment at a Korean company, so the note both overstates the work requirement and omits the 1-year employment condition.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 8: "국내 체류기간이 3년 이상이고, 영주자격(F-5) 신청 시 국내기업에 고용되어 법무부장관이 정하는 금액 이상의 임금을 받는 사람" - 체류기간 (stay), not work. 비자 내비게이터 states it as "3년 이상 국내 체류하며 국내 기업에 1년 이상 전일제 상용근로 형태로 근무" (scratchpad/navi.txt line 486, 643, 844). A reader who studied in Korea on D-2 for 3 years and then worked 1 year qualifies on residence but is told by the data that they need 3 years of work.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제8호; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 3. 구직(D-10) 유형1 3단계 / 8. 거주(F-2) 유형1 1단계
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa F-5-10 (line 1407) change the `how.en` text from "...; 3+ years' work in Korea; own income of 1× GNI; KIIP level 5." to "...; 3+ years of residence in Korea and 1+ year of full-time regular work at a Korean company; own income of 1× GNI; KIIP level 5." (and the ru/uz/uz-cyrl equivalents).

### [medium] wrong-needs: F-5-10 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: F-5-10's `needs: [{edu: 3, stem: true}, {koreanEdu: 4}]` hides the status from anyone who has the Ministry-listed technical/skills certificate but no bachelor's degree, even though the certificate is an independent, alternative qualification route.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3, 영주(F-5) item 8: "법무부장관이 정하는 분야의 학사 학위 이상의 학위증 **또는** 법무부장관이 정하는 기술자격증이 있는 사람으로서 국내 체류기간이 3년 이상이고..." - degree OR certificate (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt). 비자 내비게이터 repeats it: "(F-5, 학·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자) 특정 분야의 학사 학위 이상 또는 기술자격증 취득자..." (scratchpad/navi.txt line 486). The in-repo manual document list for F-5-10 asks for a "Copy of the degree or licence certificate" (data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-10'].change). The engine has no fact for a professional licence, and per engine.md a reader who answered edu = 1 or 2 is BLOCKED by this `needs`, so the certificate-only route can never be offered to anyone who answered the education question honestly. The data's own visa name ("Degree or licence holder") and `how` ("...or engineer licence") contradict its own `needs`.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제8호; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 8. 거주(F-2) 유형1 / 3. 구직(D-10) 유형1 3단계; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 7. 학사·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자[F-5-10] > 다. 제출서류
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa F-5-10 (line 1405) delete the `needs:` block (lines 1408-1410) and keep the degree-or-certificate rule in `how` only, since the engine has no licence fact; if some gating is wanted, loosen it to `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 4}]` so that a non-STEM degree or a certificate holder is not hard-blocked.

### [medium] wrong-note: F-5-19 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-5-19's `how` says the family member qualifies "after 5 years on F-2-8 or F-2-81"; the 5 years is the investor's investment-maintenance period, not the family member's own period of stay, and F-5-19 has no income or Korean-language requirement at all.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 14 attaches the 5-year condition to the investor and then extends the status to the family: "...체류자격을 받은 후 5년 이상 계속 투자 상태를 유지하고 있는 사람으로서 ... 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람과 그 배우자 및 자녀" (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt). 비자 내비게이터 PART 05 FAQ 9 is explicit: "부동산 투자자 영주 자격의 경우 ... '5년 이상'은 투자기간을 뜻하며, 국내 체류기간이 아닙니다" (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 1298-1305). The in-repo manual document list for F-5-19 contains only the common documents plus "Proof of family relationship" and "Proof of being unmarried" - no income, asset or Korean-ability documents - confirming there is no own-stay/income test (data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-19'].change). Compare the data's own F-5-22, the same situation for the public-interest investor, whose `how` correctly says only "Spouse or unmarried child of an F-5-21 holder."
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제14호; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, PART 05 주요 상담 사례 질문 9; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 12. 관광·휴양시설 투자자[F-5-17] 및 그 배우자와 미혼 자녀[F-5-19] > 다. 제출서류
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa F-5-19 (line 1439) change `how.en` to "Spouse or unmarried child of an F-5-17 holder, once the investor has kept the investment for 5 years; no income or KIIP test." (and the ru/uz/uz-cyrl equivalents).

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-7 -> F-5-9

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-2-7 offers F-5-15 (Korean doctorate) but not F-5-9 (foreign high-tech doctorate), although the official F-5 routes out of 거주(F-2) list both doctorate categories and item 7(a) of the decree puts no condition on the prior status.
- **evidence**: 비자 내비게이터, 8. 거주(F-2) > 유형1 (거주 -> 영주) lists, side by side, "(F-5, 첨단분야 박사) 국외에서 첨단기술 분야 박사 학위 취득 후 국내 기업에서 ... 1년 이상 ... 근무" and "(F-5, 일반분야 박사) 국내 대학원에서 정규 박사과정 이수..." as available routes from 거주(F-2) (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 841-848). Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 7(a) only requires "국외에서 일정 분야의 박사 학위를 취득한 사람으로서 영주자격(F-5) 신청 시 국내 기업 등에 고용된 사람" - no prior-status restriction. In visamap.yaml F-2-7 `next` is [F-5-16, F-5-S1, F-2-T, F-5-1, F-5-15, F-5-11] (line 1202ff): F-5-15 is there, F-5-9 is not. The same gap exists on F-2-7S and F-2-99.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제7호 가목; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 8. 거주(F-2) ❷ 맞춤형 체류 흐름도 유형1 1단계 (p.33)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: F-5-9, note: {en: 'If you hold a foreign doctorate in a high-tech field: after 1+ year at a Korean company in that field.'}}` to the `next` lists of F-2-7 (line 1202), F-2-7S (line 1216) and F-2-99 (line 1259), reusing the same note object already used on E-1/E-3/E-7-1.

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-99 -> F-5-10

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: F-5-10 is only offered from E-1, E-2, E-3, E-5, E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3; it is missing from every 거주(F-2) status and from E-4, E-6-1, E-6-3, E-7-4, E-7-S1, E-7-S2 and E-7-T, although it is an official route out of both 거주(F-2) and the whole 전문인력 (E-1~E-7) group.
- **evidence**: 비자 내비게이터, 8. 거주(F-2) 유형1 lists "(F-5, 학·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자)" among the F-5 routes available from 거주(F-2) (scratchpad/navi.txt line 844), and the 전문인력 chapter note says "첨단분야 박사, 학·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자, 일반분야 박사의 경우 요건 충족 시 [전문인력]에서 [영주] 자격으로 바로가기 가능", where 전문인력 is C-4/E-1~E-7 (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 536-538, chapter 4 heading line 574). Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 8 sets no prior-status condition at all. E-4 (기술지도, technical instructor) is the clearest omission since its holders are exactly the 기술자격증 population. Reverse index of visamap.yaml confirms F-5-10 has no inbound edge from any F-2-* node or from E-4/E-6/E-7-4/E-7-S1/E-7-S2/E-7-T.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제8호; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 3. 구직(D-10) 유형1 3단계 각주 및 8. 거주(F-2) 유형1 1단계
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add the bare entry `- F-5-10` to the `next` lists of F-2-99 (line 1259), F-2-7 (line 1202), F-2-7S (line 1216), E-4 (line 665), E-6-1, E-6-3, E-7-S1, E-7-S2 and E-7-T.

### [medium] missing-edge: NONE -> F-5-T1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: F-5-T1 is only reachable from F-2-T1, but the manual documents F-5-T1 as a visa that can also be issued from abroad (사증발급), and F-2-T1 itself has no inbound edge anywhere in the graph, so the Top-Tier family PR branch can never be reached by a reader who starts outside Korea.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-T1'] has BOTH a `change` and an `abroad` document list, both sourced to "외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 > Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등 > 동반가족(배우자·미성년자녀)의 영주 (F-5-T1) > 사증발급·자격변경", with the case "Spouse or minor child of a Top-Tier holder" - i.e. the manual treats visa issuance abroad as an available route. F-5-T1 is the only F-5 code with a documented `abroad` list that the map does not wire from NONE (F-5-5 and F-5-11, the other two, both appear in NONE's `next`). A reachability pass over visamap.yaml shows F-2-T1 has zero inbound edges and F-5-T1 is unreachable from NONE. The data's `how` for F-5-T1 ("3 years on F-2-T1") also conflicts with the manual's abroad case, which states only the family relationship.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 > Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등 > 동반가족(배우자·미성년자녀)의 영주 (F-5-T1) > 사증발급·자격변경 (as copied into data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-T1'].abroad); scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt line "F-5-T1: change,abroad"
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa NONE (line 1481) add to `next`: `- {to: F-5-T1, needs: {spouse: f5}, note: {en: 'Spouse or minor child of a Top-Tier (F-5-T) holder: the PR visa can be issued by a consulate.'}}`; separately give F-2-T1 at least one inbound edge (e.g. from NONE and from F-3) so the in-Korea branch is reachable too.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-1 -> F-5-12

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: F-5-12 (특별공로자, person of special merit to Korea) does not exist as a node at all, so this real F-5 sub-code can never be offered; the data's F-5-28 is a different category (특별기여자, the Afghan special-contributor programme), not 특별공로자.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 10: "대한민국에 특별한 공로가 있다고 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람" (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt). 비자 내비게이터, 10. 영주(F-5) ❶ 영주 비자의 종류 lists "특별 공로자" in the 12th position of an otherwise sub-code-ordered list (scratchpad/navi.txt lines 1021-1053). The KIS 동포 manual names the code explicitly in the 기본소양 exemption list: "고액 투자자(F-5-5), 첨단분야 박사(F-5-9), 일반분야 박사(F-5-15), 특정분야 능력소유자(F-5-11), 특별공로자(F-5-12)" (scratchpad/dongpo.txt lines 701-703). Meanwhile data/visadocs.yaml sources F-5-28 to "외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 18. 특별기여자[F-5-28]", a separate category, so nothing in visamap.yaml covers 특별공로자.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제10호; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 10. 영주(F-5) ❶ 영주 비자의 종류; 동포 맞춤형 길라잡이, 영주(F-5) 기본소양 면제 대상 ("특별공로자(F-5-12)")
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add a visa node `F-5-12: {name: {en: Person of special merit}, how: {en: 'Recognised by the Minister of Justice as having rendered special merit to Korea; no residence, income or KIIP requirement.'}}` and add `- F-5-12` to the `next` lists of the long-term work/residence statuses (at minimum E-1, E-3, E-6-1, E-6-3, E-7-1 and F-2-7), mirroring how F-5-11 is wired.

### [low] missing-edge: E-7-4R -> F-5-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-7-4R offers no F-5 target at all, while the otherwise parallel E-7-4 offers F-5-1; E-7-4R is an E-7 status and therefore inside the status range that qualifies for 일반영주.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 1 counts any 별표 1의2 제10~20호 status, i.e. all of 특정활동(E-7) including its sub-codes, for the 5-year 일반영주 requirement; nothing in the decree or the manual carves out the regional skilled-worker sub-code. In visamap.yaml E-7-4 `next` contains F-5-1 (gated with `needs: {edu: 3}`) while E-7-4R `next` (line 870) is [F-2-R, F-6-1, F-2-3, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4, D-10-1] with no F-5 entry, so the tool can never show permanent residence to an E-7-4R holder.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제1호 (별표 1의2 제20호 특정활동(E-7)); 법무부 비자 내비게이터 Ver 2022.12, 4. 취업(전문인력) 3단계 "(F-5, 일반영주) ... E-1~E-7 체류자격이나 F-2 체류자격으로 5년 이상 국내 체류"
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa E-7-4R (line 870) add to `next`: `- {to: F-5-1, needs: {edu: 3}, note: *<the same note object already used on E-7-4's F-5-1 entry>}`.

### [low] missing-edge: H-2 -> F-5-7

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: F-5-7 (ethnic Korean who already meets the naturalization/nationality-restoration requirements) is offered only from F-4, but it has no prior-status condition, so H-2 holders who qualify are never shown it.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 5 reads simply "「재외동포의 출입국과 법적 지위에 관한 법률」 제2조제2호의 외국국적동포로서 「국적법」에 따른 국적 취득 요건을 갖춘 사람" - no requirement to hold F-4 (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt). The KIS 동포 manual's F-5-7 common document list asks for "외국인등록증(거소신고증)", i.e. any registered status, and separately notes that an F-5-7 applicant who wants their family with them should change to F-4 first - which only makes sense if the F-5-7 applicant is not already on F-4 (scratchpad/dongpo.txt lines 466-510, 604-606). In visamap.yaml H-2 `next` is [F-4, F-5-14, E-7-4, E-7-4R] (line 1048ff).
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제5호; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 / 동포 맞춤형 길라잡이, 5. 외국국적동포 영주자격(F-5) 부여 세부절차 > 나. > 국적취득요건을 갖춘 사람(F-5-7) 공통제출서류
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa H-2 (line 1048) add `- F-5-7` to `next`.

### [low] wrong-note: F-5-4 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.4)
- **claim**: F-5-4's `how` adds a condition that the F-5 relative must already have held permanent residence for 2+ years; the rule only requires that the applicant is the spouse or minor child of an F-5 holder and has stayed in Korea 2+ years.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 2: "국민 또는 영주자격(F-5)을 가진 사람의 배우자 또는 미성년 자녀로서 대한민국에 2년 이상 체류하고 있는 사람" - the 2-year clock is the applicant's own stay in Korea and there is no waiting period attached to the principal's F-5 (scratchpad/byulpyo13.txt). The in-repo manual document list for F-5-4 ("3. 일반 영주자의 배우자 또는 미성년 자녀[F-5-4]") asks only for proof of family relationship plus the common income/asset/Korean-ability documents, with no document evidencing the date the principal obtained F-5 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-5-4'].change). The extra "after the family member has held F-5 for 2+ years" clause makes the tool tell eligible families they must wait.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 제2호; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 > 32. 영주(F-5) > 체류자격 변경허가 > 3. 일반 영주자의 배우자 또는 미성년 자녀[F-5-4] > 다. 제출서류
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml visa F-5-4 (line 1379) change `how.en` to "Spouse or minor child of an F-5 holder, after 2+ years of stay in Korea (normally on F-2-3); income of 1× GNI; KIIP level 5." (and the ru/uz/uz-cyrl equivalents).

### [low] other: F-4-R -> F-5-6R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.3)
- **claim**: F-5-6R looks like an invented sub-code: it is the only F-5 node in the map with no counterpart anywhere in the manual-derived data, and its stated rule mixes numbers that belong to other codes.
- **evidence**: UNSOURCED - I could not find any official text for an F-5-6R sub-code and my web-search budget was exhausted before I could check 지역특화형 비자 guidance. Circumstantial evidence: data/visadocs.yaml `targets` contains entries for every other F-5 node in the map except F-5-6R, F-5-12, F-5-14, F-5-23, F-5-27 (scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt), and it does document the other brand-new programmes (F-5-S1/S2 for K-STAR, F-5-T/T1 for Top-Tier) as well as F-4-R itself; Enforcement Decree table 1-3 has no 지역특화 item; and the figures in the data's `how` ("income of 70% of GNI", "4 years / 2 years") are the 방문취업 장기근속자 F-5-14 figures from 동포 맞춤형 길라잡이 (scratchpad/dongpo.txt lines 397, 507-530), which suggests they were carried over rather than sourced. F-5-6R is the only F-5 target offered from F-4-R, so if the code is wrong that whole branch is wrong.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) (no 지역특화 item); scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt (F-5 codes documented in 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 - F-5-6R absent); 동포 맞춤형 길라잡이 F-5-14 요건 (source of the 70% GNI and 4-year figures)
- **fix**: Verify the code against 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 ch. 36/37 (지역특화형 비자) with immigration (1345). If no F-5-6R exists, delete the F-5-6R node from data/visamap.yaml (line 1390) and change F-4-R's `next` (line ~1353) to `[F-5-6, F-5-7]` so regional F-4-R holders are routed to the real overseas-Korean PR codes.

## graph / missing

### [high] missing-edge: F-3 -> E-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: F-3 dependants are offered only E-7-1 and E-2, although the manual gives the F-3 spouse of an E-1~E-7 professional a named, document-listed route into E-1, E-3, E-4 and E-5 as well.
- **evidence**: The E-1, E-3 and E-5 chapters each carry a change-of-status variant specifically for "E-1~E-5 / E-6(E-6-2 제외) / E-7 전문인력의 배우자(F-3)" taking a professional job; the repo copies them verbatim: data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-3'].change variant "Spouse of an E-1~E-5 / E-6 (not E-6-2) / E-7 professional", targets['E-1'].change variant "Spouse (F-3) of an E-1~E-5 / E-6 (not E-6-2) / E-7 professional taking a professional job", targets['E-5'].change same wording. visamap.yaml F-3 'next' contains E-7-1 and E-2 only, so the tool can never suggest the researcher/professor/licensed-professional route to a trailing spouse - exactly the 'dependent spouse of a worker who starts working' journey.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 21. 연구(E-3) › 체류자격 변경허가; › 19. 교수(E-1) › 체류자격 변경허가; › 23. 전문직업(E-5) › 체류자격 변경허가 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-3'|'E-1'|'E-5'].change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, F-3 `next:` (after the existing `- E-2`) add `- E-1`, `- E-3`, `- E-4`, `- E-5`.

### [high] missing-edge: F-6-1 -> F-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: F-6-1 offers only F-5-2, F-6-2 and F-6-3, so a marriage migrant whose marriage ends but who does not qualify as a 혼인단절자 (no child, fault not provable) is shown a dead end, even though the manual gives them an explicit change to F-1 (혼인단절 결혼이민자, F-1-11).
- **evidence**: MOJ Visa Navigator, 9. 결혼이민(F-6) ❹ 유의사항: "국민과 혼인이 단절되었으나 F-6(혼인단절자)에 해당하지 않는 사람으로서 재산분할, 가사정리 등의 사유로 국내 체류가 불가피한 경우 F-1(혼인단절 결혼이민자) 자격으로 변경 가능." The sub-code F-1-11 (혼인단절 결혼이민자) also appears in the repo's own data/visadocs.yaml. visamap.yaml F-6-1 'next' (line ~1319) has no F-1 entry, and F-1 has in-degree 0 across the whole graph, so this node can never be reached at all.
- **citation**: 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부, 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.39 '9. 결혼이민(F-6) ❹ 유의사항'; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 29. 방문동거(F-1) (F-1-11)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, F-6-1 `next:` add `- to: F-1` with a note that this is for a marriage that ended without qualifying for F-6-3 (property settlement / winding up affairs).

### [high] missing-edge: E-3 -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: E-3 (and E-1, E-5) offer no move to E-7-1, so a researcher/professor/licensed professional who takes a company job on E-7 can never be shown that route, even though the E-7 chapter explicitly lists E-1~E-6 holders as eligible to change to E-7 inside Korea.
- **evidence**: The sojourn manual's E-7 change-of-status section lists the eligible prior statuses as "국내 체류 중인 D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6(E-6-2 제외) 자격 소지자 및 그 배우자(F-3)" - i.e. every E-1 through E-6 status except E-6-2 may change to E-7 in Korea. The repo's own copy of that list is data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change variant "Registered D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6 (not E-6-2) holders and their F-3 spouses changing to E-7". In visamap.yaml E-2, E-4, E-6-1 and E-6-3 all have E-7-1 in 'next', but E-1, E-3 and E-5 do not, which is an internal inconsistency as well as a factual gap. Moving from a university/institute post to a corporate R&D or professional post is one of the most common mid-career moves for these holders.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (as copied into data/visadocs.yaml, targets['E-7-1'].change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- E-7-1` to the `next:` list of E-3 (line ~634), of E-1 and of E-5. No `abroad` flag and no move-level `needs` (the E-7-1 visa-level needs already gate education/experience).

### [high] missing-edge: D-2-4 -> D-8-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: D-2-4 (doctoral student) has no entrepreneurship branch at all: unlike D-2-1/D-2-2/D-2-3 it offers neither D-8-4 (tech start-up) nor D-10-2 (start-up preparation) nor D-9-5 (graduate entrepreneur), although a PhD student meets those requirements more easily than anyone below them.
- **evidence**: D-8-4 requires "국내 전문학사 이상 취득, 국외 학사 이상 취득" plus start-up points; D-10-2 requires 학사(국내 전문학사) 이상; D-9-5 (유학무역경영) requires "국내 석사 이상 취득(예정)자로 1억원 이상을 투자하여 국내에서 회사 경영". A doctoral student clears all three thresholds. In visamap.yaml D-2-3 offers D-8-4, D-10-2 and D-9-5, and even D-2-1 (associate degree) offers D-8-4, but D-2-4's `next` has none of them - so the 'graduate who starts a company' journey is undrawable for PhD students.
- **citation**: 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) 유형3 '유학생이 창업, 무역경영 후 영주 자격 취득' pp.11-12; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 4. 기술창업(D-8-4); › 구직(D-10) › 2) 기술창업준비(D-10-2)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, D-2-4 `next:` (line ~412) add `- D-8-4`, `- D-10-2` and `- D-9-5`.

### [high] missing-edge: F-2-7 -> F-5-10

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: F-2 holders are offered no degree/qualification-based permanent-residence route: F-2-7 'next' lacks F-5-10 and F-5-9, and F-2-99 and F-2-R lack F-5-10, F-5-9 and F-5-15, although the decree conditions those F-5 categories on the person, not on a particular prior status, and the official flow chart lists them as routes out of 거주(F-2).
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-3 item 8 (F-5-10) requires only "법무부장관이 정하는 분야의 학사 학위 이상의 학위증 또는 기술자격증 + 국내 체류기간 3년 이상 + 영주 신청 시 국내기업 고용 및 임금기준"; item 7 (F-5-9/F-5-15) requires only the doctorate plus 1 year of work. The MOJ Visa Navigator's "8. 거주(F-2) › 유형1 거주 자격자의 영주 자격 취득" lists the routes out of F-2 as 일반영주(F-5-1), 첨단분야 박사(F-5-9), 학·석사 학위증 및 자격증 소지자(F-5-10), 일반분야 박사(F-5-15) and 점수제 영주자(F-5-16). visamap.yaml gives F-2-7 only F-5-16/F-5-S1/F-5-1/F-5-15/F-5-11, F-2-99 only F-5-1/F-5-13 and F-2-R only F-5-1 - so a points-based or long-term resident who holds a Korean master's or a listed engineer licence is steered to the 5-year F-5-1 route and never shown the faster F-5-10.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제8호 (and 제7호); 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.33 '8. 거주(F-2) › 유형1 › 1단계 (거주 → 영주)'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-5-10` and `- F-5-9` to F-2-7's `next:` (line ~1202); add `- F-5-10` and `- F-5-15` to F-2-99's `next:`; add `- F-5-10` to F-2-R's `next:`.

### [high] missing-edge: C-3-1 -> F-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: No status in the graph offers F-3; it is reachable only from NONE. A family member already in Korea on a short-term or long-term status can therefore never be shown the in-Korea change to F-3, even though the manual documents exactly that change and the repo's own ground truth marks F-3 as having a 'change' list.
- **evidence**: visadocs-targets.txt records `F-3: change,abroad,extend`, and data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-3'].change variant 1 is "Dependant who, after entry, needs humanitarian consideration (pregnancy, birth, child-raising, illness, etc.): entered visa-free/short-term, or lawful long-term resident" (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 31. 동반(F-3) › 체류자격 변경허가). The MOJ Visa Navigator says for the family of a D-2 student: "불법체류다발국가 및 중점관리국가 국민의 경우 재외공관에 사증을 신청하여야 하며, 일반 국가의 경우 국내에서 체류자격변경 허용" - i.e. for ordinary nationalities the change to F-3 is made inside Korea. An in-degree scan of visamap.yaml shows F-3 is offered by NONE only.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 31. 동반(F-3) › 체류자격 변경허가; 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부, 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.13 ❹ 동반 가족 초청
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-3` to the `next:` lists of C-3-1, C-3-9, B-1, B-2 (these four with `abroadFor: notified`) and plain `- F-3` to D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4, D-4-1 and F-1.

### [high] missing-edge: D-4-3 -> D-10-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: The whole 'Korea-raised foreign youth' track is undrawable: D-4-3 (Korean school student) offers only D-2-1 and D-2-2, and E-7-Y has in-degree 0, so a young person who finished Korean elementary/middle/high school can never be routed D-4-3 → D-10-1 → E-7-Y → F-2-R even though visamap already contains E-7-Y with exactly that exit list.
- **evidence**: The D-10 chapter's points-exempt list includes "(국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년) 신청일 기준 18세 이상 24세 이하의 합법체류자로 「초·중등교육법」에 따른 국내 초·중·고교를 모두 졸업하였거나 이와 동등한 학력을 갖춘 사람" - any lawful status, which covers D-4-3. The 2026 manual has a whole chapter 38 '국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업·정주 체류제도' whose Ⅲ.2 is 국내성장인력(E-7-Y) and Ⅲ.3 지역특화 우수인재, and the D-10 chapter notes that the youth's address should be chosen with the later F-2-R change in mind. visamap.yaml D-4-3 (line ~285) has next: [D-2-1, D-2-2] only, and no visa anywhere offers E-7-Y.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 구직(D-10) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1-2) 일반 구직(D-10-1): 점수제 면제 특례자 › (국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년); › 38. 국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년 취업‧정주 체류제도 › Ⅲ.2 국내성장인력(E-7-Y)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to D-4-3's `next:` an entry `- to: D-10-1` with `needs: {}` (the youth track is points- and degree-exempt) plus a note about the 18-24 / Korean-school rule; and add `- E-7-Y` to D-10-1's `next:` (line ~? D-10-1 block) so the youth track can continue into work.

### [high] missing-edge: C-3-1 -> G-1-5

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: G-1-5 (refugee applicant) has in-degree 0, so the entire refugee path G-1-5 → F-2-4 → F-5-27 that the data already models is unreachable: nobody can ever be shown how to get to it.
- **evidence**: HiKorea's official refugee/residence/nationality guide states that a person who files a refugee application must obtain residence permission at the competent office and that abusive applicants "체류자격(G-1) 변경 허가가 불허될 수 있습니다" - i.e. the normal route into G-1-5 is a change of status in Korea from whatever status the applicant holds (typically B-2, C-3 or an expiring D/E status). The D-10 chapter likewise lists G-1 among the statuses one may hold and change from, confirming G-1 is a status people move into and out of. An in-degree scan of visamap.yaml shows no visa lists G-1-5 in 'next', and NONE does not either.
- **citation**: HiKorea / 법무부, 난민·체류·국적 안내 (hikorea.go.kr, '난민 신청' 섹션: 체류자격(G-1) 변경 허가); 난민법 제5조·제6조
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- G-1-5` to the `next:` lists of B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-9, D-2-2, D-4-1 and E-9 (no `abroad` flag - it is an in-Korea change).

### [medium] missing-edge: D-4-1 -> D-4-6

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: D-4-1 offers no move to the other D-4 sub-codes, although the repo's own manual-derived data records D-4-1 → D-4-6, D-4-1 → D-4-7 and D-4-1 → D-4-3 as documented in-Korea changes of status.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml `within` contains `D-4-1>D-4-3: {use: change}`, `D-4-1>D-4-6: {use: change}` and `D-4-1>D-4-7: {use: change}` (also listed in visadocs-targets.txt under '# within'). visamap.yaml D-4-1 (line ~255) next: [D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4, D-10-1, E-7-1, E-2, F-6-1, F-2-3] - none of the D-4 sub-codes. This matters because D-4-6 (private-institute trainee) is itself a documented feeder into E-7-1/E-7-2/E-7-3 for people without a degree, so a language student who switches to vocational training is cut off from that whole branch.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 10. 일반연수(D-4) › 체류자격 변경허가 (as copied into data/visadocs.yaml `within`: D-4-1>D-4-3, D-4-1>D-4-6, D-4-1>D-4-7)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, D-4-1 `next:` add `- D-4-6`, `- D-4-7` and `- D-4-3` (the last gated by its own `age: [0, 19]`).

### [medium] missing-edge: NONE -> F-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: NONE's 61 first-visa options omit F-1 (방문동거) entirely, so the parent of a Korean-university graduate student, the parent of a minor D-4 student, the parent of an F-2/F-5 holder or a relative joining family in Korea can never be given a first-visa route - and F-1 then has in-degree 0 in the whole graph.
- **evidence**: The MOJ Visa Navigator lists F-1 (방문동거) as a long-term status with named sub-targets (결혼이민자의 부모 등 가족, 우수인재·투자자 및 유학생 부모, 고등학교 이하 외국인 유학생 동반 부모, 재외동포(F-4)·방문취업(H-2) 자격의 배우자 및 미성년자녀, 거주(F-2) 자격의 배우자 또는 미성년 자녀, 국적신청자 ...), and says of the parent categories "해당 자격의 경우, 원칙적으로 국내 체류자격 변경 불가하며 사증발급인정서 신청 대상" - i.e. the route is a visa issued abroad on a confirmation of visa issuance, which is exactly a NONE → F-1 abroad move. data/visadocs.yaml contains F-1 sub-codes F-1-1, F-1-5, F-1-11, F-1-15, F-1-51, F-1-52.
- **citation**: 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부, 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.13 ❹ 동반 가족 초청 and p.31 '7. 방문동거, 동반(F-1, F-3)'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: F-1, abroad: true}` to NONE's `next:` (line ~1481).

### [medium] missing-edge: D-10-T -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-10-T offers only E-7-T and F-2-T, so a top-100-university master's/PhD holder on the pre-Top-Tier job-seeker visa who takes an ordinary professional job has no drawable route and appears to be stuck.
- **evidence**: The E-7 change-of-status list covers "D-4~D-10" holders, which includes every D-10 sub-code (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change, variant "Registered D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6 (not E-6-2) holders ... changing to E-7"). Nothing in the Top-Tier chapter removes the ordinary E-7 route from a D-10-T holder - E-7-T is an additional, more favourable track, not an exclusive one. visamap.yaml D-10-T next: [E-7-T, F-2-T] only, and it is also the only D-10 sub-code with no D-10-1 fallback.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-1'].change); › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, D-10-T `next:` add `- E-7-1`, and also `- D-10-1` (fallback to ordinary job-seeking) and `- E-3`.

### [medium] missing-edge: G-1-6 -> D-10-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: G-1-6 (humanitarian stay) offers only D-2-2 and D-4-1, and G-1-5 offers only F-2-4, although the D-10 chapter explicitly contemplates G-1 holders changing to D-10-1 (and from there to the whole professional-work branch).
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1-1) 점수제 적용 대상자: "학사(국내 전문학사 포함) 이상의 학위를 소지한 국내 합법 체류자 ... 단, 법무부장관 고시국가 국민 중 B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, D-3, E-9, E-10, G-1 자격에 대해서는 총 득점이 80점 이상인 경우에만 체류자격 변경 허용." G-1 is named as a status one may change from (with the higher 80-point bar for notified countries), so the route exists. visamap.yaml G-1-6 (line ~1360) next: [D-2-2, D-4-1] only.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 구직(D-10) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 1-1) 일반 구직(D-10-1): 점수제 적용 대상자 (scratchpad/d10.txt lines 70-77)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to G-1-6's `next:` an entry `- to: D-10-1` with a note that citizens of the 21 notified countries need 80+ points; add the same entry to G-1-5's `next:`.

### [medium] missing-edge: F-2-99 -> F-2-R

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: No F-2 sub-code offers F-2-R or F-2-7S, although the repo's own manual-derived 'within' table documents an in-Korea change from every F-2 sub-code to F-2-R and to F-2-7S (and from F-2-71 to F-3).
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml `within` contains F-2-2>F-2-R, F-2-3>F-2-R, F-2-4>F-2-R, F-2-5>F-2-R, F-2-7>F-2-R, F-2-8>F-2-R, F-2-12>F-2-R, F-2-71>F-2-R, F-2-81>F-2-R, F-2-99>F-2-R, F-2-T>F-2-R, F-2-T1>F-2-R, F-2-R>F-2-7S and the parallel >F-2-7S set, all `use: change`; visadocs-targets.txt reproduces the same list. The F-2-R chapter's change variant is "Lawful resident in Korea applying for regional talent (F-2-R)", with no prior-status restriction. In visamap.yaml none of the F-2-* nodes lists F-2-R or F-2-7S in 'next' (F-2-R's 20 in-edges all come from D/E statuses), so a resident who moves to a depopulating area cannot be shown the regional-talent route.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 37. 지역특화형비자 › Ⅱ. 지역특화형 우수인재(F-2) › (1) 체류자격 변경 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['F-2-R'].change and `within` F-2-*>F-2-R)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-2-R` to the `next:` lists of F-2-99, F-2-3, F-2-5, F-2-7, F-2-8, F-2-12, F-2-71, F-2-T; add `- F-2-7S` to F-2-R's `next:`; add `- F-3` to F-2-71's `next:`.

### [medium] unreachable: NONE -> D-4-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: D-4-2 (general trainee) and E-6-2 (hotel/venue performer) exist as visas with their own 'next' lists but have in-degree 0 and are not offered by NONE either, so no reader can ever land on them or see their onward routes.
- **evidence**: An in-degree scan of data/visamap.yaml shows D-4-2 and E-6-2 are never named as a target by any visa, and NONE's 61 first-visa options include neither. Both are real, currently-issued statuses: 일반연수(D-4-2) is the D-4 sub-code for training at a company, public institute or international organisation (Enforcement Decree table 1-2 item 7 '일반연수(D-4)'; MOJ Visa Navigator lists '대학부설 어학원 제외기관 연수생(외국인투자기업 등)' as a D-4 target group), and E-6-2 is the 호텔·유흥업소 공연 sub-code of 예술흥행(E-6) (decree table 1-2 item 19; Visa Navigator lists '호텔업 시설, 유흥업소 등에서 공연하는 자'). Both are issued at a consulate, which is why visadocs-targets.txt records only 'extend' for them in the in-Korea change tables - that is the reason to add them as abroad-only first visas, not to omit them.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제7호(일반연수 D-4) and 제19호(예술흥행 E-6); 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) pp.9, 23
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: D-4-2, abroad: true}` and `- {to: E-6-2, abroad: true}` to NONE's `next:` (line ~1481).

### [medium] unreachable: NONE -> F-2-13

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: The investor-family and Top-Tier-family nodes F-2-13, F-2-81 and F-2-T1, and the special-contributor node F-2-16, all have in-degree 0 and are not offered by NONE, so their F-5 goals (F-5-22, F-5-19, F-5-T1, F-5-28/F-5-29) are unreachable dead branches.
- **evidence**: An in-degree scan of data/visamap.yaml shows F-2-13, F-2-81, F-2-T1 and F-2-16 are never named as a target by any visa and are absent from NONE's list, yet each has a 'next' pointing at an F-5 goal. The equivalent family node F-2-71 IS reachable (from F-1 and F-3), which shows the omission is an oversight rather than a rule. The manual documents these family statuses: 톱티어 거주 동반가족(배우자·미성년자녀) is handled under "사증발급·체류자격 변경·체류기간 연장" (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 39. 톱티어 비자 › Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등), and the MOJ Visa Navigator says for investor families "투자자 본인의 거주(F-2) 자격 취득에 따라 배우자 및 미성년 자녀를 거주(F-2) 자격으로 ... 허용".
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 › Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등 › 톱티어 거주 동반가족; 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.36 ❻ 동반 가족 초청
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- {to: F-2-13, abroad: true}`, `- {to: F-2-81, abroad: true}` and `- {to: F-2-T1, abroad: true}` to NONE's `next:`, and add `- F-2-T1` to F-3's and `- F-2-13`/`- F-2-81` to F-1's `next:` for the in-Korea case; give F-2-16 at least one in-edge (e.g. from F-1 or G-1-6) or drop it together with F-5-28/F-5-29.

### [medium] missing-edge: F-4 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: No F-4, F-4-R, H-2, D-8, D-9 or F-2 status offers F-6-1, so an ethnic Korean on F-4, a visitor-worker on H-2, an investor on D-8 or a resident on F-2-7 who marries a Korean citizen is shown no marriage route at all - while almost every D/E status in the graph does offer it.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-2 item 27 가목 defines 결혼이민(F-6) simply as "국민의 배우자", with no restriction on the prior status, and the manual's F-6-1 change-of-status chapter lists only document variants (by nationality / child / programme exemption), not eligible prior statuses. The only prior-status restriction the repo's own data records is the opposite one - an exception allowing short-term holders (C-3-8 etc.) to change only when pregnancy or childbirth makes it unavoidable (data/visadocs.yaml exceptions['C-3-8>F-6-1']), which implies lawful long-term holders change normally. visamap.yaml offers F-6-1 from 46 nodes but from none of F-4, F-4-R, H-2, D-8-1..4, D-9-1/4/5, D-10-2/3/T, D-4-3, D-4-6 or any F-2 sub-code.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제27호 가목 (결혼이민 F-6); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 체류자격 변경허가(F-6-1); data/visadocs.yaml exceptions['C-3-8>F-6-1']
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- F-6-1` to the `next:` lists of F-4 (line ~1344), F-4-R, H-2, D-8-1, D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4, D-9-1, D-9-4, D-9-5, D-10-2, D-10-3, D-10-T, D-4-6 and F-2-7 / F-2-99 / F-2-R.

### [medium] missing-edge: E-7-4R -> F-2-99

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-7-4R, E-7-T and E-7-Y have no F-2-99 move, although F-2-99 (기타 장기체류자) is open to 특정활동(E-7) generally and the sibling sub-code E-7-4 does offer it.
- **evidence**: The F-2-99 eligibility list is by status family: "신청 대상 체류자격: 문화예술(D-1), 취재(D-5), 종교(D-6), 주재(D-7), 기업투자(D-8), 무역경영(D-9), 교수(E-1), 회화지도(E-2), 연구(E-3), 기술지도(E-4), 전문직업(E-5), 예술흥행(E-6-1, 3), 특정활동(E-7), 방문동거(F-1)" - E-7 without sub-code qualification; the only E-7 exclusion anywhere in the F-2 chapter is E-6-2 for the F-2-7 professional track. visamap.yaml gives F-2-99 to E-7-1, E-7-2, E-7-3, E-7-4, E-7-S1 and E-7-S2 but not to E-7-4R, E-7-T or E-7-Y, which leaves the regional skilled worker (E-7-4R) with only F-2-R as a residence route.
- **citation**: 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.35 '❹ 장기체류자(F-2-99) 자격 요건 - 신청 대상 체류자격'; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제24호 바목
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to E-7-4R's `next:` an entry `- to: F-2-99` with the same note E-7-4 carries (5 years on E-7; E-9 years do not count), and add `- F-2-99` to E-7-T's and E-7-Y's `next:`.

### [medium] other: - -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The A series is missing from the data entirely: A-1 (외교), A-2 (공무) and A-3 (협정/SOFA) appear in neither the 'statuses' list nor the 'visas' list, and C-1 (일시취재) is missing too, so diplomats', officials' and SOFA-covered people's family members - who do change to D-2, E-2 and other statuses in Korea - have no starting point.
- **evidence**: Enforcement Decree table 1-2 items 1-3 are "1. 외교(A-1) ... 2. 공무(A-2) ... 3. 협정(A-3) 대한민국정부와의 협정에 따라 외국인등록이 면제되거나 면제할 필요가 있다고 인정되는 사람과 그 가족", and the MOJ Visa Navigator's status table opens with the same three plus C-1 (일시취재). These are not dead letters in the change tables either: data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-2'].change carries a variant "Registered foreigner (incl. A-1/A-2/A-3) meeting E-2 requirements", and the D-10 chapter's notified-country rule names C-1 as a status one may change from. visamap.yaml's statuses map runs B-1, B-2, C-3, C-4, D-1 ... with no A-1/A-2/A-3 and no C-1.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제1호~제3호 (외교 A-1, 공무 A-2, 협정 A-3); 법무부 비자 내비게이터 (Ver 2022.12) p.5 체류자격 표; data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-2'].change
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add A-1, A-2, A-3 to the `statuses:` map and add minimal `visas:` entries for them (abroad-issued, `next:` at least D-2-2, D-4-1, E-2, F-6-1, F-2-3), plus C-1 as a short-term status feeding D-10-1 and D-5; add `- {to: A-1, abroad: true}` etc. to NONE only if the tool is meant to cover them, otherwise leave them reachable only as a chosen starting status.

### [medium] missing-edge: D-2-2 -> E-7-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: E-7-4 (skilled worker) is reachable only from E-9, E-10 and H-2, so the graduate route into it is missing: a graduate of a Korean 뿌리산업 training college may change status to E-7-4 under a dedicated annual quota.
- **evidence**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 특정활동(E-7) › 숙련기능공 뿌리산업체(S740), (유형별 자격요건 및 허가절차): "현지 선발자와 국내 전문대학 이상 졸업자, 비전문취업(E-9)자 등 각각의 특성에 부합하도록 자격요건 및 허가절차 등 별도 설정 ... ② 뿌리산업 인력 양성대학 졸업자 : 체류자격변경(원칙) - (자격요건) 뿌리산업학과 전공 + 뿌리산업 분야 기능사 이상 자격증 취득 또는 기량검증단의 기량검증 통과 - (허가절차 등) 연간 300명 한도에서 체류자격 변경을 허용" (③ is the separate E-9/E-10/H-2 points route). visamap.yaml E-7-4 has in-edges only from E-9, E-10 and H-2.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 숙련기능공〔뿌리산업체(S740)〕 › (유형별 자격요건 및 허가절차) ② 뿌리산업 인력 양성대학 졸업자 (scratchpad/e7.txt lines 2138-2146)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add to D-2-1's and D-2-2's `next:` an entry `- to: E-7-4` with `needs: {koreanEdu: 2}` and a note that this is the 뿌리산업 training-college graduate track (major + national technical certificate or skills verification, 300 places a year), separate from the E-9/E-10/H-2 points route.

### [low] missing-edge: D-2-3 -> E-7-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: D-2-3 and D-2-4 offer only E-7-1 among the E-7 sub-codes, so a master's or doctoral graduate who can only find a semi-professional (E-7-2) or skilled-trade (E-7-3) job is shown no work route, although D-2-1, D-2-2 and D-2-7 all offer those sub-codes.
- **evidence**: The E-7 change-of-status list is written by prior status ("D-1, D-2, D-4~D-10, E-1~E-6(E-6-2 제외)"), not by sub-code, and the E-7-2/E-7-3 occupation criteria set only a minimum (학사 이상 또는 관련 경력), which a higher degree exceeds. visamap.yaml D-2-1 next includes E-7-2 and E-7-3; D-2-3 and D-2-4 do not, which is an internal inconsistency in the same family.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 › 25. 특정활동(E-7) › 체류자격 변경허가 (data/visadocs.yaml targets['E-7-2'].change / targets['E-7-3'].change)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- E-7-2` and `- E-7-3` to D-2-3's `next:` and to D-2-4's `next:`.

## graph / none-rest

### [high] wrong-needs: NONE -> F-2-T

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: F-2-T (Top-Tier resident) is gated as "master's from a top-100 university OR 5 years of experience", so the tool offers this first visa as OPEN to anyone who answers 5+ years of work experience, with no degree or ranking at all.
- **evidence**: The Top-Tier criteria are cumulative, not alternative. Education: master's or higher obtained within the last 5 years from a university in the top 100 of QS / THE / US News (or their engineering subject rankings). Career: 3+ years at a global company (Forbes Global 2000 / Fortune Global 500 / Time 100) within a total of 8+ years' career, OR 3+ years at a world-class research institute within 5+ years of post-doctorate research in an advanced field. Plus income >= 3x GNI and a ministry recommendation. Only an income of >= 4x GNI relaxes ONE of education/career, it never removes both. The engine's `needs:` list semantics (ANY ONE map is enough) therefore makes `{experience: 5}` a standalone pass for people who cannot get this status. Verified against the Top-Tier visa manual (F-2-T / D-10-T / E-7-T) and the repo's own ground truth, which places F-2-T issuance in 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 39. 톱티어 비자 › Ⅰ › 2 톱티어 거주(F-2-T).
- **citation**: 첨단분야 해외 인재 탑티어(Top-Tier) 비자 매뉴얼 (F2-T, D10-T, E7-T) https://yoonhjs.com/living_in_korea/%EC%B2%A8%EB%8B%A8%EB%B6%84%EC%95%BC-%ED%95%B4%EC%99%B8-%EC%9D%B8%EC%9E%AC-%ED%83%91%ED%8B%B0%EC%96%B4top-tier-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EB%A7%A4%EB%89%B4%EC%96%BCf2d10e7-visa/ ; 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 39. 톱티어 비자 › Ⅰ.2 톱티어 거주(F-2-T)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: F-2-T (line 1274): replace `needs: [{edu: 4, rank: 3}, {experience: 5}]` with `needs: [{edu: 4, rank: 3, experience: 8}, {edu: 5, rank: 3, experience: 5}]` (corporate track = top-100 master's + 8 years total career incl. 3 at a global firm; research track = top-100 doctorate + 5 years post-PhD research). Put the 4x-GNI relaxation in the note on the NONE -> F-2-T move instead of a bare experience alternative.

### [medium] wrong-note: NONE -> D-8-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: D-8-4's `how` states "60+ start-up points"; the technology start-up points system requires 80 points, not 60.
- **evidence**: The 점수제 기술창업비자(D-8-4) scoring table totals 368 points and the pass mark is "총 368점 중 80점 이상" with at least one mandatory item satisfied (the worked example in the same source adds 5+10+15+25+25 = 80 points to qualify). The rest of the D-8-4 gating in the file is right: `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]` matches 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 11. 기업투자(D-8) 다목 1) "국내에서 전문학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람" / 2) "외국에서 학사 이상의 학위를 취득한 사람".
- **citation**: 점수제 기술창업비자(D-8-4), 강남노무법인 https://k-labor.co.kr/main/klabor_04_view.html?pgubun=8&lang=ko&chapter_idx=443&items_idx=6906 ("총 368점 중 80점 이상의 점수를 득점"); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 11. 기업투자(D-8) 다목
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: D-8-4 (line 1135), `how.en`: change "60+ start-up points" to "80+ start-up points (out of 368)", and update the ru/uz/uz-cyrl strings to the same number.

### [medium] missing-edge: NONE -> F-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: NONE never offers F-1 (방문동거), and no other visa lists F-1 either, so the tool can never suggest it even though it is a normal first visa issued at a consulate.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 23. 방문동거(F-1) 가목 covers "친척 방문, 가족 동거, 피부양, 가사정리, 그 밖에 이와 유사한 목적", and 나목 covers domestic helpers of A-1/A-2 holders and of USD 500k / high-tech investors on D-8, F-2, F-5, F-6. These visas are applied for at the Korean mission abroad: the MOFA notice on F-1-5 (foreign parents/family of a marriage migrant, in force 2022.1.3) says the applicant obtains it from the "주소지 관할 재외공관". A graph scan of data/visamap.yaml confirms F-1 has zero inbound edges (it is unreachable from NONE), so the whole status is dead data.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 23. 방문동거(F-1) 가목·나목 (https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf); 주네팔 대한민국 대사관, 결혼이민자의 외국인 부모 등 가족(F-1-5) 사증 발급 관련 안내 https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/np-ko/brd/m_1730/view.do?seq=1344338
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: NONE.next (around line 1534, next to F-6-1/F-6-2/F-2-2): add `- F-1`. Leave F-1's own gating empty as it is now (line 1286), since F-1 covers relative visits, dependants and domestic helpers alike.

### [medium] wrong-note: NONE -> F-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: F-3's `how` says "Spouse or minor child of a D, E or F-2 status holder", which wrongly promises F-3 to the families of E-9, E-10 and D-3 holders.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 25. 동반(F-3): "문화예술(D-1)부터 특정활동(E-7)까지의 체류자격에 해당하는 사람의 배우자 및 미성년 자녀로서 배우자가 없는 사람 [기술연수(D-3) 체류자격에 해당하는 사람은 제외한다]". The range stops at E-7, so 비전문취업(E-9) and 선원취업(E-10) - the largest groups of E-visa holders in Korea - are excluded, and D-3 is excluded by name. Conversely the manual extends F-3 to families the `how` omits: the repo's own visadocs F-3 abroad list reads "Spouse or unmarried minor child of a D-1, D-2, D-4-E-7, F-2, F-4 or H-2 holder", with F-3-19 for F-4 families and F-3-20 for H-2 families. Telling an E-9 worker their family can follow on F-3 is a factual error, not a simplification.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 25. 동반(F-3) (https://wiki.kcce.or.kr/images/1/1a/%EC%B6%9C%EC%9E%85%EA%B5%AD%EA%B4%80%EB%A6%AC%EB%B2%95%EC%8B%9C%ED%96%89%EB%A0%B9_%EB%B3%84%ED%91%9C1-2.pdf); 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 31. 동반(F-3) › 공관장 재량으로 발급할 수 있는 사증
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: F-3 (line 1298), `how`: replace "Spouse or minor child of a D, E or F-2 status holder" with "Spouse or unmarried minor child of a D-1 to E-7 holder (not D-3, E-9 or E-10), or of an F-2, F-4 or H-2 holder" in all four languages.

### [medium] wrong-needs: NONE -> D-8-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: D-8-1 is gated with `needs: {invest: 1}`, which blocks the essential-professional (필수전문인력) half of the status - staff posted to a foreign-invested Korean company who personally invest nothing - even though the file's own `how` says the visa covers them.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 11. 기업투자(D-8) 가목 defines the status as "「외국인투자 촉진법」에 따른 외국인투자기업의 경영·관리 또는 생산·기술 분야에 종사하려는 필수전문인력으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람(국내에서 채용하는 사람은 제외한다)" - the KRW 100M / 10% threshold is the company's foreign investment under the Foreign Investment Promotion Act, not a personal requirement of every D-8-1 applicant. The repo's own ground truth agrees: visadocs target D-8-1 abroad variant is "Investor / essential staff of a foreign-invested Korean corporation". The investment select in layouts/visa-map.html offers an explicit "None" option (i18n vm_invest_0), so a reader who answers None is really blocked, not left unknown.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 11. 기업투자(D-8) 가목; data/visadocs.yaml targets.D-8-1.abroad (사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 16. 기업투자(D-8) › 공관장 재량 › 1.)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: D-8-1 (line 1097): replace `needs: {invest: 1}` with `needs: [{invest: 1}, {edu: 3}, {experience: 5}]` so the essential-staff route is not hidden from readers who answer "no investment"; keep the KRW 100M / 10% figures in `how`.

### [medium] wrong-needs: NONE -> F-5-11

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: F-5-11 (permanent residence for outstanding ability) has no `needs`, no `age` and no `countries`, so the tool shows every reader - including a 20-year-old with no degree - an OPEN, direct route from no visa at all to permanent residence.
- **evidence**: F-5-11 corresponds to 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제9호, "과학·경영·교육·문화예술·체육 등 특정 분야에서 탁월한 능력이 있는 사람 중 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람", and is scored on a separate 특정분야 능력 점수표 whose mandatory block (세계적 저명인사, Nobel-class prizes, former heads of state, executives of world-class corporations, etc.) must score at least 30 points, with 50+ points in total. Nothing in the engine filters that, and because any F-5 code is a goal node the path ends there, so the reader is shown a completed route they cannot take. The file's own `how` ("outstanding ability ... proven by a points test") is accurate; only the gating is missing.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의3 제9호 (https://www.law.go.kr/flDownload.do?flSeq=53439633); F-5-11 영주권: 특정 분야 능력 소유자, 점수표 (필수항목 30점 이상, 합산 50점 이상) https://immikorea.com/f-5-11/
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: F-5-11 (line 1412): add a gate, e.g. `needs: [{edu: 5}, {rank: 3, experience: 10}, {experience: 10, edu: 4}]`, so that only readers who claim a doctorate, or a top-100 degree plus a long career, see it as OPEN; readers who leave those details blank still get "maybe" under the engine's unknown-never-blocks rule.

### [medium] wrong-needs: NONE -> F-2-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: The NONE -> F-2-3 move overrides the visa's needs with `needs: {spouse: f5}`, so a minor child of an F-5 permanent resident who answers "parent: F-5 holder" is blocked from F-2-3, and because F-3 requires `parent: foreigner` that reader is left with no family route out of NONE at all.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 가목 grants F-2 to "국민의 미성년 외국인 자녀 또는 별표 1의3 영주(F-5) 체류자격을 가지고 있는 사람의 배우자 및 그의 미성년 자녀" - the minor child of an F-5 holder is entitled to the status in their own right, and the repo's own F-2-3 change and extend lists both carry a "Minor child of an F-5 holder" variant. The only support for the override is that the consul's-discretion section of the visa manual (사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 공관장 재량 › 1.) lists just the spouse; the child route runs through a 사증발급인정서 (CCVI) instead, which is still a visa from abroad. The engine's `parent` fact only applies to readers under 19, exactly the people this override excludes.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 24. 거주(F-2) 가목; data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-2-3.change / .extend (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 30. 거주(F-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 2. 영주자격 소지자의 배우자 및 미성년 자녀)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: NONE.next (line 1537-1538): change the F-2-3 entry from `- {to: F-2-3, needs: {spouse: f5}}` to `- {to: F-2-3, needs: [{spouse: f5}, {parent: f5}]}` (or drop the override so the visa's own needs apply), and add a note that the child's visa is issued on a CCVI rather than at the consul's discretion.

### [low] missing-edge: NONE -> F-2-T1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The accompanying family of a Top-Tier talent (F-2-T1) is never offered: NONE does not list it and no other visa does either, so F-2-T1 and the F-5-T1 that follows it are unreachable.
- **evidence**: The Top-Tier scheme gives the spouse and minor children a residence (F-2) status with free employment, not F-3, and the manual documents visa issuance for them: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 › Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등 › 톱티어 거주 동반가족(배우자·미성년자녀) › "사증발급·체류자격 변경·체류기간 연장" (the repo filed this section under `extend` only, which is why the graph has no route in). F-5-T1 is recorded in the same repo data as change+abroad, and it is reachable only from F-2-T1, so both nodes are dead. A graph scan of data/visamap.yaml confirms F-2-T1 has zero inbound edges.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 39. 톱티어(Top-Tier) 비자 › Ⅳ. 동반 가족 등 (as recorded in data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-2-T1); Top-Tier manual summary https://yoonhjs.com/living_in_korea/%EC%B2%A8%EB%8B%A8%EB%B6%84%EC%95%BC-%ED%95%B4%EC%99%B8-%EC%9D%B8%EC%9E%AC-%ED%83%91%ED%8B%B0%EC%96%B4top-tier-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EB%A7%A4%EB%89%B4%EC%96%BCf2d10e7-visa/
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml: add `- F-2-T1` to visas: NONE.next (next to the existing `- F-2-T`, around line 1543); F-2-T1's own `needs: [{spouse: foreigner}, {parent: foreigner}]` (line 1281) already gates it correctly. Also add `- F-2-T1` to visas: F-2-T.next so the family route exists from inside Korea.

### [low] wrong-note: NONE -> F-6-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: F-6-2's `how` claims the status is only for after "the marriage to a Korean citizen ended", and the gating `spouse: [exKorean, none]` enforces that; the status actually belongs to any parent raising a child born of a marriage or de facto marriage with a Korean national.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 27. 결혼이민(F-6) 나목: "국민과 혼인관계(사실상의 혼인관계를 포함한다)에서 출생한 자녀를 양육하고 있는 부 또는 모로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람" - there is no requirement that the relationship has ended. The repo's own ground truth matches: the F-6-2 change variant is "Parent on a status other than F-6 who is raising in Korea a minor child born of a (de facto) marriage with a Korean national". A reader in a 사실혼 relationship with a Korean who answers "spouse: Korean" is therefore pushed to F-6-1, which does require a legally registered marriage, and is blocked from the status that actually fits them.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2, 27. 결혼이민(F-6) 나목; data/visadocs.yaml targets.F-6-2.change (외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 33. 결혼이민(F-6) › 자녀 양육자에 대한 체류자격(F-6-2) 변경허가)
- **fix**: data/visamap.yaml, visas: F-6-2 (line 1332): change `needs: {koreanChild: true, spouse: [exKorean, none]}` to `needs: {koreanChild: true}` and reword `how` to "Raising in Korea your minor child born of a marriage, including a de facto marriage, with a Korean citizen; legally married spouses normally use F-6-1 instead." in all four languages.

## graph / none-study

### [medium] wrong-gives: D-2-7 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: D-2-7 (일-학습연계유학) has no 'gives', so the engine never credits the Korean degree it awards, and the NONE -> D-2-7 -> D-10-1 step the data itself advertises is BLOCKED for the typical user.
- **evidence**: D-2-7 is a regular degree course (전문학사 이상 정규 학위과정) at a Korean university linked to employment, so a graduate holds a Korean associate/bachelor degree; the repo's own note on the D-2-7 -> D-10-1 move even says 'After graduating from a Korean college or university: the first year is points-exempt'. But D-2-7 carries no gives (verified: zero 'gives:' keys between lines for D-2-7 in data/visamap.yaml), while the sibling degree visas do (D-2-1 gives {edu:2,koreanEdu:2}, D-2-2 {edu:3,koreanEdu:3}). Engine trace (assets/js/visamap.js:107-110, 118-129, 185-189): a reader who answers 'High school' and does not tick 'From a Korean university' gets known.edu=1 AND known.koreanEdu=0 (koreanEdu is explicitly set to 0, not left unknown). With no gives on the path, D-10-1's needs [{edu:3},{koreanEdu:2},{koreanEdu:1,ageUnder:25}] all evaluate 'no', so the option is hidden behind the blocked list with an education reason. The same reader is also blocked from E-7-1 once they answer any experience value.
- **citation**: 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침 (법무부), D-2-7 일-학습연계유학 = 국내 대학의 전문학사 이상 정규 학위과정 (as summarised at https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf and the subcode table at https://hanvisa.net/korea-visa-%EC%B2%B4%EB%A5%98%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%A9%EB%B3%84-%EC%95%BD%ED%98%B8%EB%AA%85%EC%B9%AD-%ED%95%9C%EA%B5%AD%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90%EC%A2%85%EB%A5%98/); scratchpad/engine.md section 'gives'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-2-7 add a gives block, at the same indent as its 'next:' key: gives: {edu: 2, koreanEdu: 2} (associate level is the conservative floor, since D-2-7 covers 전문학사 and 학사 programmes; the engine treats a gives level as a minimum that is raised by the reader's own answer).
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed at the primary source. The 법무부 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침 defines D-2-7 verbatim as "⑦ D-2-7(일-학습연계 2016.5.16. 신설) : 정부초청 장학생 등으로 선발되어 국내 대학 전문학사 이상의 정규 학위과정에서 유학하고자 하거나 재학중인 유학생" (scratchpad/students_guideline.txt lines 111-114), and the same guideline's D-2-7 자격변경 특례 table lists 학사/석사/박사 및 석박사통합 application timings (lines 731-740). So a D-2-7 holder is on a Korean 전문학사-or-higher degree course, and 전문학사 (edu/koreanEdu 2) is the correct conservative floor. The data defect and engine trace both check out: D-2-7 (data/visamap.yaml:480-495) has next, note and again but no gives, while D-2-1 has gives {edu:2,koreanEdu:2} and D-2-2 {edu:3,koreanEdu:3}; profileFacts (assets/js/visamap.js:111) sets known.koreanEdu = person.eduKorea ? person.edu : 0, i.e. hard 0 rather than unknown, so a reader answering 'High school' without the Korean-university box reaches D-10-1 with edu=1, koreanEdu=0 and all three of D-10-1's needs groups [{edu:3},{koreanEdu:2},{koreanEdu:1,ageUnder:25}] return 'no'. With gives {edu:2,koreanEdu:2} the {koreanEdu:2} group becomes yes via Math.max(0,2) in factsAt, so the fix works as written. One minor overstatement, not enough to refute: the side remark that the reader is 'blocked from E-7-1 once they answer any experience value' holds only for answers under 5, since E-7-1's {experience: 5} group opens at 5+ years.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: add `gives: {edu: 2, koreanEdu: 2}` to D-2-7 in data/visamap.yaml at the same indent as its `next:` key.

### [medium] wrong-gives: D-4-3 -> -

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-4-3 (Korean elementary/middle/high school) has no 'gives', so both of its only two exits (D-2-1, D-2-2) are blocked for exactly the people it is meant for: readers who have not yet finished high school.
- **evidence**: D-4-3 is enrolment at a Korean school below university level, so someone who completes it is a Korean high-school graduate (edu 1, koreanEdu 1). data/visamap.yaml line 285-289 gives D-4-3 only `next: [D-2-1, D-2-2]` and `age: [0, 19]`, with no gives. Both targets carry `needs: {edu: 1}`. Engine trace (assets/js/visamap.js:107-110, 134-146, 185-189): the form offers 'Below high school' as edu 0 (i18n/en.yaml vm_edu_0), and the reader age floor is 14 (visamap.js:265), so the realistic D-4-3 reader answers edu 0; checkFact then returns 'no' for edu>=1 and the step NONE -> D-4-3 -> D-2-1 / D-2-2 is blocked with an education reason. D-4-3 therefore becomes a dead end, and the Korean-schooling fact that later steps (D-10-1 {koreanEdu:1,ageUnder:25}, F-2-7 points) depend on is never recorded.

- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-4-3 (after `age: [0, 19]`) add: gives: {edu: 1, koreanEdu: 1}
