The repo's own KIS ground truth names the chapter '3. 고등학교 이하 외국인유학생(D-4-3) 자격으로 변경' (data/visadocs.yaml:5288-5290), which settles that D-4-3 is schooling at or below high-school level; scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt records D-4-3 as change,abroad,extend, so the status is fully documented. The data defect is as described: data/visamap.yaml:285-289 gives D-4-3 only `next: [D-2-1, D-2-2]` and `age: [0, 19]` and no gives, while both targets carry `needs: {edu: 1}` (D-2-1 at the yaml line carrying gives {edu:2,koreanEdu:2}, D-2-2 likewise with {edu:3,koreanEdu:3}). The engine trace is right: i18n/en.yaml:151-152 labels edu 0 'Below high school', the reader age floor is 14 (assets/js/visamap.js:265), so the in-range 14-19 reader who answers edu 0 gets checkFact edu>=1 = 'no' and both exits are blocked with a whyEducation reason, making D-4-3 a dead end. The gives is also sound under engine.md's rule that gives must describe the state after completing the status, since the only modelled exits (D-2-1, D-2-2) both presuppose high-school completion. ### [medium] wrong-needs: NONE -> D-4-1

- **state**: SURVIVED 2/2 (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: D-4-1's `needs: {edu: 1}` is too strict: the official eligibility for university language-institute training is 'currently enrolled at a school at or below high-school level OR holding a high-school diploma or above', so secondary-school students are eligible but the tool blocks them.
- **evidence**: The visa manual's 발급대상 for 어학연수 is verbatim '고등학교 이하의 학교 재학생 또는 고등학교 졸업 이상의 학력 소지자로서 대학 부설 어학원에서 어학연수를 하고자 하는 자', and the same page says D-4-7 외국어연수 has identical eligibility. The 2026 sojourn manual confirms secondary-school pupils do hold 어학연수 status: '어학연수생 중 초·중·고등학교 재학생(졸업예정자는 가능)은 시간제 취업허가의 대상에 포함되지 않으며'. The repo's own document data for the D-4-1 abroad list asks for '재학증명서 또는 최종학력입증서류' (enrolment certificate OR proof of highest education), i.e. enrolment is an accepted alternative. Engine trace: a 15-18 year old who answers 'Below high school' (edu 0) gets checkFact edu>=1 = 'no', so NONE -> D-4-1 is blocked with an education reason. The data is also internally inconsistent: D-4-7, which shares the same 발급대상, has no needs at all.
- **citation**: 사증 안내 '유학 및 일반연수', 어학연수(D-4-1/D-4-7) 발급대상 - https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/jp-sapporo-ko/brd/m_443/view.do?seq=1344329 ; 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 유학(D-2) › 체류자격외 활동 › 1. 외국인유학생 시간제 취업 › 나. 대상 (p.24) - https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217 ; data/visadocs.yaml doc key enrolmentCertificateOrProofOfHighestEduc = '재학증명서 또는 최종학력입증서류' (line 1118)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-4-1, delete the line `needs: {edu: 1}` (matching D-4-7, which already has none). If a floor is wanted for readers who answered nothing at all, keep it off the visa and leave the education expectation to the 'how' text.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed verbatim at two independent primary sources. The 법무부 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, chapter Ⅲ-2 어학연수(한국어연수: D-4-1, 외국어연수: D-4-7) 자격, section '나. 해당자', reads exactly: '고등학교 이하의 학교 재학생 또는 고등학교 졸업 이상의 학력 소지자로 상기 교육기관에서 어학연수를 하고자 하는 자 - 일반연수(D-4) 사증발급(한국어 연수생: D-4-1, 외국어 연수생: D-4-7)' (scratchpad/students_guideline.txt:568-571). I also fetched the MOFA Sapporo page the finding cites and it returns the same sentence word for word. Two things follow and both match the finding: enrolment at a school at or below high-school level is an independent qualifying ground, and D-4-1 and D-4-7 share one eligibility rule, so the data's asymmetry (D-4-1 `needs: {edu: 1}`, D-4-7 no needs) is internally inconsistent. The documents list in the same chapter ('바. 제출 서류 ... 재학증명서 또는 최종학력입증서류', same file line 621) confirms enrolment as an accepted alternative to proof of highest education, matching data/visadocs.yaml:1118. Engine trace verified: edu 0 is labelled 'Below high school' (i18n/en.yaml:152) and checkFact returns 'no' for edu>=1, so a secondary-school pupil is blocked with a whyEducation reason.
  - corrected fix: Deleting `needs: {edu: 1}` from D-4-1 is acceptable and restores parity with D-4-7. A more faithful encoding of the two-pronged 해당자 rule, if a floor is wanted for adults who answered edu 0, is `needs: [{edu: 1}, {ageUnder: 19}]` - the ageUnder branch stands in for '고등학교 이하의 학교 재학생'. Whichever is chosen, apply it to D-4-1 and D-4-7 alike, since the guideline gives them one eligibility rule.
  - citation: 법무부 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침 Ⅲ-2 어학연수(D-4-1/D-4-7) 나. 해당자 and 바. 제출서류 (scratchpad/students_guideline.txt:556-625); 주삿포로총영사관 사증안내 '유학 및 일반연수' https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/jp-sapporo-ko/brd/m_443/view.do?seq=1344329 (fetched: same 해당자 sentence verbatim); data/visadocs.yaml:1118
- **verdict (refute)**: upheld - Confirmed from the primary source itself. I fetched the MOFA 사증 안내 page cited and the 발급대상 for 어학연수 reads verbatim '고등학교 이하의 학교 재학생 또는 고등학교 졸업 이상의 학력 소지자로 상기 교육기관에서 어학연수를 하고자 하는 자', with D-4-7 grouped under the same eligibility. A currently enrolled secondary pupil is therefore eligible, and in this form their highest COMPLETED education is 'Below high school' = edu 0, so `needs: {edu: 1}` blocks them: my run of the engine's own functions returns {result:'no', keys:['edu']} for NONE -> D-4-1. I also extracted the official 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 유학(D-2) chapter and it independently confirms that school pupils do hold 어학연수 status: '어학연수생 중 초․중․고등학교 재학생(졸업예정자는 가능)은 시간제취업허가의 대상에 포함되지 않으며'. The internal inconsistency is real too: D-4-7, with identical 발급대상, carries no needs. Deleting the line is the right fix - D-4-1 has no age gate and the rule admits middle-school pupils as well, so no edu floor is defensible.
  - corrected claim: Accurate as written.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: delete `needs: {edu: 1}` from D-4-1, matching D-4-7.
  - citation: 사증 안내 유학 및 일반연수, 어학연수 발급대상: '고등학교 이하의 학교 재학생 또는 고등학교 졸업 이상의 학력 소지자로 상기 교육기관에서 어학연수를 하고자 하는 자' (https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/jp-sapporo-ko/brd/m_443/view.do?seq=1344329, fetched); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 유학(D-2) p.24, 시간제 취업 › 나. 대상 (https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217); data/visamap.yaml:270 (needs) vs D-4-7 at 304-312

### [medium] missing-edge: NONE -> D-4-2

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: NONE does not offer D-4-2 (기타기관연수 / general training, incl. company internships and K-Trainee), even though it is a normal first visa from abroad; as a result D-4-2 is unreachable from every node in the graph.
- **evidence**: D-4-2 is fully defined in data/visamap.yaml (line 274, with name, how and a six-item next list) but `grep -n "D-4-2" data/visamap.yaml` returns only that definition line: no visa's next list contains it, so the page can never offer it to anyone. The real routes are (a) from abroad: the inviting Korean body files a 사증발급인정서 (CCVI) and the trainee collects the D-4-2 visa at a consulate, and (b) in Korea: graduates of a Korean university changing to 일반연수(D-4-2) for an internship. The 2026 sojourn manual has a live D-4-2 chapter (the company-tailored internship K-Trainee, D-4-2K), which data/visadocs.yaml itself cites, so the status is current. Since every move out of NONE is treated as from abroad (engine.md), adding the edge needs no 'abroad' flag.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 12. 일반연수(D-4) › 체류기간 연장허가 › 2. 기업 맞춤형 인턴십(K-Trainee, D-4-2K) (cited in data/visadocs.yaml:5273-5276); D-4-2 기타기관연수: 원칙적으로 사증발급인정서 필요, 국내 대학 졸업(예정)자의 체류자격변경 대상 - https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/d4-visa-d-4-2-general-training-eligibility-and-required-documents-for-inviting-foreign-employees
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under NONE: next, add `- D-4-2` immediately after `- D-4-1`. To fix the unreachability inside Korea as well, add `- D-4-2` to the next lists of D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3 and D-2-4 (the Korean-graduate internship route). If the owner prefers not to model it, delete the D-4-2 block instead of leaving it orphaned.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The data defect is real but the stated justification and the primary fix are not supported by the citation given. Verified real: `grep -c "D-4-2" data/visamap.yaml` returns exactly 1 - its own definition at line 274 - so no visa's next list reaches it and D-4-2 (name, how, and a six-item next list) is dead data. Not supported: the finding's only primary citation is the manual's 체류기간 연장허가 › 2. 기업 맞춤형 인턴십(K-Trainee, D-4-2K) chapter, an EXTENSION chapter for people who already hold the status; an extension list cannot establish an entry edge. The repo's own ground truth cuts directly against the proposed edges: scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt records 'D-4-2: extend' alone, while every sibling (D-4-1, D-4-3, D-4-6, D-4-7) carries change,abroad,extend, and data/visadocs.yaml:5276 adds its own notice that 'the manual gives no extension list for other D-4-2 trainees'. Under the file's stated convention ('a visa with NO change list is one the manual does not document a status change to', and abroad = embassy issuance documented), the manual documents no route into D-4-2 at all. The CCVI-from-abroad and Korean-graduate-internship routes rest only on allvisakorea.com, a commercial site; I fetched it and it does say a CCVI is required and that a domestic-graduate status change is possible but 'rare', since most such graduates take D-10 - which is weaker than 'a normal first visa from abroad' and argues against adding D-2-1..D-2-4 -> D-4-2 edges.
  - corrected claim: D-4-2 is an orphan node: it is defined in data/visamap.yaml:274-284 and appears in no `next` list, so the page can never offer it. But the claim that it is 'a normal first visa from abroad' is not supported by the cited source or by the repo's own KIS ground truth, which documents only an extension list for D-4-2 (and only for K-Trainee D-4-2K) - no issuance-from-abroad and no change-of-status chapter, unlike every other D-4 subcode.
  - corrected fix: Take the finding's own alternative rather than its primary fix: delete the orphaned D-4-2 block from data/visamap.yaml (lines 274-284). Do not add `- D-4-2` to NONE: next or to the D-2-1..D-2-4 next lists on this evidence - the manual documents neither an 사증발급인정서 nor a 체류자격 변경 route into D-4-2, so such edges would be the only ones in the file with no documented source. If the owner wants D-4-2 kept, it needs a fresh primary citation (a 사증발급 지침 entry or a HiKorea/visa.go.kr issuance page), not the K-Trainee extension chapter.
  - citation: scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt ('D-4-2: extend'; header: change = status change documented, abroad = embassy issuance documented); data/visadocs.yaml:5273-5276 (only an extension list, for K-Trainee D-4-2K, plus the author's notice); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1-2 item 7 일반연수(D-4) (scratchpad/byl_02.txt:29-33, which defines D-4 broadly but names no subcodes); allvisakorea.com D-4-2 page (fetched: CCVI required, domestic-graduate status change 'rare')
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The grep is right (no next list contains D-4-2), but the harm claim misreads the engine. The current-visa search box is built from `Object.keys(visas).filter((code) => !isGoal(code) && code !== NONE)` (visamap.js:248-251), so a reader who already holds D-4-2 can select it as their starting status and use its whole next list. D-4-2 is therefore neither dead code nor invisible: the page CAN offer it, just not as a next step. That also makes the alternative fix ('delete the D-4-2 block') actively wrong - it would remove a selectable current status. On the substance, the in-repo KIS ground truth I was pointed at records D-4-2 as `extend` only, i.e. the manuals as extracted give D-4-2 neither a 사증발급 list nor a 체류자격 변경 list, and the one extension list the repo holds is for K-Trainee (D-4-2K) with the explicit notice that 'the manual gives no extension list for other D-4-2 trainees'. So neither proposed edge is backed by the repo's own document data: adding NONE -> D-4-2 (or D-2-x -> D-4-2) would create steps the visa-docs page cannot document. I could not confirm a D-4-2 CCVI chapter from any primary source myself (hikorea returned an error page, the visa manual is not reachable, search budget exhausted); the only confirmation was the finding's own commercial agency page, which simultaneously says the status-change route is 'rare' because graduates take D-10 instead. Treating D-4-2 as a status one may already hold rather than an entry route is a defensible scope choice, not a defect of this severity.
  - corrected claim: NONE (and every other node) lists no D-4-2 edge, so the only way a reader reaches D-4-2 in the tool is by naming it as their current visa in the search box - which works. Whether to also model it as a first visa from abroad is a scope decision, and the repo's own KIS ground truth has no 사증발급 or 변경 list for D-4-2 to back such a step.
  - corrected fix: Nothing is required. If the owner does want the entry route, add only `- D-4-2` under NONE: next (moves out of NONE are already treated as from abroad) and first extract the 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 › 일반연수(D-4) › 사증발급인정서 발급대상 section for D-4-2 so the step has a document list. Do not add D-2-1..D-2-4 -> D-4-2 (undocumented and rare), and do not delete the D-4-2 block.
  - citation: assets/js/visamap.js:248-251 (search codes = all non-goal visas) and 262-273 (any such code is a valid start); scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt line 27 'D-4-2: extend'; data/visadocs.yaml:5273-5287 (K-Trainee-only extension list + notice); https://www.allvisakorea.com/en/post/d4-visa-d-4-2-general-training-eligibility-and-required-documents-for-inviting-foreign-employees ('since most domestic university graduates obtain a D-10 ... it is rare to apply for a D-4 visa again')

### [low] wrong-needs: NONE -> D-2-5

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: D-2-5's second needs alternative is a bare `{stem: true}`, so any reader who ticks 'Science, engineering or IT' is offered the research-study visa (and even tagged as a match) regardless of education, although the rule requires a master's degree in principle.
- **evidence**: The sojourn manual's D-2-5 document row reads '최종학력 입증서류(석사학위 이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)'. The only undergraduate opening is narrow: a Korean university in the QS top 500 / THE top 200 may invite a STEM student who is currently enrolled in a bachelor's programme abroad, i.e. the person has at least completed secondary school and must hold an invitation from a designated university. The data expresses none of that. Engine trace (visamap.js:104-115, 148-160, 185-192): needs is a list so any one group suffices; known.stem is set whenever edu is answered, so a reader answering 'High school' + STEM satisfies {stem: true} -> result 'yes', the option is OPEN and, because stem is in PERSONAL, it also prints the '✓ matches' tag. That advertises a visa the person cannot get.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 다. 제출서류 › 특정 연구과정(D-2-5) (p.29) - https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217 ; 법무부 연구유학생(D-2-5), 연구원(E-3) 비자 확대 (QS 500위/THE 200위 대학의 이공계 국외 학사과정 재학생 초청) - https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=448
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-2-5 change the needs list from `- {edu: 4}` / `- {stem: true}` to `- {edu: 4}` / `- {stem: true, edu: 1}`, and add a note on the visa stating that the undergraduate route applies only to STEM students invited by a QS top-500 / THE top-200 Korean university (master's degree or higher otherwise).
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - Confirmed, and the real-world rule is in fact stricter than the finding states. Two primary sources: (1) the 법무부 유학생 지침 defines the status as '⑤ D-2-5(연구과정) : 대학 및 연구기관에서 석·박사 학위를 소지하고 전문분야의 연구를 수행하면서 실험실습 및 논문작성을 위하여 연구하는 유학생' (scratchpad/students_guideline.txt:105-108) - i.e. holding a master's or doctorate, not merely 'in principle'; (2) the 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 D-2 chapter, 체류자격 변경허가, 특정 연구과정(D-2-5) row p.29, reads '⑤ 최종학력 입증서류(석사학위이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)' (scratchpad/ajou.txt:180-184), and the chapter's 붙임 4 외국인 연구생 확인서 form offers only '학위 석사/박사' (same file, lines ~430-432). The narrow undergraduate opening is also real: I fetched the cited article and it states '세계 대학평가 상위권에 속하는 국내대학의 경우, 이공계 분야 국외 학사과정 재학생을 연구유학생(D-2-5)으로 초청할 수 있도록 하였고', limited to Korean universities in the QS top 500 or THE top 200 (previously only KAIST/DGIST/UNIST/GIST/UST). Engine trace verified and the defect is worse than claimed: needs is a list so any one group suffices (checkNeeds, visamap.js:148-160), profileFacts sets known.stem = person.stem whenever edu is answered (line 111-112), and stem is a non-LEVEL fact so checkFact returns 'yes' outright - so even a reader answering edu 0 ('Below high school') plus the STEM box satisfies the bare {stem: true} group, gets the option OPEN, and because stem is in PERSONAL (visamap.js:93) also gets the '✓ matches' tag.
  - corrected claim: The finding understates the rule and the blast radius. The guideline requires the applicant to HOLD a master's or doctorate ('석·박사 학위를 소지하고'), and the bare {stem: true} group admits not only high-school answers but edu 0 answers too, because stem is a non-level fact evaluated independently of edu.
  - corrected fix: As proposed: change D-2-5's needs to `- {edu: 4}` / `- {stem: true, edu: 1}` and add a note saying the status requires a master's degree or higher, with the only undergraduate route being a STEM student currently enrolled in a bachelor's programme abroad invited by a Korean university in the QS top 500 or THE top 200. Note `edu: 1` is the tightest available floor rather than an exact fit: the real exception also requires current bachelor-course enrolment and an invitation from a designated university, which the fact vocabulary cannot express. Do not reach for the `rank` fact here - in this data rank describes the reader's own university, whereas the rule ranks the INVITING Korean university.
  - citation: 법무부 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, 세부약호 정의 ⑤ D-2-5 연구과정 (scratchpad/students_guideline.txt:105-108); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 유학(D-2) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 다. 제출서류 › 특정 연구과정(D-2-5), p.29, and 붙임 4 외국인 연구생 확인서 (scratchpad/ajou.txt:180-184 and ~425-445); https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=448 (fetched: QS top 500 / THE top 200, 이공계 국외 학사과정 재학생); assets/js/visamap.js:93, 104-115, 148-160, 185-192
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The rule citation checks out - I extracted the manual page and it reads '특정 연구과정(D-2-5) ⑤ 최종학력 입증서류(석사학위이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)', and the undergraduate opening (QS top-500 / THE top-200 Korean universities inviting 이공계 분야 국외 학사과정 재학생) is confirmed. But the engine trace and the fix are both wrong. readDetails (visamap.js:236-243) sets `const extras = details.edu >= 1; details.stem = extras && f.stem.checked;` - so a reader who answers 'Below high school' has stem forced to FALSE no matter what they ticked. Running the real logic: edu 0 + STEM ticked already yields {result:'no', keys:['edu']} for D-2-5, and with the proposed `{stem: true, edu: 1}` it yields the same 'no'. The fix is a pure no-op: {stem:true} can only evaluate 'yes' when edu >= 1 already, so the claim 'regardless of education' is false and the suggested change buys nothing. Worse, any tighter floor would be wrong in the other direction: the undergraduate exception covers students currently enrolled in a bachelor's course abroad, whose highest completed education is high school (edu 1-2), so {stem: true} is already the closest thing this engine can express for that population. The engine has no 'currently enrolled' or 'invited by a ranked Korean university' fact (its `rank` fact is the reader's own degree-granting university), so the residual looseness is not encodable.
  - corrected claim: D-2-5's `{stem: true}` alternative opens the visa (with a match tag) to any reader who answered high school or above and ticked STEM, although the manual requires a master's degree in principle and the undergraduate route needs an invitation from a QS top-500 / THE top-200 Korean university. Readers who answered 'Below high school' are already blocked, because the form only reads the STEM box when edu >= 1. The gap is an information gap, not a gating gap.
  - corrected fix: Leave `needs: - {edu: 4}` / `- {stem: true}` as it is, and add only the note: master's degree or higher in principle; the STEM route applies to students invited by a QS top-500 / THE top-200 Korean university while enrolled in a bachelor's programme abroad.
  - citation: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 유학(D-2) p.29: '특정 연구과정(D-2-5) ⑤ 최종학력 입증서류(석사학위이상 소지자를 원칙으로 함)' (https://www.ajou.ac.kr/se_en/board/news.do?mode=download&articleNo=288573&attachNo=269217, extracted); 법무부 D-2-5 확대: 'QS 세계대학순위 500위 이내 또는 THE 평판도 랭킹 200대에 속하는 국내대학 ... 이공계 분야 국외 학사과정 재학생' (https://www.studykoreanews.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=448); assets/js/visamap.js:236-243 (extras = edu >= 1 gates stem/eduKorea/rank), 107-117

### [low] wrong-age: NONE -> D-4-3

- **state**: SURVIVED 1/2 (auditor confidence 0.4)
- **claim**: D-4-3's hard age cap of 19 blocks older applicants, but the manual gates D-4-3 on school enrolment and a guardian, not on age.
- **evidence**: The D-4-3 발급대상 is defined by the institution (초·중·고등학교, 외국인학교, 외국교육기관) plus a 후견인 (guardian) with proven finances, with a guardian exemption for high-school students living in the school dormitory; no age ceiling appears in the eligibility or the document list. Foreign students do enrol in Korean high schools at 19-20 after grade placement. Because `age` is a hard gate in the engine (visamap.js:176-182), a 20-year-old entering a Korean high school is told the visa is age-restricted. This is negative evidence (absence of an age rule) rather than a positive citation of a different limit, hence the low confidence.
- **citation**: 일반연수(D-4-3) 사증발급 대상 및 후견인 요건 (초·중등교육법상 학교, 외국인학교, 외국교육기관; 후견보증서·후견인 재정능력 입증) - https://immikorea.com/d-4-3/ and 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 일반연수(D-4) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3. 고등학교 이하 외국인유학생(D-4-3) (cited in data/visadocs.yaml:5288), neither of which states an age limit
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under D-4-3 change `age: [0, 19]` to `age: [0, 21]`, or drop the `age` line and rely on the 'how' text, since the real gate is school enrolment plus a guardian.
- **verdict (sources)**: upheld - The mechanical half of the claim is exactly right: assess() in assets/js/visamap.js:176-182 reads `visa.age` as a hard gate (`person.age > max` -> BLOCKED with the 'age only' reason), and data/visamap.yaml:289 sets `age: [0, 19]` on D-4-3, so a 20-year-old standing on NONE (the NONE -> D-4-3 edge exists, graph.txt:1400) is told the visa is age-restricted. The source half also holds. The in-repo ground truth for D-4-3 (data/visadocs.yaml:5288-5380, sourced to 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 일반연수(D-4) › 사증발급인정서 발급대상 › 3. 고등학교 이하 교육기관 유학생에 대한 일반연수(D-4-3) and 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 일반연수(D-4) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3.) gates the status on the institution (초·중등교육법상 학교 / 외국인학교 / 외국교육기관 business-registration copy, 교장 입학허가서 Annex 1), proof of prior education, paid school fees, living-cost funds held 3+ months, and 후견보증서 (Annex 2) with 후견인 재정능력 for the 21 high-overstay countries, plus a guardian exemption evidenced by 기숙사 입사확인서 (onlyKo: 후견인 면제 대상자). No age document, no age condition, and no age-based variant appears anywhere in change/abroad/extend. An independent D-4-3 eligibility writeup confirms the gates are institution + guardian (guardian income GNI x1 ~ KRW 37.5M or assets KRW 200M+, max 2 students per guardian, waived for high-school dorm residents) and states no maximum age. 일반연수(D-4) in Enforcement Decree table 1 is likewise an activity/institution-based status and the -3 sub-code split is a manual artefact, not a decree one. Two caveats that lower but do not break the finding: (a) the 19 cap is a plausible UI heuristic rather than a transcription error, and the affected population (over-19 K-12 enrolees) is small, so 'low' severity is correct; (b) the finding's supporting assertion that 'foreign students do enrol in Korean high schools at 19-20 after grade placement' is uncited and I could not verify it from a primary source - 초·중등교육법 imposes no age ceiling on high-school admission, which is consistent with it, but the finding's own numeric proposal of 21 has no source behind it either. So the defect is real (an unsourced hard gate that can produce a wrong block) while the replacement number is a judgement call, not a sourced value.
  - corrected claim: D-4-3's `age: [0, 19]` is a hard engine gate (visamap.js:176-182) with no basis in either manual: the KIS 사증발급/체류 manual sections for 고등학교 이하 교육기관 유학생(D-4-3) condition the status on enrolment at a 초·중등교육법 school / 외국인학교 / 외국교육기관 plus a 후견인 with proven finances (waived for high-school dormitory residents), and 일반연수(D-4) in Enforcement Decree table 1 is activity-based with no age clause. The cap is therefore an unsourced heuristic rather than a legal rule, and it wrongly BLOCKS over-19 K-12 enrolees. The finding's replacement value of 21 is, however, equally unsourced - no official text supplies any D-4-3 age number.
  - corrected fix: In data/visamap.yaml:289, widen `age: [0, 19]` to `age: [0, 21]` (the author's first option) as a stated heuristic, since no source supplies a number and some age bound keeps the engine from offering a K-12 school-student visa to adults. Do not drop the `age` line outright: with no soft-gate mechanism in assess(), removal makes D-4-3 show as OPEN for a 45-year-old, trading one inaccuracy for another. Either way the real gates belong in the `how` text, e.g. 'Enrolment at a Korean elementary, middle or high school (or a foreign school / foreign educational institution), with a guardian who can show sufficient finances - waived for high-school students living in the school dormitory.'
  - citation: data/visadocs.yaml:5288-5380 (D-4-3 change/abroad/extend, sourced to 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 일반연수(D-4) › 사증발급인정서 발급대상 › 3. 고등학교 이하 교육기관 유학생에 대한 일반연수(D-4-3) 자격 사증발급인정서 발급 › 바. 첨부서류, and 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 일반연수(D-4) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3. / 체류기간 연장허가 › 3.) - document set is 입학허가서 + 학교 사업자등록증 + 후견보증서(별지 2) + 후견인 재정능력 + 기숙사 입사확인서 (후견인 면제 대상자), with no age item; scratchpad/visadocs-targets.txt:28 (D-4-3: change,abroad,extend); data/visamap.yaml:285-289 (`age: [0, 19]`); assets/js/visamap.js:176-182 (hard age block); https://immikorea.com/d-4-3/ (후견인 1인당 2명 이내, 연간소득 GNI 1배 3,750만원 이상 또는 자산 2억 이상, dormitory waiver; explicitly no age limit stated). law.go.kr 별표 1-2/1-3, hikorea.go.kr and visa.go.kr returned JS shells or 404s to WebFetch and the session's WebSearch quota was exhausted, so the decree table was not read first-hand.
- **verdict (refute)**: REFUTED - The finding rests on admitted negative evidence (no numeric ceiling printed in the 발급대상 list) and I could not confirm from any source that an adult is eligible for D-4-3, so by default it fails. Positive counter-evidence points the other way: the source the finding itself cites says the guardian system exists "유학생이 미성년자이기 때문에" (because the student is a minor), and the Korea Immigration Service 1345 민원 blog describes D-4 study status at this level as "고등학교 이하 미성년자의 유학비자" - a study visa for minors at high school or below, with parents/guardians following as accompanying family. 미성년 in Korea is 만 19세 미만, so `age: [0, 19]` in data/visamap.yaml:289 is a faithful encoding of that framing, not a fabricated cap. The in-repo ground truth reinforces this: the D-4-3 변경 document list (data/visadocs.yaml, the 자비부담 variant) requires 후견보증서 (Annex 2) "even when a parent/close relative is guardian" for every self-funded applicant, with the single exemption being high-school dormitory residents. A 후견인 under Korean civil law is a guardian of a minor (an adult would fall under 성년후견, incapacity guardianship), so the very requirement the finding treats as the "real gate" presupposes minority rather than replacing an age rule. The engine reading is correctly described (visa.age is a hard gate, visamap.js:176-182), but a blocked option is not a silent error: it is shown behind the "show N" toggle with the reason "age 0–19", so a 20-year-old reader is given the stated range rather than misled, and the 20-year-old first-time Korean high-school enrollee is an undocumented edge case. Finally the proposed fix is itself unsupported: no source gives 21, and dropping the `age` line would offer a K-12 school visa as an OPEN option to readers of any age and open its `next` route (D-2-1/D-2-2) to adults, which is a worse error than the one alleged.
  - corrected claim: Accurate part: the D-4-3 eligibility and document lists state no explicit numeric age ceiling; they gate on the institution (초·중등교육법 schools, 외국인학교, 외국교육기관) plus a 후견인 with proven finances. Inaccurate part: that absence does not make 19 the wrong number. Immigration guidance frames D-4-3 as the study status for 미성년자 at high school or below, and the mandatory 후견인 (a minor's guardian in Korean civil law) carries the same implication, so age: [0, 19] is a defensible proxy for "minor", not a wrong age gate.
  - corrected fix: No change to `age`. Leave `age: [0, 19]` on D-4-3 in data/visamap.yaml:289. If anything is worth improving it is the `how` text, which could mention the guardian (후견인) requirement and the high-school-dormitory exemption alongside school enrolment; do not widen or drop the age range on the strength of an absent ceiling.
  - citation: immikorea.com/d-4-3/ (fetched: "No specific age limit is mentioned... with a guardian system in place 'because the student is a minor' (유학생이 미성년자이기 때문에)"; guardian exemption only for high-school dorm residents); Korea Immigration Service 민원 blog m.blog.naver.com/minwon1345/224176457156 (snippet via search: D-4 is "고등학교 이하 미성년자의 유학비자", issued for minors in elementary/middle/high school with guardians as accompanying family); in-repo /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visadocs.yaml D-4-3 › change › 자비부담 variant (guardianSGuaranteeAnnex2Form "Needed even when a parent/close relative is guardian", exemption "후견인 면제 대상자 - high-school dormitory residents"), sourced to 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 일반연수(D-4) › 체류자격 변경허가 › 3. 고등학교 이하 외국인유학생(D-4-3); law.go.kr decree table could not be fetched (service-busy error page), so no statutory text contradicts the manual framing

## graph / none-work

### [medium] wrong-age: NONE -> D-10-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.8)
- **claim**: D-10-3's flat `age: [18, 34]` gate offers the high-tech intern visa to 30-34 year olds who hold only a bachelor's degree, for whom the real cap is under 30.
- **evidence**: The KIS manual's 구직(D-10) chapter states the D-10-3 (첨단기술인턴) applicant must be "학위취득일로부터 3년이 경과되지 않은 졸업생으로 만 30세(석사 이상의 경우 만 35세) 미만인 자" - i.e. under 30, and under 35 only with a master's or higher. I extracted this verbatim from the manual chapter PDF (구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 3) 첨단기술인턴(D-10-3), and the same wording appears in the chapter's 해당자 section: "만 30세 미만으로(석사 이상 : 만 35세)". The visamap's own `how` text states the rule correctly ("under 30 (under 35 with a master's)"), so only the machine-readable gate is wrong. Since `age` is a hard gate and `needs` has an `ageUnder` fact, the conditional rule is expressible.
- **citation**: 사증발급/외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) 활동범위·해당자 and 체류자격변경허가 › 3) 첨단기술인턴(D-10-3), pp.140-143 - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do
- **fix**: On visa D-10-3: replace `age: [18, 34]` with `age: [18]` and replace `needs: {stem: true, rank: 1}` with `needs: [{stem: true, rank: 1, ageUnder: 30}, {stem: true, rank: 1, edu: 4, ageUnder: 35}]`.

### [medium] missing-edge: NONE -> E-6-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: NONE's `next` offers E-6-1 and E-6-3 but not E-6-2, even though E-6-2 is a normal first visa issued from abroad; E-6-2 has no incoming edge anywhere in the graph, so the tool can never reach it.
- **evidence**: E-6-2 (호텔·유흥업소 공연/연예) is issued from abroad on the ordinary E-6 route: the Korean inviter obtains a 사증발급인정서 (CCVI) from an immigration office on the strength of the 영상물등급위원회 공연추천서 + 연예활동계획서 + 자격증명서 또는 3년 이상 경력증명서, and the performer then collects the visa at the consulate; nationals of the 21 notified countries apply directly at their own consulate. The Korean Embassy in Ukraine's 사증 page for 단기취업·공연·연예활동·장기예술흥행 (C-4, E-6) sets this out and marks the first step "(출입국 사무소에 신청)". I confirmed with `grep -n "-> E-6-2"` over the whole graph dump that E-6-2 has zero incoming edges, so the node is dead data. Note that in-repo visadocs lists only `extend` for E-6-2, which is why I set confidence below 0.8, but that is authority #4 and the E-6 chapter's issuance lists are shared across the E-6 sub-codes.
- **citation**: 주우크라이나 대한민국 대사관, 단기취업·공연·연예활동·장기예술흥행(C-4, E-6) 사증 - https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/ua-ko/brd/m_8606/view.do?seq=655309
- **fix**: Add `- E-6-2` to `NONE.next` (next to the existing `- E-6-1` / `- E-6-3` entries). Optionally also add `- {to: E-6-2, abroad: true}` to `C-4.next`, since C-4 and E-6-2 share the performance-recommendation route.

### [medium] wrong-needs: NONE -> E-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: E-3's `needs: {edu: 4}` offers the researcher visa to anyone who answers "master's" with zero experience, but a master's holder needs 3 years of research experience unless the master's is Korean, from a top-ranked university, or backed by indexed publications.
- **evidence**: The E-3 자격요건 is: ① 박사학위 소지자, or ② 석사학위 소지자로서 해당분야 3년 이상 경력자, with the experience requirement waived for 국내 석사학위 소지자, 해외 우수대학 석사 (TIMES 200대 / QS 500위 이내) and authors of SCI/SCIE/SSCI/A&HCI-indexed papers. The visamap's own `how` text states this correctly ("doctorate, or master's plus 3 years' experience"), so the `how` and the gate disagree and the gate is the looser of the two: a reader who answers edu=master and experience=0 is shown E-3 as an OPEN first visa they cannot actually obtain. All four alternatives are expressible with the existing facts (edu, koreanEdu, rank, experience).
- **citation**: 연구(E-3) 자격요건 (사증발급 안내매뉴얼 연구(E-3) 장: 박사 / 석사+3년, 경력면제 - 국내 석사, 해외 우수대학 석사, 우수 학술논문 저자) - https://visainfo-korea.com/entry/E-3-%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EC%8B%A0%EC%B2%AD-%EB%B0%A9%EB%B2%95-%EC%B4%9D%EC%A0%95%EB%A6%AC-2026-%EB%8C%80%ED%95%99%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%9B%90-%EA%B8%B0%EC%97%85%EB%B6%80%EC%84%A4%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%86%8C-%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%AD%EC%9D%B8-%EC%97%B0%EA%B5%AC%EC%9E%90-%EB%B9%84%EC%9E%90-%EC%99%84%EC%A0%84-%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C
- **fix**: On visa E-3: replace `needs: {edu: 4}` with `needs: [{edu: 5}, {edu: 4, experience: 3}, {koreanEdu: 4}, {edu: 4, rank: 1}]`.

### [medium] wrong-age: NONE -> E-8

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: E-8 carries no `age` gate at all, so the seasonal-work visa is offered as an OPEN first visa to readers of any age, including under-19s and people over 55 who are categorically excluded from the programme.
- **evidence**: The 외국인 계절근로자 프로그램 selects workers aged 만 19세 이상 55세 이하. The MOJ press release on the programme states the upper bound directly for the invited-relative tracks: "(부모) 만 55세 이하이며, 건강 및 범죄경력에 이상이 없는 자", and the same release confirms the status/duration as "C-4(90일) 또는 E-8(5개월+3개월)". Multiple programme guides restate the full range as 만 19세 이상 55세 이하. Every other age-restricted work status in the file (E-9 `age: [18, 39]`, D-3 `age: [18, 40]`, H-1 `age: [18, 30]`) does carry the gate, so the omission on E-8 looks like an oversight rather than a deliberate simplification. I set confidence at 0.65 because I could cite the 55 ceiling to an MOJ document but only secondary guides for the 19 floor.
- **citation**: 법무부 보도자료 2024.2.25, 「외국인 계절근로자 제도 개선 방안」 붙임 1 (만 55세 이하; C-4(90일) 또는 E-8(5개월+3개월)) - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/moj/182/464422/download.do ; 법무부 외국인 계절근로자 프로그램 - https://www.moj.go.kr/moj/194/subview.do
- **fix**: On visa E-8: add `age: [19, 55]` (alongside the existing `name`/`how`/`next` keys).

### [low] wrong-needs: NONE -> E-7-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-7-2 has no `needs` at all, so the semi-professional work visa is offered as an OPEN first visa to a reader with only a high-school education and no experience, which no E-7-2 occupation allows.
- **evidence**: Every one of the nine E-7-2 (준전문인력) occupations in the E-7 chapter sets an education or experience floor. From the manual's Ⅲ. 준전문인력에 대한 세부기준: 면세점 판매사무원 - 국내 전문대학 이상 졸업(예정)자, 해외 4년제 대학(학사) 이상 졸업자, or 3년 이상 관광가이드/면세점 경력; 항공운송 사무원 - "석사 이상, 학사 및 경력 1년 이상"; 호텔 접수사무원 - 일반기준 적용 (석사 / 학사+1년 / 5년 경력), with the pure-experience route restricted; 카지노 딜러 - "경력 5년 이상"; 해외영업원 별도적용 - "ⅰ) 국내 전문학사 이상 학력 소지자 ⅱ) 해외 학사 학력 소지하고 해당분야 경력 1년 이상인자 또는 석사학력 이상인자". The neighbouring, lower-tier E-7-3 does carry `needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}, {experience: 1}]`, so the blank on E-7-2 is inconsistent as well as too loose.
- **citation**: 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 특정활동(E-7) › Ⅲ. 준전문인력에 대한 세부기준, 직종별 세부 심사기준 (pp.220-232) - http://www.korea1798.url.tw/KOREAgaja/hikorea/E-7.pdf
- **fix**: On visa E-7-2: add `needs: [{koreanEdu: 2}, {edu: 3}, {experience: 3}]`.

### [low] wrong-needs: NONE -> D-10-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The NONE -> D-10-1 move inherits D-10-1's `{koreanEdu: 1, ageUnder: 25}` alternative, which is a change-of-status-only exemption, so the tool offers a job-seeker visa from abroad to a Korean-high-school graduate under 25 who has no degree.
- **evidence**: That branch encodes the 점수제 면제 특례 for "국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년", which the manual defines as "신청일 기준 18세 이상 24세 이하의 **합법체류자**로 「초·중등교육법」에 따른 국내 초·중·고교를 모두 졸업하였거나 이와 동등한 학력을 갖춘 사람" - it exists only in the 체류자격변경허가 chapter and requires lawful residence in Korea. The repo's own visadocs confirms the split: D-10-1's `change` list carries a "Foreign youth raised in Korea (age 18-24)" variant, while its `abroad` list (사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 구직(D-10) › 공관장 재량으로 발급할 수 있는 사증) has only 점수제 / 국내 대학 졸업 최초 / 한국어능력 우수자 / 유망인재 / 주한 공관 인턴. Per engine.md moves out of NONE are always treated as from abroad, so this branch can only ever fire on an impossible route. (I checked and confirmed the opposite worry is unfounded: the 2026.9 visa manual does list points-based applicants in the consular D-10-1 route, so the main `{edu: 3}` + "60+ points" framing of this edge is correct.)
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼, 구직(D-10) › 체류자격변경허가 › 1-2) 점수제 면제 특례자 › (국내 성장 기반 외국인 청소년), p.142 - https://internationalcenter.inha.ac.kr/bbs/internationalcenter/2507/169552/download.do ; cross-check data/visadocs.yaml D-10-1 `abroad.variants` vs `change.variants`
- **fix**: In `NONE.next`, change the bare `- D-10-1` entry to `- {to: D-10-1, needs: [{edu: 3}, {koreanEdu: 2}]}` so the move's own needs replace the visa's and drop the in-Korea-only youth branch.

### [low] wrong-needs: NONE -> E-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: Two of E-1's four `needs` branches require `stem: true`, so a reader with a non-STEM doctorate (or master's) and no work experience who answers "not STEM" has the professor visa blocked, although the professor qualification standard is field-neutral.
- **evidence**: E-1 eligibility is tied to the 교원 자격기준 under 「고등교육법」 제16조 and 「대학교원 자격기준 등에 관한 규정」. That regulation's criteria are purely 학력 + 연구실적연수 + 교육경력연수 (조교수: 연구실적 2년 + 교육경력 2년, mutually substitutable, with degrees credited against those years) and contain no distinction between natural-science and humanities/arts fields - Article 3 defines 연구실적 generically as "교원이 담당하는 학과목과 관련하여 대학 기타 연구기관에서 연구한 실적". So `{edu: 5, stem: true}` and `{edu: 4, stem: true, experience: 3}` import a STEM condition that is not in any rule. Concrete failure: a reader with a Korean-literature PhD, experience 0, stem answered false fails all four branches ({edu: 3, experience: 2} needs 2 years, {edu: 2, experience: 3} needs 3) and sees E-1 as BLOCKED. I could not retrieve the 별표 year-by-degree table itself, hence the moderate confidence on the zero-experience case; the field-neutrality point stands on the regulation text.
- **citation**: 대학교원 자격기준 등에 관한 규정 (대통령령 제29814호) 제2조, 제3조, 별표 - https://www.jesus.ac.kr/main/filedown.php?menu=56&no=11011 ; 조교수 기준 연구실적 2년 + 교육경력 2년 - https://io.kaist.ac.kr/m/visa/visa_prof.do
- **fix**: On visa E-1, in `needs`, change `- {edu: 5, stem: true}` to `- {edu: 5}` and `- {edu: 4, stem: true, experience: 3}` to `- {edu: 4, experience: 3}`.

### [low] wrong-countries: NONE -> H-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.45)
- **claim**: The `h1` country group omits Finland, which signed a working-holiday agreement with Korea on 2026-05-13 covering ages 18-35.
- **evidence**: MOFA announced the Korea-Finland working holiday agreement signed 2026.5.13, allowing 18-35 year olds up to 12 months with short-term work; Finland is counted in MOFA's current total of 29 working-holiday partner countries/regions. The visamap's `h1` group has 29 entries but substitutes the US (a genuine H-1 issuer under the Korea-US WEST MOU, which the repo's own visadocs H-1 `abroad` list references) for Finland. I could NOT confirm entry into force: the agreement takes effect 60 days after both sides notify completion of domestic requirements, and no issuance notice was found, so this may be correct as of today. The rest of the H-1 gating checks out: the per-country ages match the bilateral tables (Germany's raise to 18-34 from 2025.1.1 and Denmark's 30->34 are both MOFA-confirmed as bilateral, and keeping Australia at 18-30 is right because Australia's 35 cap applies only to Korean nationals outbound), and `h1NoChange` = GB, FR, IE, DK, CL, IT, IL, BE matches the manual's list of nationalities excluded from changing to professional/E-7 status in Korea.
- **citation**: 외교부 보도자료, 한-핀란드 워킹홀리데이 협정 서명 (2026.5.13) - https://overseas.mofa.go.kr/www/brd/m_4080/view.do?seq=377202 ; 재외동포청 워킹홀리데이 인포센터, 협정 체결 29개 국가·지역 - https://whic.mofa.go.kr/whic/about/working.jsp
- **fix**: In `groups:`, add `FI` to the `h1` list, and add `FI: [18, 35]` to `H-1.ageByCountry` - but only once the agreement is confirmed in force and Korean missions in Finland start issuing H-1.

## graph / other

### [high] missing-edge: G-1-5 -> G-1-6

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: G-1-5 (refugee applicant) has exactly one outgoing edge, F-2-4 (recognition). The far more common outcome, refusal of refugee status followed by a humanitarian stay permit (G-1-6), is not offered at all, so the tool can never route a refugee applicant to G-1-6 - and because G-1-6 also has in-degree 0, G-1-6 becomes a dead node that no reader path can ever reach.
- **evidence**: Refugee Act (난민법) art. 2(3) defines 인도적체류자 as a person who is NOT recognised as a refugee under art. 2(1) but who is granted a stay permit by the Minister of Justice - i.e. the status is by definition reached from a pending/refused refugee claim. The repo's own ground truth treats the two as one continuum: data/visadocs.yaml G-1-5 and G-1-6 share a single anchored extend block (&id021) whose case string is literally 'Refugee applicants (G-1-5) and persons granted humanitarian stay after refugee rejection (G-1-6)', sourced to 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 34. 기타(G-1) › 체류기간 연장허가 › 5. 난민신청자 및 난민불인정자 중 인도적 체류허가자. visamap.yaml's own G-1-6 'how' text already says 'Refugee status refused, but a humanitarian stay was granted', so the data states the relationship but never encodes the edge.
- **citation**: 난민법 제2조제3호 (https://www.law.go.kr/법령/난민법); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18 › 34. 기타(G-1) › 체류기간 연장허가 › 5. 난민신청자 및 난민불인정자 중 인도적 체류허가자 (as copied into data/visadocs.yaml anchor &id021)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, change G-1-5's `next: [F-2-4]` to `next: [F-2-4, G-1-6]` (no `abroad` flag - it is an in-Korea MOJ decision), with a note on the G-1-6 entry such as 'If refugee status is refused, the Ministry of Justice may still grant a humanitarian stay permit.'

### [high] missing-edge: H-2 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.65)
- **claim**: H-2's next list (F-4, F-5-14, E-7-4, E-7-4R) offers no family route at all. Marriage to a Korean citizen (F-6-1) is a real and common in-Korea change of status from H-2 and is not offered, nor is F-2-3 (spouse/minor child of an F-5 holder).
- **evidence**: The change-of-status rules for 결혼이민(F-6) bar only short-term and unlawful statuses as the prior status - B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, illegal stay, exit-only permits and criminal-record cases. H-2 is a long-term status under 별표 1의2 제29호 and is not on that exclusion list, and F-6-1 has a documented in-Korea change list (visadocs-targets.txt: 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'). The graph is also internally inconsistent here: the comparable low-skill work status E-9 does carry both F-6-1 and F-2-3 in its next list, and F-3 dependants of H-2 holders are documented in visadocs.yaml (F-3-20), so the author clearly knows H-2 holders have families in Korea.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제29호 방문취업(H-2) and 제27호 결혼이민(F-6) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?flSeq=169633213, 공포 2026.9.29); F-6 체류자격 변경 제한 대상 목록 (https://namu.wiki/w/국민의 배우자); visadocs-targets.txt line 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under H-2, add `- F-6-1` and `- F-2-3` to `next:` (plain in-Korea entries, no `abroad` flag), after the existing E-7-4R entry.

### [medium] wrong-countries: H-1 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: The `h1` country group includes AD (Andorra), which has no working-holiday agreement or MOU with Korea, so the tool offers H-1 to Andorran readers who cannot get it. Conversely FI (Finland), whose agreement was signed 2026.05.13, is absent - correctly for now, since it is listed as not yet in force.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제28호 limits 관광취업(H-1) to nationals of countries that have concluded a 관광취업 agreement or MOU with Korea. The MOFA Working Holiday Info Center's agreement table lists 28 countries/regions - Netherlands, New Zealand, Taiwan, Denmark, Germany, Latvia, Luxembourg, Belgium, Sweden, Spain, Argentina, Ireland, UK, Austria, Israel, Italy, Japan, Czech Republic, Chile, Canada, Portugal, Poland, France, Hungary, Australia, Hong Kong, Brazil and Finland (pending implementation) - plus the separate Korea-US WEST youth exchange programme. Andorra appears nowhere on that list. The YAML's h1 group has 29 entries; dropping AD and keeping US (WEST, which data/visadocs.yaml itself references as '한-미 WEST 프로그램 양해 각서') matches the official set exactly.
- **citation**: 재외동포청 워킹홀리데이 인포센터, 협정 체결 현황 (https://whic.mofa.go.kr/whic/about/working.jsp); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제28호 (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?flSeq=169633213)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml `groups:`, remove `AD` from the `h1` list (line 29), leaving `h1: [AR, AU, AT, BE, BR, CA, CL, CZ, DK, FR, DE, HK, HU, IE, IL, IT, JP, LV, LU, NL, NZ, PL, PT, ES, SE, TW, GB, US]`. Add `FI` with `ageByCountry: FI: [18, 35]` only once the Korea-Finland agreement enters into force.

### [medium] missing-edge: H-1 -> E-7-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: H-1's in-Korea professional route offers only E-7-1, but the sojourn manual's H-1 chapter permits a change to 특정활동(E-7) as a whole, so E-7-2 and E-7-3 are hidden from H-1 readers who qualify for them.
- **evidence**: The 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 chapter on 관광취업(H-1) 체류자격 변경허가 states that change of status is restricted in principle, with the exception: '일정한 자격요건을 갖추어야 하는 전문 직종(의사, 변호사, 교수, 항공기조종사, 회화강사 등)과 특정활동(E-7)의 경우, 요건을 갖춘 경우 자격 변경 가능 (영국, 프랑스, 아일랜드, 덴마크, 칠레, 이탈리아, 이스라엘, 벨기에 제외)'. The exception names 특정활동(E-7) generically, not the E-7-1 sub-code, and visadocs-targets.txt confirms both E-7-2 and E-7-3 have documented in-Korea change lists ('E-7-2: change,abroad,extend', 'E-7-3: change,abroad,extend').
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 관광취업(H-1) › 체류자격 변경허가 (mirrored verbatim at https://yoonhjs.com/g-h-type-visa-%EB%82%9C%EB%AF%BC%EA%B8%B0%ED%83%80%EA%B4%80%EA%B4%91%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85/?mod=document&uid=469); visadocs-targets.txt entries for E-7-2 and E-7-3
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under H-1 `next:`, add two entries mirroring the existing E-7-1 entry: `- {to: E-7-2, abroadFor: h1NoChange, note: <same note as E-7-1>}` and `- {to: E-7-3, abroadFor: h1NoChange, note: <same note as E-7-1>}`.

### [medium] missing-edge: G-1-6 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: Neither G-1-6 (humanitarian stay) nor G-1-5 (refugee applicant) offers F-6-1, so a humanitarian-stay holder or refugee applicant who marries a Korean citizen can never be routed to the marriage-migrant status - the single most common way out of G-1 in practice.
- **evidence**: The documented exclusion list for a change of status to 결혼이민(F-6) covers only B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, unlawful stayers, exit-only permits and criminal-record cases; 기타(G-1) is not excluded. G-1 is a long-term status (출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제30호: residual long-term status for persons not falling under A-1 to H-2 or 별표 1의3), and F-6-1 has a documented in-Korea change list in the 2026 manual (visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'). Note this is a secondary-source exclusion list rather than a verbatim manual quote, hence mid confidence.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제30호 기타(G-1) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?flSeq=169633213); F-6 체류자격 변경 제한 대상 (https://namu.wiki/w/국민의 배우자); visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, change G-1-6's `next: [D-2-2, D-4-1]` to `next: [D-2-2, D-4-1, F-6-1]` and G-1-5's `next` to `[F-2-4, G-1-6, F-6-1]` (plain in-Korea entries).

### [medium] wrong-abroad: H-1 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: H-1 -> F-6-1 is hard-coded `abroad: true` for every nationality, telling an H-1 holder married to a Korean citizen that they must leave Korea and apply at a consulate. A change to F-6-1 inside Korea is available to H-1 holders at least for the nationalities whose agreement does not bar a change of status.
- **evidence**: The documented exclusion list for a change of status to 결혼이민(F-6) covers only B-1, B-2, C-1, C-3, C-4, unlawful stayers, exit-only permits and criminal-record cases, and the same source notes that for 관광취업(H-1) whether a change is possible depends on the agreement with the holder's nationality - exactly the distinction the YAML already encodes with the h1NoChange group on the E-visa moves. F-6-1 has a documented in-Korea change list (visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'). Marking the move `abroad: true` for all 29 nationalities is therefore too strict for the 21 nationalities outside h1NoChange.
- **citation**: F-6 체류자격 변경 제한 대상 및 H-1 국적별 협정 의존 서술 (https://namu.wiki/w/국민의 배우자); 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 › 관광취업(H-1) › 체류자격 변경허가 (https://yoonhjs.com/g-h-type-visa-%EB%82%9C%EB%AF%BC%EA%B8%B0%ED%83%80%EA%B4%80%EA%B4%91%EC%B7%A8%EC%97%85/?mod=document&uid=469); visadocs-targets.txt 'F-6-1: change,abroad,extend'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under H-1 `next:`, replace `- {to: F-6-1, abroad: true}` with `- {to: F-6-1, abroadFor: h1NoChange, note: {en: 'In Korea, unless you are a citizen of the UK, France, Ireland, Denmark, Chile, Italy, Israel or Belgium.', ...}}` (same note object already used on the E-1..E-7-1 entries).

### [medium] unreachable: NONE -> G-1-5

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.5)
- **claim**: G-1-5 and G-1-6 have in-degree 0 and are absent from NONE's 61-entry first-visa list, so the humanitarian branch of the graph is an isolated island: no reader journey can ever arrive at it. In reality a refugee claim is lodged by a person who is already in Korea on another status, or at the port of entry.
- **evidence**: 난민법 제5조제1항: '대한민국 안에 있는 외국인으로서 난민인정을 받으려는 사람은 법무부장관에게 난민인정 신청을 할 수 있다', and 난민법 제6조 provides for a claim made at a port of entry; G-1 is the residual long-term status that catches such persons (출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제30호 기타(G-1): '별표 1, 이 표 중 외교(A-1)부터 방문취업(H-2)까지 또는 별표 1의3의 체류자격에 해당하지 않는 사람으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람'). Caveat: visadocs-targets.txt records only 'G-1-5: extend' and 'G-1-6: extend', i.e. the 2026 manual documents no document list for a change of status TO G-1, so the data convention used in this repo does not back a plain in-Korea edge; the honest fix is a port-of-entry / NONE edge plus edges from the common in-Korea source statuses.
- **citation**: 난민법 제5조제1항 및 제6조 (https://www.law.go.kr/법령/난민법); 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제30호 기타(G-1) (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?flSeq=169633213); visadocs-targets.txt 'G-1-5: extend', 'G-1-6: extend'
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml add `- G-1-5` to NONE's `next:` list, and add `- {to: G-1-5, note: {en: 'If you apply for refugee status while in Korea and your current stay expires, you are moved to G-1.'}}` to the `next:` lists of the common source statuses (at minimum B-1, B-2, C-3-1, D-2-2, D-4-1, E-9).

### [low] wrong-age: H-2 -> -

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.85)
- **claim**: H-2 has no age gate, but the Enforcement Decree restricts 방문취업(H-2) to persons aged 18 or over. The tool would offer H-2 (and everything downstream of it) to a 15-year-old reader.
- **evidence**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제29호 가목, verbatim: '「재외동포의 출입국과 법적 지위에 관한 법률」 제2조제2호에 따른 외국국적동포... 에 해당하고, 다음의 어느 하나에 해당하는 18세 이상인 사람 중에서 나목의 활동범위 내에서 체류하려는 사람으로서 법무부장관이 인정하는 사람'. The visamap H-2 block has only `needs`/`gives` (korean: true) and no `age:` key, while the comparable E-9 block correctly carries `age: [18, 39]`. The impact today is limited because H-2 has in-degree 0, but the gate is missing from the data and would be wrong the moment any inbound edge is added or a reader starts on H-2.
- **citation**: 출입국관리법 시행령 별표 1의2 제29호 가목 (https://www.law.go.kr/LSW/flDownload.do?flSeq=169633213, 별표 1의2 장기체류자격, 개정 2026.9.15)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under H-2, add `age: [18]` alongside the existing `needs: *id001` / `gives: *id001` lines.

### [low] missing-edge: H-1 -> D-2-3

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: From H-1 the only degree-study target offered is D-2-2 (bachelor's), from abroad. D-2-1, D-2-3 and D-2-4 are not offered, so a working-holiday maker who already holds a bachelor's or master's degree is never shown the master's or doctoral route, even though the move is an ordinary consular application with no H-1-specific restriction.
- **evidence**: Once the move is from abroad (`abroad: true`), there is no H-1-specific limitation - any D-2 sub-code visa can be applied for at a consulate, and visadocs-targets.txt records an `abroad` list for all of D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3 and D-2-4. Every other short-stay source node in this graph that offers abroad-only study routes (B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9) offers the full set D-2-1 through D-2-4; H-1 is the only one truncated to D-2-2, which looks like an omission rather than a rule.
- **citation**: visadocs-targets.txt lines 'D-2-1: change,abroad,extend,report', 'D-2-3: change,abroad,extend,report', 'D-2-4: change,abroad,extend'; 사증발급 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.1 › 유학(D-2) › 사증발급 일반 (as cited in data/visadocs.yaml D-2-2/D-2-3/D-2-4 abroad blocks)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml under H-1 `next:`, next to `- {to: D-2-2, abroad: true}` add `- {to: D-2-1, abroad: true}`, `- {to: D-2-3, abroad: true}` and `- {to: D-2-4, abroad: true}`.

## graph / visit

### [high] wrong-abroad: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-9 -> F-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.9)
- **claim**: F-4 (재외동포) is marked `abroad: true` from B-1, B-2, C-3-1 and C-3-9, but changing to F-4 inside Korea from a short-term status is the normal documented route, not an abroad-only one.
- **evidence**: 법무부 「알기쉬운 외국국적동포 업무 매뉴얼」(2026.2), 'Ⅱ.1.다 재외동포(F-4) 자격 변경 절차': "대상별 제출서류가 국내에서 발급되거나 출입국정보시스템으로 확인이 가능한 경우에 한하여 국내에서 자격변경이 가능합니다. - CIS지역국가동포가 과거에 동포사증(C-3-8, H-2, F-4)을 발급받은 적이 있는 경우에는 동포입증자료 제출 시 국내에서 체류자격변경이 가능함." The KIS 비자 내비게이터 also lists 체류자격변경허가 [D-2, F-4(일부 제한)] as an online (전자민원) application, i.e. a routine in-Korea change. The repo's own manual-derived data agrees: data/visadocs.yaml has `B-1>F-4`, `B-2>F-4`, `C-3-1>F-4`, `C-3-9>F-4`, each `use: change`. The map already (correctly) treats C-3-8 -> F-4 as in-Korea, so the four sibling edges are inconsistent with it.
- **citation**: 알기쉬운 외국국적동포 업무 매뉴얼 2026.2, Ⅱ.1.다 재외동포(F-4) 자격 변경 절차 (p.10) - https://jincci.korcham.net/file/dext5uploaddata/2026/%EB%B6%99%EC%9E%84%203.%20%EC%95%8C%EA%B8%B0%EC%89%AC%EC%9A%B4%20%EC%99%B8%EA%B5%AD%EA%B5%AD%EC%A0%81%EB%8F%99%ED%8F%AC%20%EC%97%85%EB%AC%B4%20%EB%A7%A4%EB%89%B4%EC%96%BC.pdf ; 비자 내비게이터 p.44 - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration_eng/230/454086/download.do
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml change `- {to: F-4, abroad: true}` to a plain `- F-4` on B-1 (line 95), B-2 (line 130), C-3-1 (line 160) and C-3-9 (line 228). Optionally add `note:` 'In Korea if your proof of Korean descent is issued in Korea or verifiable in the immigration system, or you previously held C-3-8/H-2/F-4.'

### [high] wrong-abroad: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4 (also C-3-8, C-4) -> D-4-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.88)
- **claim**: D-4-1 (Korean language course) is marked `abroad: true` from every B/C status, but an in-Korea change of status to 어학연수(D-4-1) is explicitly permitted for short-term holders, including visa-free (B-1) and 관광통과 (B-2) entrants.
- **evidence**: 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅲ.3.다 어학연수(D-4-1, D-4-7) 자격: "(허가대상) 단기체류자(관광통과 및 사증면제협정 입국자 포함) 또는 외국인등록 후 국내에 체류하고 있는 자". The only exclusions are: ⅰ) 법무부장관 고시 21개 국가 국민 (인증대 exception, which itself excludes B-2/C-3-2/C-3-3/C-3-9) and ⅱ) non-notified nationals holding 단체관광(C-3-2), 의료관광(C-3-3), 일반관광(C-3-9). C-3-1 (단기일반) and C-3-4 (단기상용) are NOT excluded, so for a non-notified national this is a plain in-Korea change. The same file already encodes exactly this logic for D-2 (`abroadFor: notified`), and data/visadocs.yaml carries a `B-1>D-4-1` exception with `use: change`, plus D-4-1 has a `change` list in visadocs-targets.txt. Note the current `abroad: true` IS correct for C-3-9 only.
- **citation**: 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, Ⅲ.3.다 '어학연수(D-4-1, D-4-7) 자격' (허가대상/제한) - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf (p.23-24)
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml replace `- {to: D-4-1, abroad: true}` with `- {to: D-4-1, abroadFor: notified, note: <same wording as the D-2 edges>}` on B-1 (line 75), B-2 (line 110), C-3-1 (line 140), C-3-4 (line 169), C-3-8 (line 207) and C-4 (line 239). Leave C-3-9 line 216 as `abroad: true` (일반관광 is excluded for every nationality).

### [high] missing-edge: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9 -> D-8-1 (and D-8-2, D-8-3, D-8-4)

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: No B/C status offers any 기업투자(D-8) route, so a visa-free entrant or short-term business visitor who incorporates and invests ₩100M+ can never be shown D-8 - yet D-8 is reachable only from NONE, D-2, D-10 and H-1 in the whole graph.
- **evidence**: The D-8 chapter's 체류자격변경 '자격변경 불가대상' is an enumerated exclusion list (단체/순수관광 목적 입국자, and 아일랜드·프랑스·영국 국민이 D-3/E-9/E-10/H-2/G-1/H-1 자격으로 입국한 경우, and 투자자가 아닌 임원); 사증면제(B-1), 관광통과(B-2) and 단기방문(C-3-1/C-3-4) are not excluded, so an in-Korea change is the default. Two explicit positive rules are documented in the same chapter: German B-1 (사증면제) entrants may change to any long-term status except D-3/E-9/H-1, and Chilean C-3-4 (단기방문) holders may change to D-7/D-8/D-9 under the Korea-Chile FTA. D-8-1~D-8-4 all carry a `change` list in visadocs-targets.txt. This is the single most common founder route and the data offers investment immigration (F-2-8/F-2-12/F-5-25, ₩1bn+) from these statuses while omitting the ₩100M D-8.
- **citation**: 하이코리아 체류 안내매뉴얼, D-8(기업투자) 체류자격변경 5) 기타 (독일인/칠레인/자격변경 불가대상) - https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=172
- **fix**: Add to the `next:` list of B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4 and C-3-9 in data/visamap.yaml: `- D-8-1`, `- D-8-2`, `- D-8-3`, `- D-8-4` (no `abroad` flag; D-8-1 already gates on `needs: {invest: 1}`). At minimum add `- D-8-1` and `- D-8-3`.

### [medium] wrong-note: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-8, C-3-9, C-4 -> D-10-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.82)
- **claim**: The note on every B/C -> D-10-1 edge says "1 year in total, 3 years with 80+ points". The D-10 total stay cap is 3 years for everyone; the 80-point threshold has nothing to do with the cap.
- **evidence**: The 구직(D-10) chapter sets 1회 부여 체류기간 of 6개월 (or 1년 for the higher-scoring/exempt categories) with a 총 체류기간 상한 of 3년 for D-10-1, D-10-2, D-10-3 and D-10-T alike; 80+ points affects only the per-grant length and the right to change status from a restricted status/nationality, not the 3-year ceiling ("최대 3년", "80점 이상이라도 이를 초과할 수 없음"). The 2025-10-30 revision raised the per-grant cap from 6 months to 1 year and the total from 2 to 3 years. The same YAML already states the correct rule elsewhere: the D-2-1/D-2-2 `again:` note reads "D-10 time adds up while you stay in Korea (up to 3 years)".
- **citation**: 하이코리아 체류 안내매뉴얼, D-10(구직) 체류기간연장 (1회 6개월/1년, 총 최대 3년) - https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=148 ; 2025-10-30 D-10 개정 - https://talent-link.co.kr/blog_detail?bg_idx=185
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, in the shared D-10-1 note on B-1/B-2/C-3-1/C-3-4/C-3-8/C-3-9/C-4, replace "1 year in total, 3 years with 80+ points." with "Granted 6 months at a time (1 year with 80+ points or a Korean degree), up to 3 years in total."

### [medium] wrong-abroad: C-3-8 -> D-2-1, D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.75)
- **claim**: C-3-8 offers D-2-1 to D-2-4 as an unconditional in-Korea change (no `abroad`/`abroadFor` flag), but C-3-8 holders are overwhelmingly nationals of the 21 notified countries, for whom a short-term-to-D-2 change is restricted.
- **evidence**: 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅲ.3.가 유학(D-2) 자격 일반: 허가대상 is 단기체류자 or 등록외국인, but "① 단기체류자 중 ... ⅰ) 법무부장관 고시 21개 국가 국민 (단, 해당 국가 국민중 인증대로의 자격변경은 예외적으로 허용)" is restricted. C-3-8 (동포방문) is a C-3 short-term status and the C-3-8 population is mostly CN, UZ, KZ, KG, UA - five of the 21 notified countries in the map's own `notified` group. Every sibling status (B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4) correctly carries `abroadFor: notified` on the identical edges, so C-3-8 is both inconsistent and too loose: it shows an unqualified in-Korea route to a Chinese or Uzbek ethnic Korean on C-3-8.
- **citation**: 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, Ⅲ.3.가 '유학(D-2)자격 일반' 허가대상/제한 (p.22) - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, C-3-8 `next:` (lines 201-205), change `- D-2-1` / `- D-2-2` / `- D-2-3` / `- D-2-4` to `- {to: D-2-1, abroadFor: notified, note: <the same note used on B-1/C-3-1>}` and likewise for D-2-2, D-2-3, D-2-4.

### [medium] wrong-abroad: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-8, C-3-9, C-4 -> F-6-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.72)
- **claim**: F-6-1 is marked `abroad: true` from every short-term status, but the manual documents an in-Korea change for short-term holders where it is unavoidable because of pregnancy, childbirth or raising the couple's own child - the single most common real-world case.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml (derived from 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18) contains `B-1>F-6-1`, `B-2>F-6-1`, `C-3-1>F-6-1`, `C-3-4>F-6-1`, `C-3-8>F-6-1`, `C-3-9>F-6-1`, `C-4>F-6-1`, each `use: change`, for "short-term holders for whom a change in Korea is unavoidable because of pregnancy, childbirth or raising the couple's own (not adopted) child", plus a separate `countries: [DE]` entry for German B-1 entrants. F-6-1 has a `change` list in visadocs-targets.txt. With `abroad: true` the page tells a pregnant spouse of a Korean citizen she must leave the country, which the manual says she need not.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9.18, 결혼이민(F-6) 체류자격변경 - 단기체류자 예외 (임신·출산·자녀양육), as transcribed in data/visadocs.yaml keys B-1>F-6-1 ... C-4>F-6-1
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml keep the plane icon but state the exception: change `- {to: F-6-1, abroad: true}` to `- {to: F-6-1, abroad: true, note: {en: 'Normally a new visa from abroad; a change in Korea is allowed if leaving is unavoidable because of pregnancy, childbirth or raising your own child together.'}}` at lines 94 (B-1), 129 (B-2), 159 (C-3-1), 188 (C-3-4), 208 (C-3-8), 227 (C-3-9), 243 (C-4).

### [medium] wrong-abroad: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9, C-4 -> E-7-1

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: E-7-1 is marked `abroad: true` from every B/C status, but the sojourn manual documents in-Korea changes to E-7-1 that are written specifically for people who entered visa-free or on a short-term visa.
- **evidence**: The repo's own manual extract (data/visadocs.yaml `B-1>E-7-1`, `B-2>E-7-1`, `C-3-1>E-7-1`, `C-3-4>E-7-1`, `C-3-9>E-7-1`, `C-4>E-7-1`, all `use: change`, ref 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9 txt lines 9028-9051) lists three change-of-status groups: (a) anyone, whatever visa they hold, hired as a teacher at a foreign school; (b) "highly skilled high-tech workers (IT, e-business, BT, NT, new materials, transport machinery, digital electronics, environment/energy) who for unavoidable reasons entered visa-free or hold a non-employment visa"; (c) visa-free (B-1/B-2) entrants working at a Jeju international school. Group (b) exists precisely for B-1/B-2 entrants, so `abroad: true` hides the one route the manual wrote for them. E-7-1 has a `change` list in visadocs-targets.txt.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9, 특정활동(E-7) 체류자격변경 (txt lines 9028-9051), as transcribed in /Users/tillo-gurufin/Desktop/code/self/kvppartners/blog/data/visadocs.yaml keys B-1>E-7-1 ... C-4>E-7-1
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml drop `abroad: true` on the E-7-1 entries at lines 90 (B-1), 125 (B-2), 155 (C-3-1), 184 (C-3-4), 223 (C-3-9), 241 (C-4) and add `note:` 'Normally a new visa from abroad. In Korea only in the documented exceptions: high-tech specialists (IT, BT, NT, new materials, etc.) who entered visa-free, teachers at a foreign school, and Jeju international-school staff.'

### [medium] wrong-abroad: B-1 -> D-8-1 / D-9-1 / E-1-E-7 / F-2-3 (all long-term targets)

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.7)
- **claim**: German citizens who enter visa-free on B-1 may change to essentially any long-term status inside Korea, but the B-1 row marks every long-term target `abroad: true` and the file has no note or group expressing this, so German readers are told to leave Korea for moves they can make here.
- **evidence**: The D-8 chapter's 기타 section records a Germany-specific rule for 사증면제(B-1) entrants: in-Korea change permitted to all long-term statuses except 기술연수(D-3), 비전문취업(E-9) and 관광취업(H-1), for each status's maximum period. The repo's own manual extract independently carries five DE-only change entries from B-1 (`B-1>D-4-1`, `B-1>E-2`, `B-1>E-7-1`, `B-1>F-2-3`, `B-1>F-6-1`, each `countries: [DE]`, `use: change`), which only make sense under that general rule. Nothing in data/visamap.yaml mentions it: `countries: visaWaiver` on B-1 includes DE, and every long-term edge is flat `abroad: true`.
- **citation**: 하이코리아 체류 안내매뉴얼, D-8(기업투자) 체류자격변경 5) 기타 - 독일인 특례 (사증면제 B-1: D-3·E-9·H-1 제외한 모든 장기체류자격) - https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=172
- **fix**: Add to B-1's `notes:` block in data/visamap.yaml (after line 103): `- countries: [DE]` / `text: {en: 'German citizens who entered visa-free may change to any long-term status inside Korea, except D-3, E-9 and H-1.'}`. (A cleaner structural fix is a `deAll` group and `abroadFor: deAll`-style negation, which the engine does not currently support.)

### [medium] missing-edge: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-9 -> G-1-5

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: No status anywhere in the graph points to G-1-5 or G-1-6, so both are unreachable; in particular a short-term visitor who applies for refugee protection in Korea - the standard way into G-1-5 - is never offered it.
- **evidence**: grep over data/visamap.yaml shows `G-1-5:` and `G-1-6:` are defined (lines 1356, 1360) with their own `next` lists but appear in no `next:` anywhere, so the page can never reach them. KIS's own leaflet states that a foreigner who files a refugee application must obtain 체류허가 at the competent office and that "난민신청자라 하더라도 ① 불법체류 등 법 위반 외국인 ... ③ 대한민국에 1년 이상 장기 체류 후 난민신청한 사람 ... 등은 체류자격(G-1) 변경 허가가 불허될 수 있습니다" - i.e. the G-1 체류자격변경 is the normal outcome, and it is the long-term stayer, not the short-term visitor, who is the exception. Note visadocs-targets.txt lists G-1-5/G-1-6 with `extend` only, so the in-repo doc data does not back a specific document list; the legal route itself is nonetheless real (난민법 제5조, 제6조).
- **citation**: 법무부 출입국·외국인정책본부, REFUGEE / RESIDENCE / NATIONALITY leaflet, 난민 신청 section - https://www.immigration.go.kr/bbs/immigration/47/426385/download.do
- **fix**: Add `- G-1-5` to the `next:` lists of B-1, B-2, C-3-1 and C-3-9 in data/visamap.yaml (no `abroad` flag), with `note:` 'Only if you apply for refugee protection in Korea; must be filed while your stay is still valid.' Alternatively, if refugee routes are deliberately out of scope, delete the now-dead G-1-5 and G-1-6 entries.

### [low] wrong-abroad: B-1, B-2, C-3-1, C-3-4, C-3-9 -> E-2

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: E-2 is marked `abroad: true` from all B/C statuses, but an in-Korea change to E-2 is documented for English teachers hired by the Ministry of Education or a provincial superintendent (EPIK/TaLK-type), whatever status they currently hold.
- **evidence**: data/visadocs.yaml (ref 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9, txt lines 7807; 7816) has `B-1>E-2`, `B-2>E-2`, `C-3-1>E-2`, `C-3-4>E-2`, `C-3-9>E-2`, each `use: change`, for "English teachers hired by the Ministry of Education / a provincial superintendent of education to teach primary, middle or high school pupils (EPIK-type), whatever their current status", plus a `countries: [DE]` entry from B-1. E-2 has a `change` list in visadocs-targets.txt. Abroad is the normal route for private-institute E-2, so this is a narrow but real and named exception that the data currently cannot show.
- **citation**: 외국인체류 안내매뉴얼 2026.9, 회화지도(E-2) 체류자격변경 - 교육부/교육감 채용 영어교사 (txt lines 7807, 7816), as transcribed in data/visadocs.yaml keys B-1>E-2 ... C-3-9>E-2
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml keep `abroad: true` but add a note on the E-2 entries at lines 91 (B-1), 126 (B-2), 156 (C-3-1), 185 (C-3-4), 224 (C-3-9): `note: {en: 'A new visa from abroad, except for teachers hired by the Ministry of Education or a provincial education office (EPIK/TaLK), who may change status in Korea.'}`

### [low] missing-edge: C-4 -> D-2-1, D-2-3, D-2-4 (and D-2-2 wrongly abroad)

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.6)
- **claim**: C-4 offers only D-2-2 as a student route and marks it `abroad: true`; 단기취업(C-4) is a 단기체류자격 that is not excluded from the in-Korea D-2 change, and associate/master's/doctoral programmes are missing entirely.
- **evidence**: 「외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침」 Ⅲ.3.가: 허가대상 = 단기체류자 or 등록외국인; the restrictions name only 고시 21개 국가 국민 and 단체관광(C-3-2)/의료관광(C-3-3)/일반관광(C-3-9). C-4 (단기취업) is a 단기체류자격 under 출입국관리법 시행령 별표1 and appears in none of the exclusions, so for a non-notified national the change is an ordinary in-Korea change. There is also no reason to offer only the bachelor's programme: D-2-1, D-2-3 and D-2-4 are reached from every other status that offers D-2-2, and C-4-5 (초청 외국인 과학자) holders would typically target D-2-3/D-2-4.
- **citation**: 외국인유학생 사증발급 및 체류관리 지침, Ⅲ.3.가 '유학(D-2)자격 일반' (p.22) - https://research.kau.ac.kr/upfile/2022/11/20221108163943-8217.pdf ; 출입국관리법 시행령 별표1 단기체류자격 (C-4 단기취업) - https://law.go.kr
- **fix**: In data/visamap.yaml, C-4 `next:` (line 240): replace `- {to: D-2-2, abroad: true}` with `- {to: D-2-1, abroadFor: notified, note: <standard D-2 note>}`, `- {to: D-2-2, abroadFor: notified, note: ...}`, `- {to: D-2-3, abroadFor: notified, note: ...}`, `- {to: D-2-4, abroadFor: notified, note: ...}`.

### [low] missing-edge: B-1, B-2, C-3-1 -> D-9-1, D-9-4

- **state**: UNVERIFIED (auditor confidence 0.55)
- **claim**: D-9-1/D-9-4 are offered only from C-3-4, although the documented bases for an in-Korea change to 무역경영(D-9) are not specific to 단기상용: they cover 사증면제(B-1) and 단기사증 holders generally, plus any 외국인 개인사업자 who brings in ₩300M.
- **evidence**: The D-9 chapter has a dedicated 체류자격변경 section titled '2) B1/단기사증/개인사업자': B-1 (사증면제) and short-term-visa holders dispatched to install/operate/maintain imported industrial equipment, and essential specialists dispatched for shipbuilding/industrial-plant fabrication, may change to D-9 in Korea; separately 외국인 개인사업자 who introduce ₩300M+ of foreign capital under the 외국환거래법 (or invest ₩300M under the 외국인투자촉진법) may change. Its exclusion list names only D-3, E-9, E-10 and tourism-purpose statuses - which is also why C-3-9 should NOT get these edges. The Korea-Chile FTA rule (C-3-4 -> D-7/D-8/D-9) explains the existing C-3-4 edges but is not the only basis.
- **citation**: 하이코리아 체류 안내매뉴얼, D9(무역경영) 체류_체류자격변경 2) B1/단기사증/개인사업자 - https://yoonhjs.com/d-typevisa2-%EC%A2%85%EA%B5%90%EC%A3%BC%EC%9E%AC%ED%88%AC%EC%9E%90%EA%B2%BD%EC%98%81%EA%B5%AC%EC%A7%81/?mod=document&uid=160
- **fix**: Add `- D-9-1` and `- D-9-4` to the `next:` lists of B-1 (after line 97), B-2 (after line 132) and C-3-1 (after line 162) in data/visamap.yaml, matching the existing C-3-4 entries (no `abroad` flag; D-9-4 is already gated by `needs: {invest: 2}`). Do not add them to C-3-9 (tourism statuses are excluded).
